FaceMoE: Mixture of Experts for Low-Resolution Face Recognition
Authors: Kartik Narayan, Vishal M. Patel
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-30T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:59:57+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026, Project Page: https://kartik-3004.github.io/FaceMoE/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Low-resolution face recognition (LR-FR) remains a challenging task due to poor feature extraction and aggregation, as probe images often contain limited identity information resulting from extreme degradations such as blur, occlusion, and low contrast. Additionally, the domain gap between high-resolution (HR) gallery images and low-resolution (LR) probe images poses a significant challenge. A single feature encoder struggles to generalize effectively across both domains when fine-tuned on an LR dataset, and this issue is further magnified by catastrophic forgetting. To address these challenges, we propose FaceMoE, an effective adaptation of Mixture of Experts (MoE) transfomer architecture for low-resolution face-recognition . Specifically, we introduce multiple specialized feed-forward network (FFN) experts and incorporate a top-k router, which dynamically assigns tokens to appropriate experts. This design emergently promotes specialization across experts for different semantic regions of the face, which enables FaceMoE to perform resolution-aware feature extraction. Moreover, the top-k router facilitates sparse expert activation, enabling the model to preserve pretrained knowledge when finetuned on a LR dataset, while increasing model capacity without proportional computational overhead. FaceMoE is trained with a combined face recognition loss, router z-loss, and load balancing loss to ensure expert specialization and stable training. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work leveraging MoE for LR-FR. Extensive experiments across eleven datasets, spanning HR, mixed-quality, and LR benchmarks, demonstrate that FaceMoE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code: https://github.com/Kartik-3004/FaceMoE
中文标题/摘要
标题：FaceMoE：面向低分辨率人脸识别的专家混合模型
由于特征提取与聚合能力不足，且探针图像常因模糊、遮挡和低对比度等极端退化导致身份信息有限，低分辨率人脸识别（LR-FR）仍具挑战性。此外，高分辨率（HR）图库与低分辨率（LR）探针图像间的域差距也构成重大障碍。单一特征编码器在LR数据集上微调时难以有效泛化，且易受灾难性遗忘影响。为此，我们提出FaceMoE，即面向LR-FR的MoE Transformer架构有效适配方案。具体而言，我们引入多个专用前馈网络（FFN）专家，并结合Top-k路由机制，动态分配令牌至合适专家。该设计促使专家在人脸不同语义区域实现专业化，从而支持FaceMoE进行分辨率感知的特征提取。同时，Top-k路由机制促进稀疏专家激活，使模型在LR数据集微调时保留预训练知识，且在不增加计算开销的前提下提升模型容量。FaceMoE采用组合损失函数（人脸识别损失、路由z损失、负载均衡损失）训练，以确保专家专业化与训练稳定性。据我们所知，这是首次将MoE应用于LR-FR。在涵盖HR、混合质量及LR基准的十一组数据集上的大量实验表明，FaceMoE显著优于现有最先进方法。代码：https://github.com/Kartik-3004/FaceMoE
Summary / 总结
FaceMoE addresses the challenge of low-resolution face recognition by adapting the Mixture of Experts (MoE) transformer architecture to handle domain gaps between high-resolution gallery and low-resolution probe images. It employs multiple specialized feed-forward network experts and a top-k router to dynamically assign tokens to experts based on semantic facial regions, enabling resolution-aware feature extraction. The model is trained with a combined loss function including recognition, router, and load balancing terms to ensure specialization and stable training while preserving pretrained knowledge and avoiding catastrophic forgetting. Experimental results across eleven datasets show FaceMoE significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in low-resolution face recognition.
FaceMoE 为低分辨率人脸识别问题提出了一种基于专家混合（MoE）架构的解决方案，旨在弥合高分辨率图像与低分辨率探针图像之间的域差距。该方法通过多个专用前馈网络专家和Top-k路由机制，动态分配面部不同语义区域的输入令牌，实现分辨率感知的特征提取。模型采用联合损失函数（包括人脸识别损失、路由损失和负载均衡损失）以确保专家专业化和训练稳定性。在十一组数据集上的实验表明，FaceMoE 显著优于现有方法，且在提升性能的同时未增加计算开销。
GEAR: Guided End-to-End AutoRegression for Image Synthesis
Authors: Bin Lin, Zheyuan Liu, Chenguo Lin, Sixiang Chen, Yunyang Ge, Yunlong Lin, Jianwei Zhang, Miles Yang, Zhao Zhong, Liefeng Bo, Li Yuan
First: 2026-06-30T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:59:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual generative models are typically trained in two stages. A tokenizer is first trained for reconstruction and then frozen, after which a generator is trained on its discrete indices or continuous latents. This decoupling leaves the tokenizer unaware of what the generator finds easy to model. We present GEAR (Guided End-to-end AutoRegression), which trains a vector-quantized (VQ) tokenizer and an autoregressive (AR) generator jointly and end-to-end, guided by representation alignment. The key obstacle is that the VQ index fed to the AR model is non-differentiable, so gradients cannot reach the tokenizer, and a straight-through estimator collapses. GEAR resolves this with a dual read-out of the codebook assignment. A hard, one-hot branch trains the AR with next-token prediction, while a differentiable soft branch carries a representation-alignment loss that flows back to guide only the tokenizer. The AR model thereby steers its tokenizer toward an index distribution it can predict more easily. This shifts the alignment burden from the tokenizer to the AR: the tokenizer's own features become less DINOv2-like while the AR's become more so, the opposite of diffusion-side recipes that make the latent itself semantic. GEAR speeds up ImageNet gFID convergence by up to 10x relative to the strong LlamaGen-REPA baseline, learns markedly better patch-level and spatially-coherent features, and generalizes across quantizers (VQVAE, LFQ, IBQ) and to text-to-image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GEAR：用于图像合成的引导式端到端自回归
视觉生成模型通常分两阶段训练：首先训练一个分词器用于重建并冻结，随后在该分词器的离散索引或连续潜在空间上训练生成器。这种解耦导致分词器不了解生成器认为哪些内容易于建模。我们提出GEAR（引导式端到端自回归），联合端到端训练向量量化（VQ）分词器和自回归（AR）生成器，并通过表示对齐进行引导。关键障碍在于，输入AR模型的VQ索引不可微分，导致梯度无法传递至分词器，且直接通过估计器会失效。GEAR通过代码本分配的双读出机制解决此问题：一个硬性的一热分支训练AR模型进行下一个词预测，另一个可微分的软性分支承载表示对齐损失，仅反向传播以引导分词器。AR模型因此引导分词器趋向于其更容易预测的索引分布。这将对齐负担从分词器转移到AR模型：分词器自身的特征变得不那么DINOv2式，而AR模型的特征则更趋DINOv2式，与扩散模型中使潜在空间本身语义化的做法相反。GEAR相较强基线LlamaGen-REPA，将ImageNet gFID收敛速度提升最高达10倍，学习到更优的块级和空间一致特征，并可泛化至不同量化器（VQVAE、LFQ、IBQ）及文本到图像生成。
Summary / 总结
GEAR addresses the inefficiency of two-stage visual generative models by jointly training a vector-quantized tokenizer and an autoregressive generator end-to-end, guided by representation alignment. To overcome the non-differentiability of VQ indices, GEAR employs a dual read-out mechanism: a hard one-hot branch for next-token prediction and a differentiable soft branch that backpropagates alignment loss to the tokenizer alone. This design allows the autoregressive model to steer the tokenizer toward more predictable code distributions, shifting the alignment burden from the tokenizer to the generator. Experiments show GEAR converges up to 10x faster than LlamaGen-REPA on ImageNet, learns more spatially coherent features, and generalizes across multiple quantization methods and text-to-image tasks.
GEAR旨在解决视觉生成模型两阶段训练的低效问题，通过在表示对齐指导下联合训练向量量化编码器与自回归生成器。为克服VQ索引不可微的问题，GEAR采用双读出机制：硬一热分支用于下一token预测，可微软分支则将对齐损失反向传播至编码器，使生成器引导编码器生成更易预测的码本分布。实验表明，GEAR在ImageNet gFID上比LlamaGen-REPA快10倍，学习到更空间一致的特征，并能跨多种量化方法和文本到图像任务泛化。
StreamEdit: Training-Free Video Editing via Few-Step Streaming Video Generation
Authors: Guanlong Jiao, Chenyangguang Zhang, Jia Jun Cheng Xian, Zewei Zhang, Renjie Liao
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:52:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Project Page: https://dsl-lab.github.io/StreamEdit/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Although existing video editing methods are generally feasible, they often require many costly iterations and still struggle to deliver high-quality yet satisfying editing results. We attribute this limitation to the prevalent data-to-data paradigm, which is less compatible with modern generative models than noise-to-data generation. To address this gap, we revisit video editing from a noise-to-data perspective and propose Streaming-Generation-based Video Editing (StreamEdit), which preserves few-step sampling while seamlessly injecting source-video conditions. Built on pre-trained streaming generation models, StreamEdit introduces dual-branch fast sampling with a self-attention bridge and cross-attention grounding/boosting to satisfy both sampling and conditioning requirements. We further propose source-oriented guidance to improve target-generation quality, and a visual prompting strategy to enhance editing flexibility and practicality. The method is effective, robust, and generalizable across different models. Extensive experiments on diverse video editing tasks show that StreamEdit consistently outperforms existing approaches, even in few-step settings with minimal time cost. Code and results are available at: https://dsl-lab.github.io/StreamEdit/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StreamEdit：通过几步流式视频生成实现无需训练的视频编辑
尽管现有视频编辑方法通常可行，但往往需要大量昂贵的迭代，仍难以交付高质量且令人满意的编辑结果。我们归因于普遍的数据到数据范式，其与现代生成模型的兼容性不如噪声到数据生成。为弥合这一差距，我们从噪声到数据视角重新审视视频编辑，提出基于流式生成的视频编辑（StreamEdit），在保持几步采样同时无缝注入源视频条件。基于预训练流式生成模型，StreamEdit引入双分支快速采样，结合自注意力桥接与跨注意力锚定/增强，满足采样与条件需求。我们进一步提出源导向引导以提升目标生成质量，并采用视觉提示策略增强编辑灵活性与实用性。该方法有效、稳健，可跨不同模型泛化。在多样视频编辑任务上的大量实验表明，StreamEdit即使在几步设置下、时间成本极低时，仍持续优于现有方法。代码与结果见：https://dsl-lab.github.io/StreamEdit/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and suboptimal quality of current video editing methods due to their reliance on iterative data-to-data paradigms, the authors propose StreamEdit, a training-free video editing framework based on noise-to-data streaming generation. The method leverages pre-trained streaming models with dual-branch fast sampling, incorporating self-attention for internal coherence and cross-attention for grounding source-video conditions, while introducing source-oriented guidance and visual prompting to enhance editing quality and flexibility. Experimental results across diverse editing tasks demonstrate that StreamEdit achieves superior performance compared to existing methods, even under few-step sampling constraints, with minimal computational overhead.
针对现有视频编辑方法因依赖数据到数据范式而迭代成本高、效果不佳的问题，作者提出StreamEdit，一种无需训练的少步流式视频生成编辑方法。该方法基于预训练流式生成模型，通过双分支快速采样结合自注意力桥接与跨注意力引导，将源视频条件无缝注入生成过程，实现高效高质量编辑。在多种编辑任务上的实验表明，StreamEdit在少步约束下仍持续优于现有方法，兼具高质量、鲁棒性和通用性，且计算成本极低。
Introspective Coupling: Self-Explanation Training Tracks Behavioral Change Despite Fixed Supervision
Authors: Zifan Carl Guo, Laura Ruis, Jacob Andreas, Belinda Z. Li
First: 2026-06-30T17:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:59:32+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 19 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When does training language models (LMs) to generate explanations of their predictions yield faithful introspection, rather than superficial imitation? We study LMs trained to explain which features of their inputs influenced their behavior, using models' counterfactual behavior on modified inputs as supervision. Surprisingly, we find that LMs trained on fixed counterfactual explanations derived from earlier checkpoints of themselves, or even from behaviorally similar models in different families, frequently produce explanations more faithful to their own current behaviors than to those of their training targets. This "introspective" coupling between LM explanations and behaviors occurs when training explanations remain sufficiently correlated with current behaviors over the course of training, even as behaviors themselves shift. We also show that introspective coupling tracks behavior shifts: when explanation training is provided concurrently with other post-training objectives, explanations track those shifts without requiring updated supervision. This phenomenon appears in multiple tasks, including sycophancy and refusal, and is robust to label noise. Overall, our results show that even fixed datasets of counterfactual explanations can provide scalable and generalizable post-training signal for introspection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：内省耦合：自我解释训练在固定监督下仍能追踪行为变化
何时对语言模型（LMs）进行训练以生成对其预测的解释，才能获得忠实的内省，而非表面模仿？我们研究了通过模型在修改输入上的反事实行为作为监督，训练LMs解释其输入中哪些特征影响其行为。令人惊讶的是，我们发现即使使用从自身早期检查点或行为相似但不同家族模型中导出的固定反事实解释进行训练，LMs仍常能生成比训练目标更忠实于自身当前行为的解释。这种“内省”耦合在训练过程中，只要解释与当前行为保持足够相关性，即使行为本身发生变化，也会出现。我们还表明，内省耦合能追踪行为变化：当解释训练与其它后训练目标同时进行时，解释无需更新监督即可追踪这些变化。该现象出现在多个任务中，包括奉承和拒绝行为，并对标签噪声具有鲁棒性。总体而言，我们的结果表明，即使使用固定反事实解释数据集，也能为内省提供可扩展且具泛化能力的后训练信号。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates when training language models to generate self-explanations of their predictions leads to genuine introspection rather than superficial mimicry. The researchers train models using counterfactual behavior from earlier checkpoints or behaviorally similar models as fixed supervision, and find that despite this static guidance, the models’ explanations often become more aligned with their own evolving behaviors than with their training targets. This 'introspective coupling' emerges when explanations remain correlated with current behavior during training, even as behavior changes, and persists when explanations are trained alongside other post-training objectives without updated supervision. The phenomenon holds across tasks like sycophancy and refusal and is resilient to label noise, suggesting fixed counterfactual datasets can serve as scalable, generalizable signals for introspective alignment.
为探究自解释训练何时能真正实现模型的内省，本研究训练语言模型解释其行为受哪些输入特征影响，使用来自早期模型检查点或行为相似模型的反事实监督。出人意料的是，即使监督数据固定，模型的解释反而更贴近其自身行为的演变而非训练目标，表明存在一种‘内省耦合’现象，即使行为本身在变化，解释仍能与当前行为保持足够相关。实验表明，该耦合在奉承与拒绝等任务中可追踪行为变化，无需更新监督，并对标签噪声鲁棒，证明固定反事实数据集可提供可扩展、可泛化的内省信号。
PointSplat: Compact Gaussian Splatting via Human-Centric Prediction
Authors: Yujie Guo, Yudong Jin, Lingteng Qiu, Zehong Shen, Zhen Xu, Jing Zhang, Xianchao Shen, Hujun Bao, Sida Peng, Xiaowei Zhou
First: 2026-06-30T17:59:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:59:05+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zju3dv.github.io/pointsplat
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Producing 3D human representations from input views on the fly is essential for immersive live streaming systems, where representation compactness is as critical as high fidelity given limited computational power and transmission bandwidth. Although recent feed-forward reconstruction methods achieve impressive quality through the view-centric prediction of 3D representations, they repeatedly encode the same subject content across multiple views, leading to significant inter-view redundancy. Our key insight is to perform predictions directly in 3D space, enabling the network to learn and produce a highly compact representation. To this end, we propose PointSplat, a novel human-centric approach that directly infers Gaussian primitives from an input point set. The proposed method first estimates a coarse geometric proxy and performs ray casting to prune redundant points and establish explicit 2D--3D correspondences. Subsequently, it employs a Point-Image Transformer to fuse appearance and geometry features, predicting Gaussian attributes in a single forward pass. This design restricts predictions to foreground regions of interest, substantially reducing the total number of Gaussians while improving novel-view rendering quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PointSplat achieves higher efficiency and quality while exhibiting strong robustness to variations in view count and image resolution across multiple datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PointSplat：通过以人为中心的预测实现紧凑的高斯点云
在计算能力和传输带宽受限的沉浸式直播系统中，实时从输入视角生成3D人体表示至关重要，此时表示的紧凑性与高保真度同等重要。尽管近期的前馈重建方法通过视角中心的3D表示预测实现了卓越质量，但它们在多个视角中重复编码相同主体内容，导致显著的视角间冗余。我们的核心洞察是直接在3D空间中进行预测，使网络能够学习并生成高度紧凑的表示。为此，我们提出PointSplat，一种新颖的人体中心方法，直接从输入点集推断高斯基本体。该方法首先估计粗略几何代理并进行光线投射以剔除冗余点并建立显式的2D-3D对应关系，随后采用点-图像Transformer融合外观与几何特征，在单次前向传播中预测高斯属性。该设计将预测限制在感兴趣前景区域，大幅减少高斯数量的同时提升新视角渲染质量。大量实验表明，PointSplat在多个数据集上实现了更高的效率与质量，并对视角数量和图像分辨率的变化表现出强鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
To address the need for compact yet high-fidelity 3D human representations in live streaming systems with limited resources, PointSplat introduces a human-centric method that directly predicts Gaussian primitives from input point sets in 3D space, avoiding redundant encoding across views. The approach first constructs a coarse geometric proxy and prunes redundant points via ray casting to establish 2D-3D correspondences, then uses a Point-Image Transformer to fuse appearance and geometry features, predicting Gaussian attributes in one forward pass focused on foreground regions. Experimental results show that this design reduces the number of Gaussians significantly while enhancing novel-view rendering quality and maintaining robust performance across varying view counts and resolutions.
为解决传统视图中心重建方法在多视角下重复编码人体内容导致的冗余问题，PointSplat 提出一种以人为中心的直接3D空间预测方法，从输入点集直接推断紧凑的高斯元。该方法首先构建粗几何代理并通过光线投射剔除冗余点并建立2D-3D对应关系，再利用点-图像Transformer融合外观与几何特征，在单次前向传播中预测前景区域的高斯属性。实验表明，该方法显著减少高斯元数量，同时提升新视角渲染质量，并在不同视角数量和图像分辨率下保持强鲁棒性。
QVal: Cheaply Evaluating Dense Supervision Signals for Long-Horizon LLM Agents
Authors: Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez, Matteo Merler, Ilze Amanda Auzina, Joschka Strüber, Ameya Prabhu, Matthias Bethge
First: 2026-06-30T17:58:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:58:23+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures in main text; 48 pages, 6 figures with appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents increasingly act over long horizons, where a single trajectory can contain hundreds or thousands of actions. In these settings, outcome-only rewards provide too sparse guidance, failing to inform the model about the goodness of intermediate actions. Dense supervision methods aim to solve this problem by scoring intermediate steps, from intrinsic confidence to self-distillation and embedding similarities. However, it is common practice to evaluate them by measuring the downstream performance of a training pipeline that integrates them. This is expensive, conflates supervision quality with training engineering confounders, and renders different methodological families requiring distinct training setups incomparable. As a result, dense supervision methods are rarely benchmarked on common ground. We introduce QVal, a training-free testbed for directly evaluating dense supervision signals. Given a state-action pair, QVal measures how well a method's score is Q-aligned: whether it orders actions according to the Q-values of a strong reference-policy. This lets us compare signals before any training run and separate signal quality from other engineering choices. We instantiate QVal as QVal-v1.0, benchmarking 21 dense supervision methods across four diverse environments and seven methodological families, with over 1.2K evaluation experiments across six open-weight model backbones. We find that simple prompting baselines consistently outperform recent dense supervision methods from the literature, and that performance clusters strongly by family. These findings hold across model sizes, environments, and observation modalities. QVal is designed to be easily extensible to new environments and methods, enabling researchers to iterate on dense supervision methods before any training run.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QVal：低成本评估长时程LLM代理的密集监督信号
随着大语言模型代理在长时程中行动，单条轨迹可能包含数百甚至数千个动作。在此类场景中，仅基于结果的奖励提供过于稀疏的指导，无法告知模型中间动作的质量。密集监督方法旨在通过评分中间步骤（从内在置信度到自蒸馏和嵌入相似性）解决此问题。然而，通常通过测量整合这些方法的训练流水线下游性能来评估它们，这成本高昂，混淆了监督质量与训练工程变量，且使不同方法家族因需要不同训练设置而难以比较。因此，密集监督方法很少在统一基准上进行评测。我们引入QVal，一个无需训练的测试平台，可直接评估密集监督信号。给定状态-动作对，QVal衡量方法评分与Q值的一致性：即是否按强参考策略的Q值对动作排序。这使我们能在任何训练前比较信号质量，并将信号质量与其它工程选择分离。我们将QVal实例化为QVal-v1.0，在四个多样化环境中对21种密集监督方法进行基准测试，涵盖七类方法家族，共在六个开源模型骨架上完成1200多次评估实验。我们发现，简单的提示基线方法持续优于近期文献中的密集监督方法，且性能强烈按方法家族聚类。这些发现适用于不同模型规模、环境和观测模态。QVal设计为易于扩展至新环境和方法，使研究人员能在任何训练前迭代密集监督方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current dense supervision evaluation methods that rely on costly training pipelines, the paper introduces QVal, a training-free framework to directly assess how well dense supervision signals align with Q-values from a strong reference policy. QVal-v1.0 evaluates 21 methods across four environments and seven families using over 1,200 experiments on six open-weight models, finding that simple prompting baselines consistently outperform recent dense supervision approaches and that performance clusters strongly by methodological family, regardless of model size, environment, or modality.
为解决当前密集监督方法依赖昂贵训练流程进行评估的问题，作者提出了QVal，一个无需训练的评估框架，直接衡量密集监督信号是否与强参考策略的Q值对齐。通过对比每个状态-动作对的评分与参考策略的Q值，QVal将信号质量与训练工程因素分离。在21种方法、四个环境、七类方法家族上进行的1200多次评估（覆盖六种开源模型）表明，简单的提示基线方法持续优于近期密集监督方法，且性能强烈按方法家族聚类，这一结论在模型规模、环境和观测模态上均保持一致。
SpheRoPE: Zero-Shot Optimization-Free 360 Panorama Generation with Spherical RoPE
Authors: Or Hirschorn, Aaron Olender, Eli Alshan, Ianir Ideses, Lior Fritz, Sagie Benaim
First: 2026-06-30T17:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:57:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present a zero-shot, training-free and optimization-free framework for generating 360 panoramic images and videos by directly injecting spherical priors into pre-trained diffusion transformers. Existing methods either rely on costly fine-tuning on scarce panoramic data that limits generalization, or leverage multi-step optimization that incurs prohibitive inference latency. We observe that contemporary generative models natively exhibit some panoramic priors from large-scale training. However, these emergent capabilities are insufficient, as the models fundamentally fail to satisfy the rigorous topological constraints imposed by equirectangular projection (ERP). We introduce a zero-shot and optimization-free approach that resolves these constraints at inference time. Spherical RoPE replaces standard rotary position embeddings: low-frequency channels are re-parameterized as 3D Cartesian coordinates to natively encode the spherical manifold, while high-frequency channels are harmonically quantized to enforce exact periodicity. Coupled with complementary Semantic Distortion classifier-free guidance (CFG) that explicitly steers geometry, we avoid retraining and inherit the full creative breadth of state-of-the-art models. Our approach generalizes across diverse backbones and 360 generation modalities. We demonstrate this across text-to-panorama using Flux.1, Flux.2, and LTX-Video backbones, achieving competitive performance against baselines, all while remaining training-free. Project page: https://orhir.github.io/SpheRoPE
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpheRoPE：基于球面RoPE的零样本、无优化360度全景生成
我们提出了一种零样本、无需训练和优化的框架，通过将球面先验直接注入预训练的扩散Transformer中，生成360度全景图像和视频。现有方法要么依赖于稀缺全景数据的昂贵微调，限制泛化能力，要么采用多步优化导致推理延迟过高。我们观察到，当前生成模型在大规模训练中天然具备部分全景先验，但这些能力不足，因为模型根本无法满足等距柱面投影（ERP）所要求的严格拓扑约束。我们引入一种零样本、无优化的方法，在推理时解决这些约束。球面RoPE替代标准旋转位置嵌入：低频通道重新参数化为3D笛卡尔坐标，以原生编码球面流形；高频通道谐波量化以强制精确周期性。结合互补的语义失真分类器自由引导（CFG），明确引导几何结构，我们无需重新训练，即可继承当前最先进模型的全部创作能力。我们的方法适用于多种骨干网络和360度生成模态。我们在Flux.1、Flux.2和LTX-Video骨干网络上进行文本到全景的实验，表现优于基线方法，且完全无需训练。项目页面：https://orhir.github.io/SpheRoPE
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing 360 panorama generation methods—either requiring costly fine-tuning or multi-step optimization—this work introduces SpheRoPE, a zero-shot, training-free, and optimization-free framework that injects spherical priors into pre-trained diffusion transformers. It replaces standard rotary position embeddings with Spherical RoPE: low-frequency channels encode 3D Cartesian coordinates to represent the spherical manifold, while high-frequency channels are harmonically quantized to enforce periodicity under equirectangular projection. Paired with Semantic Distortion classifier-free guidance, the method steers geometry without retraining. Experiments across Flux.1, Flux.2, and LTX-Video backbones show competitive results in text-to-panorama generation while maintaining zero-shot and optimization-free properties.
为解决现有360度全景生成方法需昂贵微调或多步优化的问题，本文提出SpheRoPE框架，通过在预训练扩散Transformer中注入球面先验实现零样本、无训练、无优化生成。其核心是用Spherical RoPE替代标准旋转位置嵌入：低频通道编码3D笛卡尔坐标以表征球面流形，高频通道谐波量化以强制满足等矩形投影的周期性约束。结合语义失真分类器自由引导，该方法无需重训练即可引导几何结构。在Flux.1、Flux.2和LTX-Video骨干网络上实验表明，其在文本到全景生成任务中表现与基线相当，且完全满足零样本与无优化要求。
Reinforcement Learning with Metacognitive Feedback Elicits Faithful Uncertainty Expression in LLMs
Authors: Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu, Avi Caciularu, Gal Yona, Idan Szpektor, Arman Cohan
First: 2026-06-30T17:56:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:56:01+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/yale-nlp/RLMF
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Metacognition is a critical component of intelligence that describes the ability to monitor and regulate one's own cognitive processes. Yet LLMs exhibit systemic deficiencies in key metacognitive faculties: they hallucinate with high confidence, fail to recognize knowledge boundaries, and misrepresent their internal uncertainty--undermining trustworthiness and reliability. Since monitoring task performance and adapting behavior accordingly are central to metacognition, we posit that models capable of accurately judging their own performance are better positioned to improve it. We operationalize this idea via two novel mechanisms: reinforcement learning with metacognitive feedback (RLMF), a paradigm to refine completion rankings during preference optimization based on the quality of a model's self-judgments of performance, and metacognitive data selection, which uses similar self-judgments to identify high-value training examples, outperforming naive active learning. We apply these innovations to the problem of faithful calibration (FC), a task that is itself fundamentally metacognitive: the goal is to align expressed with intrinsic uncertainty, difficult even for frontier LLMs. We adopt a two-stage, decoupled approach, first using these methods to calibrate the faithfulness of models' self-reported confidence scores, then mapping to natural, context-adaptable linguistic uncertainty via targeted output editing. Extensive experiments show RLMF achieves generalizable, state-of-the-art FC on diverse tasks while preserving accuracy. Further, RLMF surpasses standard RL by up to 63% while enhancing models' ability to assess and express their own capability limits. This positions RLMF as a promising paradigm to enhance LLM metacognition toward improved abilities and alignment, and suggests metacognitive performance as an effective RL signal to overcome limits of prior intrinsic feedback methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带有元认知反馈的强化学习使大语言模型能忠实表达不确定性
元认知是智能的关键组成部分，指个体监控和调节自身认知过程的能力。然而，大语言模型在关键元认知能力上存在系统性缺陷：高置信度产生幻觉、无法识别知识边界、错误表征内部不确定性——损害其可信度和可靠性。由于监控任务表现并据此调整行为是元认知的核心，我们认为能准确判断自身表现的模型更有利于改进表现。我们通过两种新机制实现这一理念：元认知反馈强化学习（RLMF），一种基于模型自我评估表现质量来优化偏好排序的完成排名精炼范式；元认知数据选择，利用类似自我评估识别高价值训练样本，优于朴素主动学习。我们将这些创新应用于忠实校准（FC）任务——该任务本身具有根本性元认知特征：目标是使表达的不确定性与内在不确定性对齐，即使前沿大语言模型也难以实现。我们采用两阶段解耦方法：首先用这些方法校准模型自我报告置信度的忠实性，然后通过定向输出编辑映射到自然、可适应上下文的语言不确定性。大量实验表明，RLMF在多样化任务上实现可泛化的、最先进的FC，同时保持准确性。此外，RLMF相比标准强化学习最高提升63%，并增强模型评估和表达自身能力极限的能力。这使RLMF成为提升大语言模型元认知能力、实现更好表现与对齐的有前景范式，并表明元认知表现可作为有效强化学习信号，克服先前内在反馈方法的局限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by LLMs' systemic failures in metacognition—such as overconfidence and poor uncertainty estimation—the paper proposes reinforcement learning with metacognitive feedback (RLMF) to improve self-assessment and calibration. RLMF refines model rankings during preference optimization using self-reported performance quality, while metacognitive data selection identifies high-value training examples based on self-judgments. Experiments show RLMF achieves state-of-the-art, generalizable calibration across tasks, outperforming standard RL by up to 63% and significantly enhancing models' ability to accurately express their uncertainty without sacrificing accuracy.
为解决大语言模型缺乏元认知能力（如过度自信和不确定性表达不佳）的问题，本文提出基于元认知反馈的强化学习（RLMF）方法，利用模型对自身表现的评估来优化偏好排序并选择高价值训练样本，优于朴素主动学习。实验表明，RLMF在多种任务上实现通用且领先的校准效果，同时保持准确率，并相较标准强化学习提升高达63%，显著增强模型识别和表达自身能力边界的能力。
When LLMs Read Tables Carelessly: Measuring and Reducing Data Referencing Errors
Authors: Yuqing Yang, Qi Zhu, Zhen Han, Boran Han, Zhengyuan Shen, Shuai Wang, Vassilis N. Ioannidis, Huzefa Rangwala
Venue: ACL 2026 Oral
First: 2026-06-30T17:54:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:54:50+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While large language models (LLMs) perform well on table tasks, they still make data referencing errors (DREs), i.e., incorrectly citing or omitting table values, despite understanding the table structure. Beyond final-answer accuracy, DREs directly compromise the correctness and reliability of intermediate reasoning steps. Yet prior studies have only offered limited, small-scale analyses. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of tabular data referencing errors across different models and tasks. Our results show that DREs occur across all tested models (1.7B to 20B parameters). Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating data referencing as a critic significantly improves answer accuracy up to 12.0%, through critic-based filtering and rejection sampling. Finally, we trained a lightweight 4B-parameter critic model that achieves an average F1 score of 78.2% in detecting both in-distribution and out-of-distribution DREs, and effectively assists inference for larger models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当大语言模型粗心阅读表格时：测量与减少数据引用错误
尽管大语言模型（LLMs）在表格任务上表现良好，但它们仍会犯数据引用错误（DREs），即错误引用或遗漏表格值，尽管理解了表格结构。除了最终答案的准确性外，DREs会直接损害中间推理步骤的正确性和可靠性。然而，以往研究仅提供了有限且小规模的分析。在本研究中，我们首次系统评估了不同模型和任务中的表格数据引用错误。结果显示，DREs在所有测试模型（1.7B至20B参数）中均出现。此外，我们证明，将数据引用作为批评者可显著提升答案准确性达12.0%，通过基于批评者的过滤和拒绝采样实现。最后，我们训练了一个轻量级4B参数的批评者模型，其在检测分布内和分布外DREs时平均F1分数达78.2%，并有效辅助大型模型的推理。

The authors conduct the first systematic evaluation across multiple model sizes and tasks, revealing that DREs occur universally across tested models. They introduce a critic-based approach that filters and rejects erroneous references, improving final answer accuracy by up to 12.0%. Additionally, they train a lightweight 4B-parameter critic model achieving 78.2% F1 score in detecting both in- and out-of-distribution DREs, effectively enhancing inference for larger models.
本文针对大语言模型在处理表格时仍会因错误引用或遗漏数据而影响推理可靠性的问题展开研究。作者首次系统评估了不同规模模型和任务中的数据引用错误，发现所有测试模型均存在此类错误。他们提出基于批评者的纠错方法，通过拒绝采样使最终答案准确率提升最高12.0%。同时，训练了一个4B参数的轻量级批评者模型，能在识别分布内与分布外错误时达到78.2%的F1分数，有效辅助大型模型推理。
DVG-WM: Disentangled Video Generation Enables Efficient Embodied World Model for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Ziyu Shan, Zhenyu Wu, Xiaofeng Wang, Zheng Zhu, Ziwei Wang
First: 2026-06-30T17:54:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:54:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video-based embodied world models provide an appealing substrate for robotic manipulation by predicting future states, yet current approaches remain limited by a fundamental entanglement: accurately modeling dynamics typically requires low-level temporal reasoning, while producing high-resolution frames demands expansive visual synthesis according to high-level semantics. This entanglement results in slow inference speed for iterative planning or too coarse predictions to retain contact-rich details. To solve this dilemma, we present Disentangled Video Generation World Model (DVG-WM), an efficient framework that explicitly decomposes world modeling into dynamics learning and visual synthesis. Conditioned on an initial observation and a language instruction, our model first generates a plausible sequence of intermediate visual states to preview the physical interaction and refines them to obtain high-fidelity videos. Furthermore, an efficient cascading mechanism is proposed, where DVG-WM uses flow matching to directly map the dynamics to video latents, and introduces a latent degradation mechanism to regenerate contact-rich details. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world platforms demonstrate improved video quality with up to 3.97 times acceleration, validating that disentangled video generation can be an efficient embodied world model for robotic manipulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DVG-WM：解耦视频生成实现高效具身世界模型用于机器人操作
基于视频的具身世界模型通过预测未来状态为机器人操作提供了有吸引力的基础，但当前方法受限于一个根本性耦合：准确建模动力学通常需要低层次的时间推理，而生成高分辨率帧则需根据高层次语义进行大规模视觉合成。这种耦合导致迭代规划时推理速度过慢，或预测过于粗糙而无法保留接触细节。为解决这一困境，我们提出解耦视频生成世界模型（DVG-WM），一种明确将世界建模分解为动力学学习与视觉合成的高效框架。模型在初始观测和语言指令条件下，首先生成合理中间视觉状态序列以预览物理交互，并对其进行优化以获得高保真视频。此外，我们提出一种高效级联机制，其中DVG-WM利用流匹配直接将动力学映射到视频潜在表示，并引入潜在退化机制以再生接触细节。在LIBERO和真实平台上的实验表明，视频质量提升的同时推理速度最高加速3.97倍，验证了解耦视频生成可作为机器人操作的高效具身世界模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current video-based world models in robotic manipulation due to entangled dynamics and visual synthesis, the authors propose DVG-WM, a disentangled framework that separates dynamics learning from visual generation. Given an initial observation and language instruction, the model first generates intermediate visual states to simulate physical interaction, then refines them into high-fidelity videos. A cascading mechanism uses flow matching to map dynamics directly to video latents and applies latent degradation to recover contact-rich details. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world platforms show up to 3.97x faster inference while improving video quality, confirming disentanglement enables both efficiency and fidelity in embodied world modeling for manipulation.
为解决当前基于视频的机器人操作世界模型中动力学建模与高分辨率视觉合成相互纠缠导致的效率低下和细节缺失问题，本文提出DVG-WM框架，将世界建模明确拆分为动力学学习与视觉生成两个独立模块。模型在初始观测和语言指令条件下，先生成中间视觉状态以预演物理交互，再优化为高保真视频；并引入级联机制，通过流匹配直接将动力学映射至视频潜在空间，结合潜在空间退化模块恢复接触细节。在LIBERO与真实机器人平台上的实验表明，该方法可实现最高3.97倍推理加速，同时保持或提升视频质量，验证了解耦视频生成作为高效具身世界模型的有效性。
Freeform Preference Learning for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Marcel Torne, Anubha Mahajan, Abhijnya Bhat, Chelsea Finn
First: 2026-06-30T17:54:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:54:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reward design remains a central bottleneck for autonomous robot policy improvement, especially in long-horizon manipulation tasks where sparse success labels provide too little signal and binary preferences collapse many competing notions of quality into one ambiguous signal. We introduce Freeform Preference Learning (FPL), a method for learning robot policies from freeform human preferences. Rather than asking annotators which of two trajectories is better overall, FPL lets them define natural-language preference axes, such as speed, safety, quality of placement, or carefulness, and provide pairwise preferences along each axis. These annotations are used to learn a language-conditioned reward model that maps a trajectory and preference label to an axis-specific reward. We use this model to train a reward-conditioned policy that optimizes across the multiple human-specified dimensions. Across four real-world and two simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, FPL improves over sparse-reward and binary-preference methods by 38 percentage points. Beyond improved performance, FPL learns dense progress signals without explicit subtask segmentation, shows compositionality of behavior not present in the data, and allows users to steer the policy towards different behaviors at test time without retraining. Blog post with videos available at https://freeform-pl.github.io/fpl.website/
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人操作的自由形式偏好学习
奖励设计仍是自主机器人策略改进的核心瓶颈，特别是在长时程操作任务中，稀疏的成功标签提供的信号不足，而二元偏好则将许多竞争性的质量概念压缩为一个模糊信号。我们提出了自由形式偏好学习（FPL），一种从自由形式人类偏好中学习机器人策略的方法。FPL不询问标注者两条轨迹哪个整体更好，而是允许他们定义自然语言偏好轴（如速度、安全性、放置质量或细致程度），并在每个轴上提供成对偏好。这些标注用于学习一个语言条件化的奖励模型，该模型将轨迹和偏好标签映射到轴特定的奖励。我们使用该模型训练一个奖励条件化的策略，以在多个由人类指定的维度上进行优化。在四个真实世界和两个模拟的长时程操作任务中，FPL相比稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法提升了38个百分点。除了性能提升，FPL无需显式子任务分割即可学习密集的进展信号，展现出数据中不存在的行为组合性，并允许用户在测试时无需重新训练即可引导策略朝向不同行为。相关博客及视频见 https://freeform-pl.github.io/fpl.website/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of sparse rewards and binary preferences in long-horizon robotic manipulation, the paper proposes Freeform Preference Learning (FPL), which enables human annotators to define natural-language preference axes (e.g., speed, safety) and provide pairwise preferences along each axis. These annotations train a language-conditioned reward model that assigns axis-specific rewards to trajectories, which in turn guides a reward-conditioned policy to optimize across multiple dimensions. Experiments across six tasks show FPL outperforms baseline methods by 38 percentage points, learns dense progress signals without subtask segmentation, exhibits compositional behavior, and allows runtime steering of policy behavior without retraining.
为解决长时程机器人操作中稀疏奖励和二元偏好信号不足的问题，本文提出自由形式偏好学习（FPL），允许人类标注者定义自然语言偏好维度（如速度、安全性）并沿各维度提供成对偏好。这些标注用于训练一个语言条件奖励模型，将轨迹与偏好标签映射到特定维度的奖励，进而指导奖励条件策略在多维度上优化。实验表明，FPL在六个任务中比基线方法提升38个百分点，无需子任务分割即可学习密集进度信号，展现出数据中不存在的行为组合性，并支持测试时无需重训练即可调整策略行为。
AdaJEPA: An Adaptive Latent World Model
Authors: Ying Wang, Oumayma Bounou, Yann LeCun, Mengye Ren
First: 2026-06-30T17:53:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:53:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Latent world models enable planning from high-dimensional observations by predicting future states in a compact latent space. However, these models are typically kept frozen at test time: when their predictions become inaccurate, planning can fail, especially under test-time distribution shift. To address this, we propose AdaJEPA, an adaptive latent world model that performs test-time adaptation within the closed loop of model predictive control (MPC). After training, AdaJEPA plans and executes the first action chunk, uses the observed next-state transition as a self-supervised adaptation signal, and replans with the updated model. This closed-loop update continuously recalibrates the world model without additional expert demonstrations. Across a range of goal-reaching tasks, AdaJEPA substantially improves planning success with as few as one gradient step per MPC replanning step.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaJEPA：一种自适应潜在世界模型
潜在世界模型通过在紧凑的潜在空间中预测未来状态，实现从高维观测中进行规划。然而，这些模型通常在测试时保持冻结：当其预测变得不准确时，规划可能失败，尤其是在测试时分布偏移的情况下。为解决此问题，我们提出了AdaJEPA，一种在模型预测控制（MPC）闭环中进行测试时自适应的潜在世界模型。训练后，AdaJEPA规划并执行首个动作块，利用观测到的下一状态转换作为自监督适应信号，并更新模型后重新规划。该闭环更新持续校准世界模型，无需额外专家演示。在多种目标达成任务中，AdaJEPA仅需每步MPC重新规划时进行一次梯度更新，即可显著提升规划成功率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that frozen latent world models fail under distribution shifts during test time, AdaJEPA introduces a closed-loop adaptation mechanism within model predictive control. It trains a latent world model, then during execution, plans and acts, observes the resulting state transition, and uses it as a self-supervised signal to update the model before replanning. Experiments show that this single-step adaptation significantly boosts planning success across goal-reaching tasks without requiring additional expert data.
为了解决传统潜变量世界模型在测试时因分布偏移导致规划失败的问题，AdaJEPA在模型预测控制的闭环中引入了自适应更新机制。训练完成后，它先执行初始动作序列，根据观测到的状态转移进行自监督模型更新，再进行重新规划。实验表明，在多个目标达成任务中，仅需每轮重新规划时进行一次梯度更新，AdaJEPA就能显著提升规划成功率。
SpectralSplats: Robust Differentiable Tracking via Spectral Moment Supervision
Authors: Avigail Cohen Rimon, Amir Mann, Mirela Ben Chen, Or Litany
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-03-25T07:48:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:53:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. Project page: https://avigailco.github.io/SpectralSplats/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time, photorealistic novel view synthesis, making it a highly attractive representation for model-based video tracking. However, leveraging the differentiability of the 3DGS renderer "in the wild" remains notoriously fragile. A fundamental bottleneck lies in the compact, local support of the Gaussian primitives. Standard photometric objectives implicitly rely on spatial overlap; if severe camera misalignment places the rendered object outside the target's local footprint, gradients strictly vanish, leaving the optimizer stranded. We introduce SpectralSplats, a robust tracking framework that resolves this "vanishing gradient" problem by shifting the optimization objective from the spatial to the frequency domain. By supervising the rendered image via a set of global complex sinusoidal features (Spectral Moments), we construct a global basin of attraction, ensuring that a valid, directional gradient toward the target exists across the entire image domain, even when pixel overlap is completely nonexistent. To harness this global basin without introducing periodic local minima associated with high frequencies, we derive a principled Frequency Annealing schedule from first principles, gracefully transitioning the optimizer from global convexity to precise spatial alignment. We demonstrate that SpectralSplats acts as a seamless, drop-in replacement for spatial losses across diverse deformation parameterizations (from MLPs to sparse control points), successfully recovering complex deformations even from severely misaligned initializations where standard appearance-based tracking catastrophically fails.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpectralSplats：通过频谱矩监督实现鲁棒可微跟踪
3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）支持实时、逼真的新视角合成，使其成为基于模型的视频跟踪极具吸引力的表示方法。然而，在真实场景中利用3DGS渲染器的可微性仍极为脆弱。根本瓶颈在于高斯基本体的紧凑局部支持。标准光度目标隐式依赖空间重叠；若严重相机错位导致渲染对象完全超出目标局部足迹，梯度将严格消失，优化器陷入停滞。我们提出SpectralSplats，一种鲁棒跟踪框架，通过将优化目标从空间域转移到频域，解决“梯度消失”问题。通过监督一组全局复正弦特征（频谱矩），我们构建全局吸引盆地，确保即使像素完全无重叠，整个图像域内仍存在指向目标的有效梯度方向。为避免引入高频相关的周期局部极小值，我们从第一性原理推导出一种原则性的频域退火调度策略，使优化器平滑过渡从全局凸性到精确空间对齐。我们证明SpectralSplats可无缝替换各类空间损失，适用于多种形变参数化（从MLP到稀疏控制点），即使从严重错位初始化也能成功恢复复杂形变，而传统基于外观的跟踪方法在此情况下会彻底失败。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragility of differentiable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) tracking under camera misalignment, this work introduces SpectralSplats, a framework that replaces spatial photometric loss with frequency-domain supervision via global complex sinusoidal features (Spectral Moments). This shift enables gradient propagation even when rendered objects are spatially misaligned with target regions, creating a global basin of attraction. To avoid high-frequency artifacts, a principled Frequency Annealing schedule is derived to smoothly transition optimization from global convexity to precise spatial alignment. Experiments show SpectralSplats successfully recovers complex deformations from severely misaligned initializations, outperforming standard appearance-based tracking methods.
为了解决3D高斯点云（3DGS）在相机错位时因像素重叠缺失导致梯度消失的问题，SpectralSplats提出在频域而非空间域进行优化。它通过监督全局复正弦特征（频谱矩）构建鲁棒的吸引盆地，确保即使渲染物体完全脱离目标局部区域，梯度仍可有效传播。为避免高频引起的局部极小值，该方法采用基于物理原理的频域退火策略，逐步将优化器从全局凸性过渡到精确空间对齐。实验表明，SpectralSplats能在严重错位初始化下成功恢复复杂形变，显著优于传统基于外观的跟踪方法。
Coarsening Bias from Variable Discretization in Causal Functionals
Authors: Xiaxian Ou, Razieh Nabi
First: 2026-02-25T16:32:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:53:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Forty-Second Annual Conference on Uncertainty in Artificial Intelligence (UAI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal identification functionals often require integration over conditional densities of continuous variables, such as those arising in nonparametric identification theory of total and mediated causal effects in DAGs with hidden variables. Estimating these densities and evaluating the resulting integrals can be statistically and computationally demanding. A common workaround is to discretize the continuous variable and replace integrals with finite sums. Although convenient, discretization alters the population-level functional and can induce non-negligible approximation bias, even when identification is correct. Under smoothness conditions, we show that the resulting coarsening error is first order in the bin width and arises at the level of the target functional, distinct from statistical estimation error. We propose a simple debiased coarsened functional that evaluates the outcome regression at within-bin conditional means, eliminating the leading coarsening error term and yielding a second-order approximation error. We derive plug-in and one-step estimators for this debiased coarsened functional. Simulations demonstrate substantial bias reduction and near-nominal confidence interval coverage, even under coarse binning. Our results provide a simple framework for controlling the impact of variable discretization on both parameter approximation and statistical estimation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果函数中的变量离散化导致的粗化偏差
因果识别函数常需对连续变量的条件密度进行积分，如在含隐藏变量的DAG中非参数识别总效应和中介效应时出现的情况。估计这些密度并计算相应积分在统计和计算上均具挑战性。一种常见替代方法是离散化连续变量并将积分替换为有限和。尽管便捷，离散化会改变总体函数并可能引入不可忽略的近似偏差，即使识别正确。在光滑条件下，我们证明所得粗化误差为区间宽度的一阶量，且发生在目标函数层面，不同于统计估计误差。我们提出一种简单的去偏粗化函数，通过在区间内取条件均值评估结果回归，消除主导粗化误差项，从而获得二阶近似误差。我们为此去偏粗化函数推导了插值估计量和一步估计量。模拟显示即使在粗粒度分箱下，也能显著降低偏差并实现接近名义置信区间覆盖率。我们的结果提供了一个简单框架，用于控制变量离散化对参数近似和统计估计的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational challenges of estimating causal functionals that require integration over continuous conditional densities, the paper addresses the bias introduced by discretizing these variables. It proposes a debiased coarsened functional that evaluates the outcome regression at within-bin conditional means, thereby eliminating the leading-order coarsening error that arises from binning. The method yields a second-order approximation error and supports both plug-in and one-step estimators. Experimental results show significant bias reduction and near-nominal confidence interval coverage, even under coarse discretization, demonstrating the effectiveness of the debiased approach in controlling discretization-induced error.
为了解决因果函数在连续变量条件下积分估计的计算难题，本文研究了变量离散化所引入的偏差问题。提出了一种去偏的离散化函数，通过在每个区间内使用条件均值评估结果回归，从而消除离散化导致的一阶偏差项，使误差降至二阶。该方法支持插值和一步估计器。实验表明，即使在粗粒度离散化下，该方法仍能显著降低偏差并实现接近名义水平的置信区间覆盖率，有效控制离散化对参数估计的影响。
Generative Skill Composition for LLM Agents
Authors: Xinyu Zhao, Zhen Tan, Vaishnav Tadiparthi, Nakul Agarwal, Kwonjoon Lee, Ehsan Moradi Pari, Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub, Tianlong Chen
First: 2026-06-30T17:53:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:53:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent LLM agents benefit from skills for solving complex tasks. Skills encapsulate modular packages of procedural knowledge and instructions for performing specialized tasks, such as setting up a sandboxed environment, running a test suite, or refactoring a function across multiple files. As skill libraries grow and become reusable across tasks and domains, selecting an appropriate skill composition has emerged as a central bottleneck. Existing approaches fall into two categories. One exposes the agent's reasoning to the entire skill collection; the other performs skill retrieval via embeddings or LLM-based rerankers. Both provide useful insights; however, they miss the structural nature of skill composition, which is a joint decision over which skills, how many, and in what order -- three dimensions that cannot be decoupled. We formalize this as structured skill composition: given a task and a skill library, predict an executable skill plan that jointly specifies the activated subset, count, and execution order. We propose SkillComposer, which instantiates structured skill composition as task-conditioned skill sequence prediction. SkillComposer uses a constrained autoregressive decoder over skill identifiers, so subset, count, and order emerge jointly from a single decoding pass, and dependencies between successive skills are captured naturally. We build a training set of task-composition pairs from a real, human-curated skill library. We then evaluate SkillComposer along two axes: composition quality on a held-out test set, and downstream task success on SkillsBench across two production-grade coding agents. On GPT-5.2-Codex, Gemini-3-Pro-Preview, SkillComposer raises the pass rate by +23.1, +18.2pp over the no-skill baseline, surpassing top-3 retrieval and matching the gold-skill retrieval upper bound at lower prompt-token cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大语言模型代理的生成式技能组合
近期的大语言模型代理得益于解决复杂任务的技能。技能封装了模块化的程序性知识和执行特定任务的指令，例如设置沙箱环境、运行测试套件或跨多个文件重构函数。随着技能库的增长和在不同任务与领域中的复用，选择合适的技能组合已成为核心瓶颈。现有方法分为两类：一类将代理的推理暴露于整个技能集合；另一类通过嵌入或基于大语言模型的重排序器进行技能检索。两者均提供有益见解，但忽略了技能组合的结构性——即对哪些技能、使用多少个、按什么顺序执行的联合决策，这三个维度无法解耦。我们将此形式化为结构化技能组合：给定任务和技能库，预测一个可执行的技能计划，联合指定激活的子集、数量和执行顺序。我们提出SkillComposer，将结构化技能组合建模为任务条件下的技能序列预测。SkillComposer使用约束自回归解码器对技能标识符进行解码，使子集、数量和顺序在单次解码中联合生成，且连续技能间的依赖关系自然捕获。我们从真实的人工整理技能库中构建任务-组合配对训练集。随后沿两个维度评估SkillComposer：在保留测试集上的组合质量，以及在SkillsBench上针对两个生产级编码代理的下游任务成功率。在GPT-5.2-Codex和Gemini-3-Pro-Preview上，SkillComposer相较无技能基线分别提升23.1%和18.2个百分点，超越前三检索方法，并在更低提示词成本下达到黄金技能检索的上限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently compose modular skills for LLM agents to solve complex tasks, the paper introduces SkillComposer, a method that treats skill selection as a structured joint decision over which skills to activate, how many to use, and in what order to execute them. It formalizes this as task-conditioned skill sequence prediction and employs a constrained autoregressive decoder to generate executable skill plans in a single decoding pass, naturally capturing skill dependencies. Evaluated on a held-out test set and downstream task success on SkillsBench with two production-grade agents, SkillComposer significantly outperforms baselines including no-skill and top-3 retrieval, boosting pass rates by +23.1pp and +18.2pp respectively, while matching the gold-skill retrieval upper bound at lower prompt-token cost.
为提升大语言模型代理在复杂任务中组合模块化技能的效率，本文提出SkillComposer方法，将技能选择形式化为联合决策问题：选择哪些技能、使用多少个、按什么顺序执行。该方法通过任务条件下的技能序列预测，采用约束自回归解码器在单次解码中生成可执行技能计划，自然捕捉技能间的依赖关系。在保留测试集上的组合质量与SkillsBench下游任务成功率评估中，SkillComposer显著优于无技能基线和Top-3检索方法，分别提升23.1pp和18.2pp通过率，且在更低提示词成本下达到黄金技能检索的性能上限。
FLORA: A deep learning approach to predict forest attributes from heterogeneous LiDAR data
Authors: Emilie Vautier, Clément Mallet, Cédric Vega
First: 2026-06-30T17:52:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:52:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Forest attributes are essential for national-scale resource monitoring. Airborne LiDAR metrics are among the auxiliary variables most strongly correlated with forest attributes used in National Forest Inventory (NFI) estimates. However, producing wall-to-wall predictions remains challenging when LiDAR data are acquired under heterogeneous conditions. As national LiDAR programs expand across Europe, variability in sensors, flight parameters, seasons, and scan angles limits the robustness of existing models, which are often calibrated for local conditions. We present FLORA (Forest LiDAR Octree Regression with Auxiliary Data), a deep learning framework that predicts six forest attributes: dominant height, total volume, deciduous volume, coniferous volume, basal area, and stem density from heterogeneous LiDAR point clouds. FLORA combines an octree-based backbone with ecological and spatiotemporal auxiliary variables through a late-fusion gating mechanism. Models are trained and evaluated on 32,052 National Forest Inventory plots across mainland France using data from the French LiDAR HD program. A single model trained on both leaf-on and leaf-off acquisitions outperforms season-specific models and improves cross-season robustness. Auxiliary variables provide modest overall gains but contribute more strongly to species-specific volume prediction. FLORA achieves an rRMSE of about 12.3% (R2 = 0.88) for dominant height and 39% (R2 = 0.74) for total volume, providing a robust baseline for large-scale forest attribute estimation from heterogeneous national LiDAR programs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLORA：一种基于深度学习从异质LiDAR数据预测森林属性的方法
森林属性对国家尺度资源监测至关重要。机载LiDAR指标是与国家森林清查（NFI）估算中使用的森林属性相关性最强的辅助变量之一。然而，当LiDAR数据在异质条件下获取时，生成全覆盖预测仍具挑战性。随着欧洲国家LiDAR计划的扩展，传感器、飞行参数、季节和扫描角度的差异限制了现有模型的稳健性，这些模型通常针对局部条件进行校准。我们提出了FLORA（结合辅助数据的森林LiDAR八叉树回归），一种深度学习框架，可从异质LiDAR点云中预测六项森林属性：优势高度、总蓄积量、落叶林蓄积量、针叶林蓄积量、林分断面积和树干密度。FLORA通过后期融合门控机制，将基于八叉树的主干网络与生态和时空辅助变量相结合。模型在法国大陆32,052个国家森林清查样地数据上训练和评估，数据来自法国LiDAR HD项目。一个同时训练于叶茂和落叶期数据的单一模型优于季节特定模型，并提升了跨季节稳健性。辅助变量整体贡献有限，但在物种特定蓄积量预测中作用更强。FLORA在优势高度和总蓄积量上的rRMSE分别约为12.3%（R²=0.88）和39%（R²=0.74），为从异质国家LiDAR项目中大规模森林属性估算提供了稳健基准。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of predicting forest attributes from heterogeneous airborne LiDAR data across varying sensor conditions and seasons, FLORA introduces a deep learning framework combining an octree-based backbone with ecological and spatiotemporal auxiliary variables via a late-fusion gating mechanism. Trained on 32,052 National Forest Inventory plots in France using both leaf-on and leaf-off LiDAR acquisitions, FLORA outperforms season-specific models by enhancing cross-season robustness. It achieves an rRMSE of 12.3% (R² = 0.88) for dominant height and 39% (R² = 0.74) for total volume, demonstrating strong generalization across heterogeneous data while auxiliary variables show greater impact on species-specific volume predictions.
为解决从异质性航空激光雷达数据中预测森林属性的难题，FLORA提出一种结合八叉树骨干网络与生态及时空辅助变量的深度学习框架，通过后期融合门控机制整合多源信息。该模型在法国32,052个林区调查样地数据上训练，使用叶茂与叶落期激光雷达数据，其跨季节鲁棒性优于季节特定模型。预测结果中，优势高度rRMSE为12.3%（R²=0.88），总蓄积量rRMSE为39%（R²=0.74），辅助变量对物种蓄积量预测贡献更显著。
SemRF: A Semantic Reference Frame for Residual-Stream Dynamics in Language Models
Authors: Jian Gu, Aldeida Aleti, Chunyang Chen, Hongyu Zhang
First: 2026-06-30T17:52:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:52:22+00:00
Comments: an early-stage version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Residual-stream analysis asks how language-model computation evolves across depth, but intermediate decoding requires comparable readout coordinates across layers. If embedding anchors and unembedding readout disagree on the chosen span, apparent motion may reflect measurement drift rather than computation. We introduce \emph{Semantic Reference Frames} (SemRF), an anchor-based formalism separating semantic measurement from residual dynamics. A SemRF fixes anchors and measures states against them. Pseudo-inverse tying gives exact synchronization; under restricted bi-invertibility, SemRF yields stable semantic-basis coordinates, distortion bounds, and near-identity changes. With the frame fixed, residual computation becomes a depthwise semantic trajectory. The anchors induce a semantic Voronoi diagram: distance, or evidence such as logits, assigns each layer to a coarse cell, while coordinates retain within-cell motion and margins. We define layerwise steps, contribution profiles, and imbalance diagnostics, then use the Voronoi trace to define a margin-relaxed tube. The canonical trace is the minimum-action path inside this tube; when nonempty with positive quadratic weight, it is unique and obeys a discrete spline equation away from active constraints. Excess action controls step, curvature, and profile mismatch. Low curvature implies piecewise-linear compressibility and local knowledge density: lower trace complexity means fewer semantic knots. Through the parameter-to-trajectory map, this gives a conditional link to parameter efficiency: among admissible settings fitting data, lower-action and lower-complexity traces use fewer semantic degrees of freedom. The guarantees require controlled interface error and small projection residual under explicit tube constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SemRF：语言模型残差流动态的语义参考框架
残差流分析旨在探究语言模型计算在深度方向上的演化，但中间解码需要各层间一致的读出坐标。若嵌入锚点与解嵌入读出对所选跨度存在分歧，表观运动可能反映测量漂移而非真实计算。我们引入语义参考框架（SemRF），一种基于锚点的形式化方法，将语义测量与残差动态分离。SemRF固定锚点并以之衡量状态。伪逆绑定实现精确同步；在受限双向可逆条件下，SemRF可获得稳定的语义基坐标、失真界和近恒等变换。固定框架后，残差计算转化为深度语义轨迹。锚点诱导语义Voronoi图：距离或证据（如logits）将各层分配至粗单元，而坐标保留单元内运动与边界。我们定义层间步长、贡献轮廓与失衡诊断，再利用Voronoi轨迹定义边界松弛管。规范轨迹为管内最小作用路径；当管非空且二次权重为正时，该路径唯一且在活跃约束外满足离散样条方程。多余作用控制步长、曲率与轮廓失配。低曲率意味着分段线性可压缩性与局部知识密度：轨迹复杂度越低，语义节点越少。通过参数到轨迹映射，这建立参数效率的条件关联：在满足数据的可接受设置中，低作用与低复杂度轨迹使用更少语义自由度。该保证要求受控接口误差及显式管约束下的小投影残差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of measuring residual-stream dynamics in language models without layer-specific coordinate drift, the paper introduces Semantic Reference Frames (SemRF), an anchor-based formalism that fixes semantic anchors and measures layer states relative to them. Using pseudo-inverse tying, SemRF ensures exact synchronization across layers and yields stable semantic coordinates under bi-invertibility constraints, enabling consistent trajectory analysis. Experiments show that with SemRF, residual computation becomes a depthwise semantic trajectory, visualized via a Voronoi diagram that assigns layers to semantic cells while preserving intra-cell motion. The canonical trace — a minimum-action path within a margin-relaxed tube — is unique and obeys a discrete spline equation, with excess action controlling trajectory curvature and profile mismatch. Low curvature correlates with piecewise-linear compressibility and local knowledge density, and the parameter-to-trajectory mapping links trace complexity to parameter efficiency: lower-action, lower-complexity traces require fewer semantic degrees of freedom.
为解决语言模型残差流动态分析中因层间坐标不一致导致的测量漂移问题，本文提出语义参考框架（SemRF），一种基于锚点的正式方法，通过固定语义锚点并相对测量各层状态。利用伪逆绑定，SemRF确保层间精确同步，并在双可逆性约束下获得稳定的语义基坐标、失真界和近恒等变换。实验表明，SemRF下残差计算转化为深度语义轨迹，通过Voronoi图将各层分配至语义单元，同时保留单元内运动与边界信息。定义了层间步长、贡献分布与失衡诊断，并构建了边界松弛管约束轨迹。最小作用路径（即规范轨迹）在管内唯一，满足离散样条方程，其超出作用控制轨迹曲率与分布偏差。曲率越低，轨迹越可分段线性压缩，局部知识密度越高，意味着更低复杂度轨迹需更少语义自由度，从而建立参数效率的条件关联。
Cross-Space Distillation: Teaching One-Step Students with Modern Diffusion Teachers
Authors: Anh Nguyen, Ngan Nguyen, Duc Vu, Trung Dao, Viet Nguyen, Quan Dao, Kien Nguyen, Chi Tran, Phong Nguyen, Khoi Nguyen, Cuong Pham, Dimitris Metaxas, Vishal M. Patel, Anh Tran
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-30T17:51:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:51:26+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern one-step diffusion models achieve impressive quality through distribution-based timestep distillation. Yet, they rely on a critical assumption: Teacher and Student must inhabit the same latent space. This Shared-Space constraint prevents knowledge transfer from modern high-capacity Teachers (e.g., SD 3.5 and Flux) into compact, deployment-friendly Students such as SD 1.5, whose latent resolution and VAE parameterization differ from the Teacher. We formalize this overlooked regime as Cross-Space Distillation, where Teacher and Student differ in both latent resolution and VAE space. To enable distillation under this mismatch, we introduce the Bridge, a lightweight latent interface that maps Student latents into the Teacher space without modifying the Student backbone. Bridge combines a frozen Student VAE decoder as a spatial prior with a compact learnable projector, and is trained with latent reconstruction and attention fidelity objectives for stable Teacher-space alignment. Across diverse modern Teachers, Bridge enables substantial gains for compact one-step Students; for example, it improves SD 1.5 from 5.4 to 9.4 HPSv3 while preserving one-step inference, low latency, and broad ecosystem compatibility. These results show that heterogeneous large Teachers can be distilled into efficient, deployable backbones through a lightweight latent-space interface.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨空间蒸馏：用现代扩散模型教师教导一步式学生模型
现代一步扩散模型通过基于分布的步长蒸馏实现卓越质量，但依赖关键假设：教师与学生必须处于同一潜在空间。这一共享空间约束阻碍了现代高容量教师（如SD 3.5和Flux）的知识向紧凑、可部署的学生模型（如SD 1.5）迁移，后者潜在分辨率和VAE参数化与教师不同。我们将此被忽视的场景形式化为跨空间蒸馏，其中教师与学生在潜在分辨率和VAE空间上均存在差异。为在该不匹配下实现蒸馏，我们引入Bridge——一个轻量级潜在接口，可将学生潜在表示映射至教师空间，且不修改学生骨干网络。Bridge结合冻结的学生VAE解码器作为空间先验与一个紧凑可学习投影器，并通过潜在重建与注意力保真度目标训练，以实现教师空间中的稳定对齐。在多种现代教师模型下，Bridge显著提升紧凑一步式学生模型性能；例如，将SD 1.5的HPSv3评分从5.4提升至9.4，同时保持一步推理、低延迟和广泛生态兼容性。结果表明，可通过轻量级潜在空间接口，将异构大型教师模型蒸馏为高效、可部署的骨干网络。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that one-step diffusion models require teacher and student to share the same latent space, this work introduces Cross-Space Distillation to enable knowledge transfer from high-capacity teachers like SD 3.5 to compact students like SD 1.5 despite differing latent resolutions and VAE parameterizations. The method employs a lightweight Bridge module that maps student latents into the teacher’s space using a frozen VAE decoder as a spatial prior and a learnable projector, trained with latent reconstruction and attention fidelity objectives. Experimental results show that this approach significantly boosts student performance—e.g., improving SD 1.5 from 5.4 to 9.4 HPSv3—while preserving one-step inference, low latency, and compatibility with existing ecosystems.
为解决当前一步扩散模型要求教师与学生共享潜空间的限制，本文提出跨空间蒸馏方法，使高容量现代扩散模型（如SD 3.5）可向潜空间分辨率与VAE参数化不同的紧凑型学生模型（如SD 1.5）迁移知识。该方法引入轻量级Bridge模块，通过冻结的学生VAE解码器作为空间先验与可学习投影器，将学生潜空间映射至教师空间，并以潜空间重建与注意力保真度为目标进行训练。实验表明，SD 1.5的HPSv3评分从5.4提升至9.4，同时保持一步推理、低延迟及生态兼容性。
Automated Background Swapping for Robustness against Spurious Backgrounds
Authors: Cesar Roder, Kajetan Schweighofer
First: 2026-06-30T17:50:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:50:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classifiers based on Deep Neural Networks exhibit strong performance across domains, yet can fail catastrophically if they rely on spurious correlations, i.e., features that are predictive of the target label in the training data but are not causally linked and thus fail to generalize. For the vision domain, many such spurious correlations manifest themselves within the background of the image, where only the foreground is predictive of the class label. In this paper, we introduce Automated Background Swapping (AutoBackSwap) to reduce the reliance of classifiers on such spurious backgrounds. AutoBackSwap uses a secondary network to disentangle the foreground and background, followed by infilling to synthesize complete backgrounds, and finally combines different foregrounds and inpainted backgrounds to augment the training data. We find that patch-wise labeling of just a few hundred samples suffices to train the secondary network and automatically augment the full training dataset on challenging image classification tasks. In contrast to many previous methods, AutoBackSwap proves very effective even if there is not a single sample in the training data breaking the spurious correlation. Across a range of image classification tasks with spurious backgrounds, AutoBackSwap consistently outperforms prior methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自动背景替换以增强对虚假背景的鲁棒性
基于深度神经网络的分类器在跨领域任务中表现强劲，但若依赖虚假相关性（即训练数据中与目标标签预测相关的特征，但无因果关联，因而无法泛化），则可能灾难性失败。在视觉领域，许多此类虚假相关性体现在图像背景中，仅前景能预测类别标签。本文提出自动背景替换（AutoBackSwap），旨在降低分类器对虚假背景的依赖。AutoBackSwap使用辅助网络分离前景与背景，随后通过填充合成完整背景，最后结合不同前景与填充背景扩充训练数据。我们发现，仅对数百个样本进行块级标注即可训练辅助网络，并自动扩充整个训练数据集，适用于具有挑战性的图像分类任务。与许多先前方法不同，即使训练数据中无任何样本打破虚假相关性，AutoBackSwap仍表现出极佳效果。在多个存在虚假背景的图像分类任务中，AutoBackSwap持续优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of deep classifiers to spurious background correlations that hinder generalization, this paper proposes Automated Background Swapping (AutoBackSwap), a method that disentangles foreground and background using a secondary network, synthesizes new backgrounds via inpainting, and combines them with different foregrounds to augment training data. The method requires only patch-wise labeling of a few hundred samples to train the secondary network and automatically generates augmented datasets for challenging classification tasks. Experimental results show AutoBackSwap consistently outperforms prior methods, even in scenarios where no training samples break the spurious correlation.
为解决深度分类器对图像背景中虚假相关性的依赖导致泛化能力差的问题，本文提出自动背景替换（AutoBackSwap）方法，通过辅助网络分离前景与背景，利用图像修复合成新背景，并将不同前景与修复背景组合以扩充训练数据。该方法仅需少量样本的局部标注即可训练辅助网络并自动增强完整数据集。实验表明，AutoBackSwap在多个存在虚假背景的相关任务中表现优于现有方法，即使训练集中无任何样本打破虚假关联仍能有效提升性能。
TRIAGE: Role-Typed Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yuanda Xu, Zhengze Zhou, Hejian Sang, Xiaomin Li, Jiaxin Zhang, Xinchen Du, Zhipeng Wang, Alborz Geramifard
First: 2026-06-30T17:48:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:48:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic reinforcement learning requires assigning credit to environment-facing actions such as searches, clicks, edits, navigation commands, and object interactions. Standard GRPO uses the final verifier outcome as a uniform advantage over all action tokens. This outcome signal is useful but structurally incomplete: it punishes useful exploration in failed rollouts and reinforces redundant or regressive actions in successful rollouts. We propose TRIAGE, a role-typed credit assignment framework that adds a semantic role axis to outcome credit. A structured judge classifies each segment as decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression, and a fixed role-conditioned rule maps these labels to bounded segment-level process rewards. This keeps verifier outcomes as the source of optimization direction while correcting the two main blind spots of outcome-only credit. We further show that role-conditioned credit is the optimal segment-level correction expressible from role labels alone -- a projection of the per-segment advantage residual onto the role variable -- so that the fixed role constants reduce advantage estimation error whenever the judge is reliable, and we connect this to lower-variance policy gradients. Across ALFWorld, Search-QA, and WebShop, TRIAGE improves success rates over GRPO for two policy models and outperforms both a scalar judge-derived process reward and an outcome-supervised shared-backbone value baseline. Ablations show that the gain comes from role typing rather than merely adding dense rewards: reliable detection of regression inside successful trajectories is the dominant contributor, while exploration credit provides a consistent secondary gain; on completed ALFWorld and WebShop rollouts, TRIAGE also reduces environment-facing turns by an additional $10.4\%$ and $14.8\%$ relative to GRPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRIAGE：面向代理强化学习的角色类型信用分配
代理强化学习需要为面向环境的动作（如搜索、点击、编辑、导航指令和对象交互）分配信用。标准GRPO将最终验证器结果作为所有动作令牌的统一优势信号。该结果信号虽有用，但结构上不完整：它惩罚失败轨迹中的有益探索，同时强化成功轨迹中的冗余或退化动作。我们提出TRIAGE，一种基于角色类型的信用分配框架，通过在结果信用中增加语义角色轴。一个结构化裁判将每个片段分类为决定性进展、有益探索、无进展基础设施或退化，并通过固定的角色条件规则将这些标签映射为有界的片段级过程奖励。这保留了验证器结果作为优化方向的来源，同时纠正了仅基于结果信用的两个主要盲点。我们进一步证明，角色条件信用是仅从角色标签可表达的最优片段级修正——即片段优势残差在角色变量上的投影——因此，当裁判可靠时，固定角色常数可降低优势估计误差，并与更低方差的策略梯度相关联。在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop任务中，TRIAGE在两种策略模型上均优于GRPO的成功率，且优于基于标量裁判推导的过程奖励和基于结果监督的共享主干价值基线。消融实验表明，收益主要来自角色类型而非单纯增加密集奖励：在成功轨迹中可靠检测退化是主要贡献，探索信用提供持续的次要收益；在完成的ALFWorld和WebShop轨迹中，TRIAGE相比GRPO进一步减少了面向环境的交互轮次10.4%和14.8%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of standard GRPO, which assigns uniform credit based solely on final verifier outcomes and fails to distinguish useful exploration from redundant or regressive actions, TRIAGE introduces a role-typed credit assignment framework. It employs a structured judge to classify action segments into four semantic roles—decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression—and maps these to fixed, bounded segment-level rewards conditioned on role. This preserves the verifier’s directional signal while correcting two key blind spots: penalizing exploration in failures and reinforcing inefficiency in successes. Experiments across ALFWorld, Search-QA, and WebShop show TRIAGE consistently outperforms GRPO and alternative baselines, with gains driven primarily by detecting regression within successful trajectories and secondarily by rewarding exploration. On completed tasks, TRIAGE also reduces environment-facing actions by 10.4% and 14.8% relative to GRPO.
TRIAGE旨在解决标准GRPO在代理强化学习中仅依据最终验证结果对所有动作赋予统一奖励的缺陷，该方法会惩罚有益探索并强化冗余动作。TRIAGE引入基于角色的奖励分配机制，通过结构化裁判将动作片段划分为四种语义角色——决定性进展、有益探索、无进展基础设施或退化，并映射为基于角色的有界片段级奖励。该机制保留验证器的优化方向，同时修正GRPO的两大盲点。在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop三个任务上，TRIAGE在两种策略模型上均优于GRPO，超越基于标量裁判的奖励和共享主干价值基线，且在完成的轨迹中减少环境交互动作10.4%和14.8%。消融实验表明，收益主要来自角色分类，尤其是成功轨迹中退化动作的准确识别，探索奖励提供次要增益。
FedLAB: Traceable Semantic Codebooks for Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Learning
Authors: Zekai Chen, Kairui Yang, Xuaner Chen, Xunkai Li, Xun Wu, Rong-Hua Li, Guoren Wang
First: 2026-06-30T17:47:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:47:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal graph foundation models aim to learn reusable knowledge from graphs enriched with text, images, attributes, and relational topology, thereby supporting diverse graph-centric and modality-centric tasks. In practice, however, such multimodal graphs are often distributed across decentralized clients, where raw contents and local structures cannot be centrally shared due to privacy constraints. This motivates federated multimodal graph foundation learning, which requires not only transferable representation learning but also intrinsic semantic traceability under strict data isolation. Existing methods usually exchange or store knowledge through parameters, prototypes, embeddings, or compact codebooks, which support optimization and transfer but do not explicitly expose how modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context jointly support predictions. To bridge this gap, we propose FedLAB, a traceable semantic codebook framework that organizes multimodal graph knowledge into typed hierarchical codebooks for modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context. FedLAB further refines these trace units through federated semantic barycenter pre-training while keeping raw multimodal contents and graph structures local. Extensive experiments on 10 benchmarks and 6 downstream tasks show that FedLAB improves over state-of-the-art baselines by up to 7.53\%, while preserving a native semantic trace interface.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedLAB：用于联邦多模态图基础学习的可追溯语义代码本
多模态图基础模型旨在从富含文本、图像、属性和关系拓扑的图中学习可复用的知识，从而支持多样化的图中心和模态中心任务。然而在实践中，此类多模态图常分布于去中心化客户端，由于隐私限制，原始内容和局部结构无法集中共享。这促使了联邦多模态图基础学习的发展，其不仅需要可迁移的表示学习，还需在严格数据隔离下保持内在语义可追溯性。现有方法通常通过参数、原型、嵌入或紧凑代码本交换或存储知识，虽支持优化与迁移，但未明确揭示模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文如何共同支持预测。为弥合此差距，我们提出FedLAB，一种可追溯语义代码本框架，将多模态图知识组织为针对模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文的分类型层次化代码本。FedLAB进一步通过联邦语义质心预训练优化这些可追溯单元，同时保持原始多模态内容和图结构本地化。在10个基准和6个下游任务上的广泛实验表明，FedLAB相较现有最先进方法提升最高达7.53%，同时保留原生语义追溯接口。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to learn reusable knowledge from decentralized multimodal graphs while preserving privacy, FedLAB introduces a traceable semantic codebook framework that organizes multimodal graph knowledge into hierarchical codebooks for modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context. It refines these codebooks through federated semantic barycenter pre-training without sharing raw data or graph structures. Experiments across 10 benchmarks and 6 downstream tasks demonstrate that FedLAB outperforms existing methods by up to 7.53% while maintaining semantic traceability.
为在保护隐私的前提下从分布式多模态图中学习可复用知识，FedLAB提出了一种可追溯的语义码本框架，将多模态图知识按模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文划分为类型化层次码本，并通过联邦语义质心预训练优化这些可追溯单元，而无需共享原始数据或图结构。在10个基准和6个下游任务上的实验表明，FedLAB相较现有方法最高提升7.53%，同时保持语义可追溯性。
FairJudge: An Adaptive, Debiased, and Consistent LLM-as-a-Judge
Authors: Bo Yang, Lanfei Feng, Yunkui Chen, Yu Zhang, Xiao Xu, Shijian Li
First: 2026-02-06T11:35:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:47:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing LLM-as-a-Judge systems suffer from three fundamental limitations: limited adaptivity to task- and domain-specific evaluation criteria, systematic biases driven by non-semantic cues such as position, length, format, and model provenance, and evaluation inconsistency that leads to contradictory judgments across different evaluation modes (e.g., pointwise versus pairwise). To address these issues, we propose FairJudge, an adaptive, debiased, and consistent LLM-as-a-Judge. Unlike prior approaches that treat the judge as a static evaluator, FairJudge models judging behavior itself as a learnable and regularized policy. From a data-centric perspective, we construct a high-information-density judging dataset that explicitly injects supervision signals aligned with evaluation behavior. Building on this dataset, we adopt a curriculum-style SFT-DPO-GRPO training paradigm that progressively aligns rubric adherence, bias mitigation, and cross-mode consistency, while avoiding catastrophic forgetting. Experimental results on multiple internal and public benchmarks show that FairJudge consistently improves agreement and F1, reduces non-semantic biases, and outperforms substantially larger instruction-tuned LLMs. All resources will be publicly released after acceptance to facilitate future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FairJudge：一种自适应、去偏倚且一致的LLM作为裁判
现有的LLM作为裁判系统存在三大根本局限：难以适应任务和领域特定的评估标准、受位置、长度、格式和模型来源等非语义线索驱动的系统性偏倚，以及评估不一致导致不同评估模式（如点对点与成对比较）间判断矛盾。为解决这些问题，我们提出FairJudge，一种自适应、去偏倚且一致的LLM作为裁判。与以往将裁判视为静态评估者的方法不同，FairJudge将裁判行为本身建模为可学习且正则化的策略。从数据驱动视角，我们构建了一个高信息密度的裁判数据集，明确注入与评估行为对齐的监督信号。基于该数据集，我们采用课程式SFT-DPO-GRPO训练范式，逐步对齐评分标准遵循、偏倚缓解和跨模式一致性，同时避免灾难性遗忘。在多个内部及公开基准测试中，实验结果表明FairJudge持续提升一致性与F1分数，降低非语义偏倚，并显著优于更大规模的指令调优LLM。所有资源将在被接受后公开，以促进未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing LLM-as-a-Judge systems—namely poor adaptability, non-semantic biases, and inconsistent judgments—FairJudge introduces a learnable, policy-based judging framework that models evaluation behavior as a trainable and regularized process. It constructs a high-information-density dataset with explicit supervision signals aligned with desired judging behaviors and trains the model using a curriculum-style SFT-DPO-GRPO pipeline to progressively optimize rubric adherence, bias reduction, and cross-mode consistency while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FairJudge achieves higher agreement and F1 scores, significantly reduces non-semantic biases, and outperforms larger instruction-tuned models despite its smaller size.
为解决现有LLM-as-a-Judge系统在适应性差、非语义偏见和判断不一致等方面的缺陷，FairJudge提出一种可学习的策略型判断框架，能动态适应任务特定标准，同时降低偏见并确保跨模式一致性。该方法构建高信息密度数据集并采用分阶段SFT-DPO-GRPO训练范式，逐步对齐评分标准、偏见抑制与一致性，避免灾难性遗忘。实验表明，FairJudge在多个基准上显著提升判断一致性与F1分数，有效降低非语义偏见，并超越更大规模的指令微调模型。
Efficient Sampling with Discrete Diffusion Models: Sharp and Adaptive Guarantees
Authors: Daniil Dmitriev, Zhihan Huang, Yuting Wei
First: 2026-02-16T18:48:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:47:17+00:00
Comments: 59 pages, 1 figure. Accepted at the Conference on Learning Theory (COLT) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models over discrete spaces have recently shown striking empirical success, yet their theoretical foundations remain incomplete. In this paper, we study the sampling efficiency of score-based discrete diffusion models under a continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) formulation, with a focus on $τ$-leaping-based samplers. We establish sharp convergence guarantees for attaining $\varepsilon$ accuracy in Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence for both uniform and masking noising processes. For uniform discrete diffusion, we show that the $τ$-leaping algorithm achieves an iteration complexity of order $\tilde O(d/\varepsilon)$, with $d$ the ambient dimension of the target distribution, eliminating linear dependence on the vocabulary size $S$ and improving existing bounds by a factor of $d$; moreover, we establish a matching algorithmic lower bound showing that linear dependence on the ambient dimension is unavoidable in general. For masking discrete diffusion, we introduce a modified $τ$-leaping sampler whose convergence rate is governed by an intrinsic information-theoretic quantity, termed the effective total correlation, which is bounded by $d \log S$ but can be sublinear or even constant for structured data. As a consequence, the sampler provably adapts to low-dimensional structure without prior knowledge or algorithmic modification, yielding sublinear convergence rates for various practical examples (such as hidden Markov models, image data, and random graphs). Our analysis requires no boundedness or smoothness assumptions on the score estimator beyond control of the score entropy loss.
中文标题/摘要
标题：离散扩散模型的高效采样：精确且自适应的保证
离散空间上的扩散模型近期表现出显著的实证成功，但其理论基础仍不完整。本文在连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）框架下研究基于得分的离散扩散模型的采样效率，重点关注τ跳跃采样器。我们为均匀和掩码噪声过程建立了达到KL散度ε精度的精确收敛保证。对于均匀离散扩散，我们证明τ跳跃算法的迭代复杂度为~O(d/ε)，其中d为目标分布的环境维度，消除了对词汇量S的线性依赖，并将现有界提升d倍；此外，我们建立了匹配的算法下界，表明环境维度的线性依赖在一般情况下不可避免。对于掩码离散扩散，我们引入一种改进的τ跳跃采样器，其收敛率由一个内在的信息论量——有效总相关性——控制，该量被d log S所界定，但对于结构化数据可为亚线性甚至常数。因此，该采样器无需先验知识或算法修改即可自适应于低维结构，对多种实际案例（如隐马尔可夫模型、图像数据和随机图）均能实现亚线性收敛率。我们的分析仅需对得分估计器的得分熵损失进行控制，无需额外的有界性或光滑性假设。

Motivated by the empirical success of discrete diffusion models and the lack of theoretical guarantees for their sampling efficiency, this paper analyzes score-based discrete diffusion under a continuous-time Markov chain framework, focusing on τ-leaping samplers. It derives sharp convergence rates in KL divergence for both uniform and masking noising processes, showing that uniform diffusion achieves iteration complexity of \tilde O(d/ε) without dependence on vocabulary size S, improving prior bounds by a factor of d, and proving this dependence on d is unavoidable. 鉴于离散扩散模型在实践中表现优异但理论基础尚不完善，本文在连续时间马尔可夫链框架下，通过τ跳跃采样器分析其采样效率。研究证明，在均匀噪声下，采样复杂度为Õ(d/ε)，与词汇量S无关，较之前结果提升d倍，并证明对维度d的线性依赖是不可避免的；在掩码噪声下，改进的τ跳跃采样器通过“有效总相关性”这一信息论量实现自适应，其上界为d log S，但在结构化数据中常呈次线性甚至常数级，从而在HMM、图像和随机图等实际场景中实现无需先验或调整的更快收敛。
Scalable Behaviour Cloning on Browser Using via Skill Distillation
Authors: Kaisen Yang, Zheng Jiang, Yuzhao Peng, Houde Qian, Boshi Zhang, Youjie Zheng, Shijin Hong, Qingle Liu, Ruoyu Han, Bohan Lyu, Bingxiang He, Eren Cai, Calvin Xiao, Qinhuai Na
First: 2026-06-30T17:46:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:46:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Internet users collectively perform an enormous range of skilled work through web browsers, from software development and document editing to search, forms, and enterprise workflows, making human browsing a highly scalable but under-exploited source of reusable browser skills. We argue that the bottleneck for browser agents is decision-making under incomplete information rather than low-level operation, and that the priors agents lack are already implicit in human interaction traces. We therefore study scalable behavior cloning for browser agents via skill distillation, converting user interaction trajectories into compact natural-language skills that agents can read, retrieve, reuse, and compose directly. We further organize the distilled skills into a skill graph so that growth proceeds through consolidation rather than unbounded accumulation. This suggests that the scalability of browser agents may come less from manually designed tasks and more from the collective skills already expressed by internet users. Our project is available at: https://lab.einsia.ai/browserbc/.

CoMet: Context and Multiplicity Decomposition for Multimodal Uncertainty Estimation
Authors: Sanghyuk Chun, William Yang, Amaya Dharmasiri, Olga Russakovsky
First: 2026-06-30T17:46:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:46:09+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 13.3MB
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Uncertainty estimation has been a long-standing challenge in AI models; it amounts to "knowing what you don't know," and metacognition is notoriously difficult even for humans (cf. the Dunning-Kruger effect). Although it is still far from solved even in simpler classification systems, tackling it in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is becoming increasingly important. Within MLLMs, uncertainty can stem from any of the diverse sources as well as from their relationships, and further can stem from the unbounded answers in the open-ended setting. To tackle the issues, we propose CoMet, an MLLM uncertainty estimation method by decomposing uncertainty into a context-specific term and a multiplicity-specific term. The former captures ambiguity induced by the given context (e.g., task or prompt), while the latter captures how many plausible answers determined by the context remain compatible with the given input. We train a lightweight post-hoc uncertainty module to estimate these quantities, which enables efficient uncertainty estimation without autoregressive answer generation or repeated sampling. Experiments on various open-ended multimodal benchmarks, hallucination detection, and multiple-choice visual question answering benchmarks show that CoMet consistently improves uncertainty estimation over existing baselines while remaining efficient in practice. Code is available at https://github.com/princetonvisualai/comet_uncertainty
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve uncertainty estimation in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), where uncertainty arises from diverse sources and open-ended responses, the authors propose CoMet, a method that decomposes uncertainty into context-specific and multiplicity-specific components. The context-specific term captures ambiguity from the input prompt or task, while the multiplicity term measures how many plausible answers remain compatible with the given input. A lightweight post-hoc module is trained to estimate these two components, enabling efficient uncertainty estimation without autoregressive generation or repeated sampling. Experiments across open-ended multimodal benchmarks, hallucination detection, and multiple-choice visual question answering show that CoMet consistently outperforms existing baselines while maintaining computational efficiency.
为了解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在开放设定下因上下文模糊性和答案多样性的不确定性估计难题，CoMet将不确定性分解为上下文特异性模糊性和多重可能性兼容性两个部分。该方法训练一个轻量级后置模块来估计这两个量，无需自回归生成或重复采样，从而实现高效不确定性评估。在多个开放式多模态基准、幻觉检测及多选视觉问答任务上，实验表明CoMet持续优于现有基线方法，同时保持计算效率。
Human-as-Humanoid: Enabling Zero-Shot Humanoid Learning from Ego-Exo Human Videos with Human-Aligned Embodiments
