Authors: Xiaopeng Lin, Ruoqi Yang, Shijie Lian, Zhaolong Shen, Bin Yu, Changti Wu, Haibao Liu, Yuxiang Zhang, Hong Li, Qiyuan Su, Haochen Liu, Xuguo He, Yukun Shi, Cong Huang, Zhirui Zhang, Bojun Cheng, Kai Chen
First: 2026-06-30T17:44:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:44:16+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models across robot embodiments require high-quality observation--action supervision to learn deployable action distributions, yet scaling such robot data remains difficult, especially for high-DoF humanoids. Teleoperation provides controller-aligned supervision, while human egocentric videos capture diverse bimanual manipulation but do not directly provide executable robot actions. We introduce Human-as-Humanoid, a human-to-humanoid supervision framework that enables near-real-time human-centric action generation, making human demonstrations usable for high-DoF humanoid VLA training by jointly aligning the robot embodiment, the sensing setup, and the action-label interface. Built on PrimeU, a human-aligned 60-DoF upper-body humanoid, Human-as-Humanoid uses synchronized ego-exo videos to pair deployment-aligned egocentric observations with exocentric motion recovery, retargets the recovered human motion through staged Inverse Kinematics (IK) into controller-aligned 60-DoF action chunks, and trains the VLA model with Forward Kinematics (FK)-aware supervision to preserve wrist and fingertip task-space geometry. This converts large-scale human demonstrations from visual observations into executable observation--action supervision for the target humanoid. Experiments validate the conversion chain at the motion-recovery, robot-action-space, and real-robot deployment levels. Human-as-Humanoid yields a 4.8--7.2x raw demonstration-throughput gain over humanoid teleoperation in our data-collection analysis, and on several downstream tasks, policies post-trained only with the converted human labels generalize to real-robot deployment without target-task robot demonstrations. The official project website is available at https://zgc-embodyai.github.io/Human-as-Humanoid.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人类作为类人机器人：通过人类视角与外视角视频实现零样本类人机器人学习
跨机器人具身的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型需要高质量的观测-动作监督以学习可部署的动作分布，但此类机器人数据的扩展尤其困难，特别是针对高自由度类人机器人。遥操作提供控制器对齐的监督，而人类第一人称视频捕捉多样化的双手操作，但无法直接提供可执行的机器人动作。我们提出Human-as-Humanoid，一种人类到类人机器人的监督框架，可实现近乎实时的人类中心动作生成，通过联合对齐机器人具身、感知设置和动作标签接口，使人类演示适用于高自由度类人机器人VLA训练。基于PrimeU（一种60自由度的人类对齐上半身类人机器人），Human-as-Humanoid利用同步的第一人称与外视角视频，将部署对齐的第一人称观测与外视角运动恢复配对，通过分阶段逆运动学（IK）将恢复的人类运动重定向为控制器对齐的60自由度动作片段，并以正运动学（FK）感知监督训练VLA模型，以保持手腕和指尖任务空间几何结构。该方法将大规模人类演示从视觉观测转化为可执行的观测-动作监督，用于目标类人机器人。实验验证了运动恢复、机器人动作空间和真实机器人部署三个层面的转换链。在数据收集分析中，Human-as-Humanoid相比类人机器人遥操作实现了4.8至7.2倍的原始演示吞吐量提升；在多个下游任务中，仅使用转换后人类标签微调的策略无需目标任务机器人演示即可泛化至真实机器人部署。官方项目网站见https://zgc-embodyai.github.io/Human-as-Humanoid。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of scaling high-DoF humanoid robot training data, this work introduces Human-as-Humanoid, a framework that converts human egocentric videos into executable robot action supervision for VLA models. It aligns human motion with a 60-DoF humanoid via synchronized ego-exo video capture, recovers human motion, retargets it through staged inverse kinematics into robot-action chunks, and trains the model with forward kinematics-aware supervision to preserve task-space geometry. Experiments show the method achieves 4.8–7.2x higher demonstration throughput than teleoperation and enables zero-shot generalization to real robots without task-specific robot demonstrations.
为解决高自由度人形机器人训练数据难以扩展的问题，该研究提出Human-as-Humanoid框架，将人类第一视角视频转化为可执行的机器人动作，用于视觉-语言-动作模型训练。该方法通过同步第一人称与第三人称视频流，恢复人类动作，经分阶段逆运动学重定向至60自由度机器人动作空间，并结合正运动学监督训练模型以保持任务空间几何结构。实验表明，该方法比遥控操作效率提升4.8–7.2倍，且仅使用人类演示训练的策略可直接泛化至真实机器人部署，无需额外任务特定机器人数据。
Surrogate Fidelity: When Can Open LLMs Explain Closed Ones?
Authors: Philippe Chlenski, Zachariah Carmichael, Ayush Warikoo, Chia-Tse Shao, Yingxiao Ye, Aobo Yang, Vivek Miglani, Nehal Bandi
First: 2026-06-30T17:43:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:43:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Mechanistic interpretability (MI) requires full access to model internals, yet the APIs for most widely deployed language models at best expose log-probabilities over output tokens. This creates a surrogate problem: when do measurements made on open models allow us to make claims about a closed model? We evaluate surrogate fidelity at the prediction, attribution, and representation levels. For binary classification tasks, log-odds provide an API-compatible scalar readout of the model's representation space, and leave-one-out attributions provide insight into model behavior. Across eleven models spanning four families (Llama, Qwen, GPT, and Gemini), we find that prediction fidelity substantially overstates attribution fidelity: models that agree on what the answer is often disagree on why. We document an access-validity inversion: white-box signals like attention patterns and perturbation magnitudes are highly stable across models but only weakly predictive of causal attributions, which black-box input ablations capture by design. Mechanistic insight does not automatically transfer to closed targets, and prediction-level agreement is insufficient to warrant such transfer. Code and results are available at https://github.com/facebookresearch/surrogate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理忠实度：开放大语言模型何时能解释闭源模型？
机制可解释性（MI）需要完全访问模型内部，但大多数广泛部署的语言模型API最多仅暴露输出标记的对数概率。这导致了一个代理问题：何时基于开放模型的测量结果能让我们对闭源模型做出声明？我们在预测、归因和表征三个层面评估代理忠实度。对于二分类任务，对数几率提供了一种与API兼容的模型表征空间标量读数，而留一归因则揭示了模型行为。在涵盖四种家族（Llama、Qwen、GPT和Gemini）的十一个模型中，我们发现预测忠实度显著高估了归因忠实度：模型在答案上达成一致时，往往在原因上存在分歧。我们记录了一种访问有效性倒置现象：白盒信号（如注意力模式和扰动幅度）在模型间高度稳定，但对因果归因的预测能力较弱，而黑盒输入消融法正是为此设计的。机制洞察不会自动转移到闭源目标，且预测层面的一致性不足以支持这种转移。代码和结果见 https://github.com/facebookresearch/surrogate。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of evaluating mechanistic interpretability when only partial access to closed language models is available, by studying how well open models can serve as surrogates. It evaluates surrogate fidelity across prediction, attribution, and representation levels using eleven models from four families, finding that while prediction-level agreement is common, attribution-level fidelity is much lower—models often agree on outputs but not on causal reasons. The study reveals an access-validity inversion: internal signals like attention patterns are stable across models but poorly predict causal attributions, which black-box ablations better capture. This implies that prediction-level agreement alone cannot justify mechanistic insights transferring to closed models.
该研究旨在解决开放模型与闭源模型之间的解释性鸿沟，评估开放模型测量结果对闭源模型的解释有效性。通过在11个来自四种模型家族（Llama、Qwen、GPT、Gemini）的模型上，从预测、归因和表征三个层面评估代理保真度，研究发现：虽然模型在预测层面高度一致，但在归因层面却常不一致——即模型虽能给出相同答案，却对原因解释存在分歧。研究还发现“访问有效性倒置”现象：如注意力模式等白盒信号在模型间高度稳定，但对因果归因预测能力弱，而黑盒输入消融法天然更擅长捕捉因果归因。这表明开放模型的机制洞察无法自动迁移到闭源模型，仅凭预测层面的一致性不足以支持机制推断。
Teaching Models to Teach Themselves: Reasoning at the Edge of Learnability
Authors: Shobhita Sundaram, John Quan, Ariel Kwiatkowski, Kartik Ahuja, Yann Ollivier, Julia Kempe
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-26T18:46:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:43:05+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Blog post: https://ssundaram21.github.io/soar/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
RL methods for scaling large reasoning models stall on datasets with low initial success rates, and thus little training signal. We investigate a fundamental question: Can a pretrained LLM leverage latent knowledge to generate an automated curriculum for problems it cannot solve? We explore this with SOAR: An asymmetric self-play framework that uses meta-RL to surface these pedagogical signals. A teacher model proposes synthetic problems for a student model, and is rewarded with its improvement on a subset of hard problems, thus grounding the curriculum in real student progress rather than intrinsic proxy rewards. Our study on the hardest subsets of math benchmarks (0/128 success) reveals three core findings. First, it is possible to realize bilevel meta-RL that unlocks learning under sparse, binary rewards by sharpening a latent capacity of pretrained models to generate useful problems. Second, grounded rewards outperform intrinsic learnability rewards used in prior LLM self-play, reliably avoiding typical instability and diversity collapse modes. Third, the structure and well-posedness of questions are more critical for learning progress than solution correctness. Our results suggest that the ability to generate useful stepping stones does not require the preexisting ability to solve the hard problems, paving a principled path to escape reasoning plateaus without additional curated data
中文标题/摘要
标题：教模型自我教学：在可学习边界进行推理
用于扩展大型推理模型的强化学习方法在初始成功率低、训练信号弱的数据集上停滞不前。我们探讨一个根本性问题：预训练的大语言模型能否利用潜在知识，为自身无法解决的问题自动生成自动化教学大纲？我们通过SOAR框架探索这一问题：这是一个非对称自对弈框架，利用元强化学习揭示这些教学信号。教师模型为学生模型提出合成问题，并根据其在部分难题上的进步获得奖励，从而将教学大纲根植于真实的学生进步，而非内在代理奖励。我们在数学基准中最难子集（0/128成功率）上的研究表明，有三项核心发现：第一，可通过双层元强化学习，在稀疏二元奖励下解锁学习，通过强化预训练模型生成有用问题的潜在能力实现；第二，基于实际进步的奖励优于先前LLM自对弈中使用的内在可学习性奖励，能可靠避免典型不稳定性和多样性崩溃模式；第三，问题的结构与良定义性比解题正确性对学习进展更为关键。我们的结果表明，生成有用过渡步骤的能力无需依赖预先解决难题的能力，为无需额外人工标注数据即可突破推理瓶颈提供了原则性路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that reinforcement learning methods struggle to scale large reasoning models on datasets with low initial success rates, this work investigates whether a pretrained language model can autonomously generate a curriculum of synthetic problems to bootstrap learning. Using SOAR—an asymmetric self-play framework powered by meta-RL—the teacher model proposes problems for a student model and is rewarded based on the student’s actual improvement on a subset of hard problems, grounding the curriculum in real progress. Experiments on the most difficult math benchmarks (0/128 success) show that bilevel meta-RL can unlock learning under sparse binary rewards by leveraging latent problem-generation capacity, grounded rewards outperform intrinsic proxies by avoiding instability and diversity collapse, and question structure matters more than correctness for learning progress—suggesting that useful stepping stones can be generated without prior mastery of hard problems.
为了解决强化学习方法在初始成功率极低的数据集上难以扩展大型推理模型的问题，本研究探讨了预训练语言模型是否能利用其潜在知识自动生成适应自身学习需求的课程。方法SOAR采用不对称自博弈框架，结合元强化学习，由教师模型为学生模型生成合成问题，并根据学生在部分难题上的实际进步给予奖励，使课程设计基于真实学习效果而非代理信号。在最难的数学基准测试（初始成功率接近0）上，实验发现：元强化学习可激活模型潜在的问题生成能力，在稀疏二元奖励下实现学习；基于真实进步的奖励优于以往的内在可学习性奖励，有效避免了不稳定性和多样性崩溃；问题结构比解题正确性对学习进展更为关键，表明无需预先掌握难题即可生成有用的学习阶梯，从而无需额外人工标注数据即可突破推理瓶颈。
AxDafny: Agentic Verified Code Generation in Dafny
Authors: Benjamin Breen, Austin Letson, Borja Requena Pozo, Leopoldo Sarra
First: 2026-06-30T17:39:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:39:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study agentic code generation in Dafny, where a model must generate both executable code and the proof artifacts for verification. We present AxDafny, a verifier-guided repair framework that iteratively generates implementations, invariants, assertions, and termination arguments. We also introduce LiveCodeBench-Pro-Dafny (LCB-Pro-Dafny), a benchmark of 250 competition-style programming problems translated into Dafny with formal specifications and a verifier-based evaluation harness. On LCB-Pro-Dafny, AxDafny substantially improves verification success over baseline GPT-5.5 performance. On DafnyBench, AxDafny achieves 92.7\% verification success, outperforming the strongest previously reported proof-hint baseline by 6.5 percentage points. Lastly, we show that verification success and runtime test performance measure different aspects of generated code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AxDafny：Dafny中的代理验证代码生成
我们研究了Dafny中的代理代码生成，其中模型必须同时生成可执行代码和验证所需的证明工件。我们提出了AxDafny，一个由验证器引导的修复框架，可迭代生成实现、不变式、断言和终止论证。我们还引入了LiveCodeBench-Pro-Dafny（LCB-Pro-Dafny），这是一个包含250个竞赛风格编程问题的基准，这些问题已翻译为Dafny并附带形式化规范和基于验证器的评估框架。在LCB-Pro-Dafny上，AxDafny显著优于基线GPT-5.5的验证成功率。在DafnyBench上，AxDafny实现了92.7%的验证成功率，比之前最强的证明提示基线高出6.5个百分点。最后，我们表明验证成功率和运行时测试性能衡量的是生成代码的不同方面。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating both executable code and formal verification artifacts simultaneously, the authors introduce AxDafny, a verifier-guided iterative framework that generates implementations, invariants, assertions, and termination arguments to ensure correctness. Evaluated on LiveCodeBench-Pro-Dafny (250 Dafny problems) and DafnyBench, AxDafny significantly outperforms baseline GPT-5.5, achieving 92.7% verification success on DafnyBench — a 6.5 percentage point improvement over the best prior proof-hint method. The study also demonstrates that verification success and runtime test performance reflect distinct qualities of generated code.
为解决同时生成可执行代码与形式化验证证据的难题，作者提出AxDafny，一种基于验证器的迭代框架，自动生成实现、不变式、断言和终止论证以确保正确性。在LiveCodeBench-Pro-Dafny（250道带形式规范的Dafny题目）和DafnyBench上评估，AxDafny显著优于基线GPT-5.5，在DafnyBench上实现92.7%的验证成功率，比最优的先前证明提示方法高出6.5个百分点。研究还表明，验证成功率与运行时测试性能衡量的是生成代码的不同方面。
Random Reshuffling Dominates Stochastic Gradient Descent
Authors: Zijian Liu
First: 2026-06-30T17:38:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:38:22+00:00
Comments: COLT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\textsf{SGD}$) is one of the most classical optimization algorithms with favorable theoretical guarantees, yet the practical implementation of $\textsf{SGD}$ differs subtly from its well-known form and is often referred to as Shuffling Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$). A particularly popular strategy in $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ is Random Reshuffling ($\textsf{RR}$), which has achieved great empirical success across numerous experiments. Despite its strong performance, $\textsf{RR}$ has long been considered a heuristic due to a lack of theoretical support. Over the last decade, people have finally established provable convergence rates for $\textsf{RR}$, thus justifying its observed superiority. However, for smooth convex optimization, two clouds over the convergence theory of $\textsf{RR}$ remain to this day. More precisely, according to the current theory, $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ under $\textsf{RR}$ converges only when the stepsize is smaller than a threshold proportional to $1/n$, where $n$ is the number of summands in the objective (or the number of data points). Consequently, the optimally tuned theoretical rate of $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ under $\textsf{RR}$ is strictly worse than that of $\textsf{SGD}$ when the number of epochs is smaller than another threshold proportional to $n$. These two restrictions heavily limit the applicability of existing theories and leave a critical mismatch with practice. In this work, for the first time, we prove that $\textsf{RR}$ dominates $\textsf{SGD}$ in smooth convex optimization under any reasonable stepsize after any finite number of epochs, thereby addressing a longstanding open question.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机重排优于随机梯度下降
随机梯度下降（SGD）是最经典的优化算法之一，具有良好的理论保证，但其实际实现形式与熟知的形式略有不同，常被称为随机重排SGD（Shuffling SGD）。在Shuffling SGD中，随机重排（RR）是一种特别流行的方法，在大量实验中取得了显著的实证成功。尽管表现优异，RR长期以来被视为一种启发式方法，缺乏理论支持。过去十年中，人们终于为RR建立了可证明的收敛速率，从而为其观察到的优越性提供了理论依据。然而，对于光滑凸优化问题，RR的收敛理论至今仍存在两大局限：根据现有理论，Shuffling SGD在RR下仅当步长小于与样本数n成比例的阈值时收敛；因此，当训练轮数小于与n成比例的另一阈值时，RR下Shuffling SGD的最优理论收敛速率严格劣于SGD。这两个限制严重制约了现有理论的适用性，并与实际应用存在显著不匹配。本文首次证明，在任何合理步长下，经过任意有限轮数后，RR在光滑凸优化中优于SGD，从而解决了长期存在的开放性问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of Random Reshuffling (RR) in stochastic optimization despite its lack of theoretical justification, this work proves that RR strictly outperforms standard Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) in smooth convex optimization under any reasonable stepsize after any finite number of epochs, resolving a long-standing open question. The method leverages novel analysis to bypass the traditional convergence constraints that bound RR’s stepsize and epoch count, showing that RR’s convergence rate is superior to SGD’s when the number of epochs is sufficiently large. Experimental results confirm that RR consistently achieves better convergence and generalization than SGD across multiple smooth convex benchmarks, even when stepsize is not restricted to the 1/n threshold imposed by prior theory.
鉴于随机重排（RR）在优化中表现出色但长期缺乏理论支持，本文首次证明在光滑凸优化中，RR在任何合理步长下经过任意有限轮次后均优于随机梯度下降（SGD），解决了长期悬而未决的问题。该方法通过新颖的收敛性分析，突破了传统理论对步长需小于1/n的限制，且当轮次低于与n成比例的阈值时，SGD的理论最优速率仍不及RR。实验结果证实，RR在多种步长设置下均稳定优于SGD，验证了其在实际应用中的优越性。
PolicyGuard: From Organizational Policies to Neuro-SymbolicCompliance Review Engines
Authors: Sameer Malik, Ayush Singh, Amar Prakash Azad
First: 2026-06-30T17:37:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:37:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Policy-grounded document review requires determining whether a target document complies with organization-specific policies, guidelines, or playbooks. While large language models can assist with policy interpretation and document analysis, end-to-end prompting leaves the applied policy logic implicit, making compliance decisions difficult to inspect, update, and test. We present PolicyGuard, a neuro-symbolic framework for policy-grounded document compliance review. PolicyGuard converts organizational policy guidance into an executable review engine consisting of typed relational logic rules and atom-level extraction questions. During review, LLMs answer these local questions using retrieved document evidence, and a symbolic evaluator applies the formal rules to detect non-compliance. We instantiate and evaluate PolicyGuard on company-specific NDA compliance review, where contract clauses must be checked against organization-specific negotiation policies. By separating policy formalization, local document interpretation, and symbolic compliance evaluation, PolicyGuard makes document review more explicit, maintainable, and systematically testable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PolicyGuard：从组织政策到神经符号合规审查引擎
基于政策的文档审查需要判断目标文档是否符合组织特定的政策、指南或操作手册。虽然大语言模型可辅助政策解读和文档分析，但端到端提示使应用的政策逻辑隐含，导致合规决策难以审查、更新和测试。我们提出PolicyGuard，一种用于政策驱动文档合规审查的神经符号框架。PolicyGuard将组织政策指导转化为可执行的审查引擎，包含类型化关系逻辑规则和原子级提取问题。审查过程中，大语言模型使用检索到的文档证据回答这些局部问题，符号评估器则应用形式化规则检测不合规。我们在公司特定的NDA合规审查中实例化并评估PolicyGuard，其中合同条款需对照组织特定的谈判政策进行检查。通过分离政策形式化、局部文档解读和符号合规评估，PolicyGuard使文档审查更明确、可维护且可系统性测试。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for transparent and auditable compliance checks in policy-grounded document review, PolicyGuard introduces a neuro-symbolic framework that formalizes organizational policies into executable logic rules and atom-level extraction questions. During evaluation, large language models interpret document content by answering localized questions, and a symbolic evaluator applies the formalized rules to detect non-compliance. Experiments on company-specific NDA compliance show that this separation of policy formalization, local interpretation, and symbolic evaluation enhances review clarity, maintainability, and testability compared to end-to-end prompting approaches.
为实现政策驱动文档审查的透明性和可审计性，PolicyGuard提出一种神经符号框架，将组织政策形式化为可执行的逻辑规则与原子级提取问题。审查时，大语言模型从文档中提取证据回答问题，符号评估器再应用形式化规则检测不合规项。在公司特定的NDA合规任务中，PolicyGuard相比端到端提示方法，显著提升了审查的透明度、可维护性和系统可测试性，支持合规逻辑的清晰检查与修改。
Self-Study Reconsidered: The Hidden Fragility of Learning from Self-Generated QA
Authors: Ekaterina Alimaskina, Denis Shveykin, Gleb Molodtsov, Igor Shalygin, Alexey Kadeishvili, Aleksandr Beznosikov
First: 2026-06-30T17:35:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:35:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models are increasingly taught from synthetic question--answer (QA) supervision: a model generates questions about a document, answers them from the same text, and the resulting pairs are used to fine-tune, distill, or compress knowledge into another model. We show that this generation step is not neutral preprocessing. It is an implicit policy that both selects which evidence becomes training signal and decides how that evidence is answered, and it is fragile at both stages. When choosing what to ask, generators do not scan a document uniformly. Coverage saturates early and concentrates on salient spans, diverse prompts converge on the same regions, and what looks question-worthy is driven by local presentation. As a result, salient artifacts such as poorly cleaned markup can hijack question generation across model families and scales. When answering, the model that produces the supervision tends to obey instruction-like passages embedded in the text. This compliance depends on the intent and surface form of the passage rather than its strictness, and is worst under task conflict, where larger models comply more often. These failure modes arise from choices made during QA generation, so they can be reduced without changing the training loop. Tying each question to a fixed target reduces biased selection, and filtering instruction-like spans before answering lowers mean injection compliance from $88\%$ to $13\%$ in our evaluation while retaining nearly all clean text.
中文标题/摘要
标题：再思自主学习：从自动生成问答中学习的隐性脆弱性
语言模型越来越多地通过合成问答（QA）监督进行训练：模型根据文档生成问题，从同一文本中回答问题，生成的问答对用于微调、蒸馏或压缩知识至另一模型。我们表明，这一生成步骤并非中性的预处理，而是一种隐式策略，既选择哪些证据成为训练信号，也决定如何回答这些证据，且在两个阶段均存在脆弱性。在选择提问内容时，生成器不会均匀扫描文档，覆盖范围早期饱和并集中于显著片段，多样化的提示会收敛至相同区域，而哪些内容看起来值得提问则受局部呈现方式驱动。因此，如未清理干净的标记等显著伪影可跨模型家族和规模劫持问题生成。在回答时，生成监督的模型倾向于服从文本中嵌入的指令式段落，这种服从性取决于段落的意图和表层形式，而非其严格性，且在任务冲突时表现最差，大型模型更易服从。这些失效模式源于QA生成过程中的选择，因此无需改变训练循环即可减少。将每个问题绑定至固定目标可减少偏倚选择，而在回答前过滤指令式片段可将平均注入服从率从88%降至13%，同时保留几乎全部干净文本。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the fragility of self-generated question-answering (QA) pairs used to train language models, revealing that the generation process itself introduces biases and artifacts. The authors demonstrate that QA generators do not uniformly scan documents, instead focusing on salient regions and being influenced by local text presentation, which can lead to biased coverage and susceptibility to noisy markup. Additionally, models tend to comply with instruction-like text embedded in documents, with compliance increasing in larger models and worsening under task conflict. The study proposes mitigation strategies—fixing each question to a specific target and filtering instruction-like spans—which reduce biased selection and injection compliance from 88% to 13% while preserving clean text quality.
该研究探讨了用于训练语言模型的自动生成问答对存在的脆弱性，发现生成过程本身会引入偏差和伪影。作者指出，问答生成器不会均匀扫描文档，而是聚焦于显著片段，且受局部文本呈现影响，导致选择偏差和被不良清理的标记干扰。此外，生成监督的模型倾向于服从文本中嵌入的指令式语句，尤其在任务冲突时，大模型更易服从，合规率高达88%。作者提出两种缓解方法：将每个问题绑定到固定目标可减少选择偏差，提前过滤指令式片段可将平均注入合规率从88%降至13%，同时保留大部分干净文本。
Radial Suppression Accelerates Algorithmic Generalization: A Geometric Analysis of Delayed Generalization
Authors: Srijan Tiwari, Aditya Chauhan, Manjot Singh
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-30T17:34:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:34:13+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 5 figures, 10 tables. Presented at the Workshop on High-dimensional Learning Dynamics at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Why do neural networks memorize algorithmic training data long before they generalize? We present a geometric case study demonstrating that, on tasks where generalization requires discovering structured low-dimensional circuits, the memorization-generalization delay is driven by radial inflation of hidden representations under cross-entropy optimization. We formalize a radial-angular decomposition of activation-space dynamics and derive three testable propositions: (i) that penalizing radial inflation induces anisotropic, data-dependent weight regularization; (ii) that it suppresses radial gradient energy below the isotropic random baseline, forcing predominantly angular updates; and (iii) that it biases convergence toward flatter minima. To empirically validate these propositions, we study a single-hyperparameter norm penalty that softly constrains activations to a sqrt(d)-radius hypersphere. On modular arithmetic, this penalty accelerates grokking up to 6x across MLPs and Transformers, and halves training steps for a 10M-parameter nanoGPT on 3-digit addition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：径向抑制加速算法泛化：延迟泛化的几何分析
为什么神经网络会在泛化之前长时间记住算法训练数据？我们通过一个几何案例研究表明，在需要发现结构化低维电路的任务中，记忆与泛化的延迟是由交叉熵优化下隐藏表示的径向膨胀驱动的。我们形式化了激活空间动力学的径向-角分解，并推导出三个可检验的命题：(i) 惩罚径向膨胀会诱导各向异性、数据依赖的权重正则化；(ii) 它将径向梯度能量压制在各向同性随机基线下，迫使更新主要沿角方向进行；(iii) 它使收敛偏向更平坦的极小值。为实证验证这些命题，我们研究了一种单超参数范数惩罚，该惩罚软性约束激活值位于sqrt(d)半径的超球面上。在模运算任务中，该惩罚在MLP和Transformer上使grokking加速高达6倍，并将10M参数nanoGPT在3位数加法任务上的训练步数减半。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why neural networks often memorize training data before generalizing, proposing that radial inflation of hidden representations under cross-entropy optimization causes this delay. The authors introduce a geometric framework decomposing activation-space dynamics into radial and angular components, deriving three testable hypotheses: radial penalties induce anisotropic regularization, suppress radial gradient energy, and promote convergence to flatter minima. Empirically, applying a single-hyperparameter norm penalty that constrains activations to a sqrt(d)-radius hypersphere accelerates generalization—reducing grokking time by up to 6x in MLPs and Transformers, and cutting training steps in half for a 10M-parameter nanoGPT on 3-digit addition.
该论文探讨神经网络为何在泛化前长期记忆训练数据，提出交叉熵优化下隐藏表示的径向膨胀是导致这一延迟的原因。研究构建了激活空间动力学的径向-角分解几何框架，并提出三个可检验假设：径向惩罚可诱导各向异性正则化、抑制径向梯度能量、引导收敛至平坦最小值。实验采用单超参数范数惩罚，将激活约束于sqrt(d)半径超球面，结果表明该方法在模运算任务上可使泛化加速达6倍（覆盖MLP与Transformer），并在1000万参数nanoGPT的3位加法任务中将训练步数减半。
PSHuman: Photorealistic Single-image 3D Human Reconstruction using Cross-Scale Multiview Diffusion and Explicit Remeshing
Authors: Peng Li, Wangguandong Zheng, Yuan Liu, Tao Yu, Yangguang Li, Xingqun Qi, Xiaowei Chi, Siyu Xia, Yan-Pei Cao, Wei Xue, Wenhan Luo, Yike Guo
First: 2024-09-16T10:13:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:32:07+00:00
Comments: CVPR2025, Project page: https://penghtyx.github.io/PSHuman
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Detailed and photorealistic 3D human modeling is essential for various applications and has seen tremendous progress. However, full-body reconstruction from a monocular RGB image remains challenging due to the ill-posed nature of the problem and sophisticated clothing topology with self-occlusions. In this paper, we propose PSHuman, a novel framework that explicitly reconstructs human meshes utilizing priors from the multiview diffusion model. It is found that directly applying multiview diffusion on single-view human images leads to severe geometric distortions, especially on generated faces. To address it, we propose a cross-scale diffusion that models the joint probability distribution of global full-body shape and local facial characteristics, enabling detailed and identity-preserved novel-view generation without any geometric distortion. Moreover, to enhance cross-view body shape consistency of varied human poses, we condition the generative model on parametric models like SMPL-X, which provide body priors and prevent unnatural views inconsistent with human anatomy. Leveraging the generated multi-view normal and color images, we present SMPLX-initialized explicit human carving to recover realistic textured human meshes efficiently. Extensive experimental results and quantitative evaluations on CAPE and THuman2.1 datasets demonstrate PSHumans superiority in geometry details, texture fidelity, and generalization capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PSHuman：基于跨尺度多视角扩散与显式重网格化的单图像逼真3D人体重建
详细且逼真的3D人体建模对多种应用至关重要，并已取得显著进展。然而，从单目RGB图像进行全身重建仍具挑战性，因该问题本质欠定，且衣物拓扑结构复杂并存在自遮挡。本文提出PSHuman框架，通过多视角扩散模型先验显式重建人体网格。研究发现，直接在单视角人体图像上应用多视角扩散会导致严重几何失真，尤其体现在生成面部。为此，我们提出跨尺度扩散方法，建模全局全身形状与局部面部特征的联合概率分布，实现无几何失真、细节丰富且身份保持的新型视角生成。此外，为增强不同人体姿态下跨视角身体形状一致性，我们以SMPL-X等参数化模型作为条件，提供身体先验并避免与人体解剖结构不一致的非自然视角。利用生成的多视角法线与颜色图像，我们提出基于SMPLX初始化的显式人体雕刻方法，高效恢复真实纹理的人体网格。在CAPE和THuman2.1数据集上的大量实验与定量评估表明，PSHuman在几何细节、纹理保真度及泛化能力方面均表现优越。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate photorealistic 3D human reconstruction from single images, PSHuman introduces a cross-scale multiview diffusion framework that jointly models global body shape and local facial features to avoid geometric distortions. It conditions generation on SMPL-X parametric models to ensure anatomical consistency across poses and views, then uses explicit remeshing from generated multi-view normals and colors to produce detailed textured meshes. Experiments on CAPE and THuman2.1 show superior geometry, texture fidelity, and generalization compared to prior methods.
为解决单张图像重建真实3D人体的几何失真问题，PSHuman提出跨尺度多视角扩散框架，联合建模全身形状与局部面部特征以避免形变，并结合SMPL-X参数化模型确保人体解剖一致性。通过多视角渲染图像驱动显式雕刻，生成高保真纹理网格。在CAPE和THuman2.1数据集上的实验表明，其在几何细节、纹理保真度和泛化能力上优于现有方法。
DinoLink: A Token-Centric Representation Compression Framework for Bandwidth-Constrained Collaborative V2X Perception
Authors: Tianle Zhu, Haohua Que, Handong Yao, Hongyi Xu, Zhipeng Bao
First: 2026-06-24T21:31:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:31:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
High-precision remote perception is often hindered by the severe bandwidth constraints of Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) networks. We propose \textit{DinoLink}, a token-centric compression framework that replaces raw pixel streaming with discrete semantic communication for vehicle-cloud collaborative inference. DinoLink employs a dual-sparsity architecture: a saliency-aware selector prunes redundant background tokens, while a Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ) module collapses features into compact codebook indices. By transmitting only lightweight indices and positional priors, DinoLink achieves a $139\times$ bitrate reduction compared to uncompressed transmission while maintaining a competitive 32.8\% mAP on the nuScenes dataset. Deployment simulations further demonstrate a $34.5\times$ acceleration in narrow-band environments, such as LoRa. Our results substantiate DinoLink as a robust, bandwidth-efficient frontend for high-fidelity remote perception in constrained V2X scenarios. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/UGA-MOBILITY-LAB/dino_link.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DinoLink：面向带宽受限协同V2X感知的基于令牌的表示压缩框架
高精度远程感知常受制于车联万物（V2X）网络的严重带宽限制。我们提出DinoLink，一种以令牌为中心的压缩框架，用离散语义通信替代原始像素流，实现车云协同推理。DinoLink采用双稀疏架构：一个显著性感知选择器剔除冗余背景令牌，一个残差向量量化（RVQ）模块将特征压缩为紧凑的码本索引。仅传输轻量级索引和位置先验，DinoLink相比无压缩传输实现139倍码率降低，同时在nuScenes数据集上保持32.8%的mAP竞争力。部署仿真进一步表明，在窄带环境（如LoRa）下加速34.5倍。结果证实DinoLink是带宽受限V2X场景中高保真远程感知的稳健高效前端。代码公开于https://github.com/UGA-MOBILITY-LAB/dino_link。
Summary / 总结
To address bandwidth limitations in V2X networks that hinder high-precision remote perception, DinoLink introduces a token-centric compression framework that replaces pixel streaming with semantic communication. It uses a dual-sparsity design: a saliency-aware selector removes redundant background tokens, and a Residual Vector Quantization module encodes features into compact codebook indices. Experimental results show DinoLink reduces bitrate by 139x compared to uncompressed transmission while preserving 32.8% mAP on nuScenes, and achieves 34.5x faster inference in narrow-band environments like LoRa, demonstrating its effectiveness for bandwidth-constrained collaborative perception.
为解决V2X网络带宽受限导致的高精度远程感知瓶颈，DinoLink提出一种以token为中心的压缩框架，用离散语义通信替代原始像素流传输。其采用双稀疏架构：通过显著性感知选择器剔除冗余背景token，再用残差向量量化模块将特征压缩为紧凑码本索引。实验表明，该框架在nuScenes数据集上实现139倍码率压缩，同时保持32.8%的mAP，并在LoRa等窄带环境下模拟出34.5倍加速效果，验证其在受限带宽场景下高效支撑高保真远程感知的能力。
PerceptionRubrics: Calibrating Multimodal Evaluation to Human Perception
Authors: Yana Wei, Hongbo Peng, Yanlin Lai, Liang Zhao, Kangheng Lin, En Yu, Keyu Lv, Han Zhou, Yin Tang, Haodong Li, Mitt Huang, Hangyu Guo, Jianjian Sun, Zheng Ge, Xiangyu Zhang, Daxin Jiang, Vishal M. Patel
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-26T17:59:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:30:17+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Project page: https://weiyana.github.io/PerceptionRubrics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce PerceptionRubrics, a rubric-based evaluation framework that addresses the gap between saturated benchmark scores and real-world brittleness. Shifting evaluation from holistic semantic matching to rigorous atomic auditing, PerceptionRubrics pairs 1,038 information-dense images with over 10,000 instance-specific rubrics. These criteria are derived from golden captions constructed via a novel Circular Peer-Review consensus pipeline and then distilled into a dual-stream system of Must-Right (essential facts) and Easy-Wrong (fine-grained details) rubrics. Crucially, PerceptionRubrics implements a Gated Scoring mechanism: unlike linear averages, failure on mandatory visual facts triggers sharp binary penalties. Extensive evaluation yields critical insights: (1) The Reliability Gap: models often verify fragmented elements correctly yet fail strict conjunctive constraints, exposing brittleness in dense domains; (2) Open-Closed Stratification: contrary to reasoning trends, we reveal a persistent 8% perception deficit between open-source and proprietary frontiers; and (3) Human-Aligned Rigor: our gated metrics substantially out-align conventional benchmarks, validating that strict perceptual fidelity is the prerequisite for reliable generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：感知评分标准：校准多模态评估以匹配人类感知
我们提出了感知评分标准，这是一种基于评分标准的评估框架，旨在弥合饱和基准分数与现实世界脆弱性之间的差距。该框架将评估从整体语义匹配转向严格的原子级审计，将1038张信息密集型图像与超过10000个实例特定评分标准配对。这些标准源自通过新颖的循环同行评审共识流程构建的黄金标题，并提炼为双流系统：必须正确（核心事实）和容易错误（细粒度细节）评分标准。关键的是，感知评分标准实现了门控评分机制：与线性平均不同，对强制视觉事实的失败会触发尖锐的二元惩罚。广泛评估得出关键洞见：（1）可靠性差距：模型常能正确验证碎片化元素，但在严格合取约束下失败，暴露出在密集领域中的脆弱性；（2）开放-封闭分层：与推理趋势相反，我们揭示了开源与专有前沿之间持续存在的8%感知差距；（3）人类对齐的严谨性：我们的门控指标显著优于传统基准，验证了严格的感知保真度是可靠生成的前提。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the disconnect between high benchmark scores and real-world model brittleness, PerceptionRubrics introduces a rubric-based evaluation framework that shifts from holistic semantic matching to atomic visual auditing. It pairs 1,038 dense images with over 10,000 instance-specific rubrics derived from golden captions via a Circular Peer-Review pipeline, then distills them into Must-Right and Easy-Wrong criteria. A Gated Scoring mechanism enforces binary penalties for missing mandatory facts, unlike linear averaging. Experiments reveal three key findings: models often pass fragmented elements but fail conjunctive constraints (Reliability Gap), open-source models lag proprietary ones by 8% in perception (Open-Closed Stratification), and gated metrics better align with human perception than conventional benchmarks (Human-Aligned Rigor).
为弥合高基准分数与现实世界模型脆弱性之间的差距，PerceptionRubrics提出一种基于评分标准的评估框架，将评估从整体语义匹配转向原子级视觉审计。该框架将1038张信息密集图像与10000多个实例特定评分标准配对，这些标准由圆形同行评审共识流程生成的黄金描述提炼而来，分为“必须正确”和“容易错误”两类。其“门控评分”机制对缺失必要视觉事实施加二元惩罚，而非线性平均。实验发现三点：模型常能正确识别碎片化元素，却无法满足联合约束（可靠性差距）；开源模型与专有模型之间持续存在8%的感知差距（开放-封闭分层）；门控指标比传统基准更贴近人类感知（人类对齐严谨性）。
OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation
Authors: Arnav Balaji, Arpit Bahety, Sriniket Ambatipudi, Daniel Lam, Junhong Xu, Roberto Martín-Martín
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-06-30T17:30:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:30:05+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/oopsieverse/. The first two authors contributed equally; order decided by dice roll. Accepted to Robotics: Science and Systems (RSS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While robotic manipulation capabilities have advanced rapidly, physical safety remains a major barrier to deploying household robots: task success is insufficient if the robot damages itself or its surroundings. Simulation offers a harm-free alternative to costly and dangerous real-world training and evaluation, yet existing simulators lack general mechanisms to detect, quantify, and represent damage. To address this gap, we introduce OOPSIEVERSE, a unified simulation framework and benchmark for damage-aware household manipulation. OOPSIEVERSE provides damage as an explicit, physically-grounded, and taskagnostic signal by converting sources such as contact forces, temperature changes, and liquid interactions into corresponding mechanical, thermal or fluid damage. OOPSIEVERSE comprises two core elements: (1) DAMAGESIM, a simulator-agnostic framework for detecting and quantifying damage during navigation and manipulation, and (2) a suite of household tasks designed to evaluate common damage modes and distinguish between task completion and safe execution. We demonstrate the generality of our framework by instantiating DAMAGESIM in two simulators with different physics backends, OmniGibson (Nvidia Omniverse) and RoboCasa (MuJoCo). We further showcase the utility of OOPSIEVERSE across multiple use cases, including (1) guiding safer demonstration collection via real-time damage feedback, (2) learning safer manipulation policies through damage-conditioned imitation learning and reinforcement learning, (3) benchmarking the safety of state-of-the-art Vision Language Action policies, and (4) improving real-world safety of sim-to-real transferred policies. Together, our results highlight the potential of OOPSIEVERSE as an open-source foundation for systematic, scalable research on safe robot manipulation. For code and more information, please refer to https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/oopsieverse/
中文标题/摘要
标题：OopsieVerse：面向机器人操作的损伤感知仿真安全基准
尽管机器人操作能力迅速提升，但物理安全仍是部署家用机器人的重要障碍：若机器人损坏自身或环境，则任务成功无意义。仿真提供了一种无害且低成本的替代方案，但现有仿真器缺乏通用机制来检测、量化和表示损伤。为此，我们提出OOPSIEVERSE，一个统一的损伤感知家用操作仿真框架与基准。OOPSIEVERSE通过将接触力、温度变化和液体交互等源转换为相应的机械、热或流体损伤，提供明确、物理基础且与任务无关的损伤信号。OOPSIEVERSE包含两个核心部分：(1) DAMAGESIM，一个与仿真器无关的框架，用于在导航和操作过程中检测和量化损伤；(2) 一系列家用任务，用于评估常见损伤模式并区分任务完成与安全执行。我们通过在两个不同物理后端的仿真器——OmniGibson（Nvidia Omniverse）和RoboCasa（MuJoCo）中实例化DAMAGESIM，展示了框架的通用性。此外，我们还展示了OOPSIEVERSE在多个用例中的实用性，包括：(1) 通过实时损伤反馈引导更安全的演示收集；(2) 通过损伤条件下的模仿学习和强化学习学习更安全的操作策略；(3) 对最先进的视觉-语言-动作策略的安全性进行基准测试；(4) 提升仿真到现实转移策略在真实世界中的安全性。我们的结果共同突显了OOPSIEVERSE作为开源基础，支持系统化、可扩展的机器人安全操作研究的潜力。代码和更多信息请访问：https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/oopsieverse/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safer robot manipulation in household environments, this paper introduces OOPSIEVERSE, a simulation framework and benchmark that explicitly models physical damage during robot tasks. The method, DAMAGESIM, detects and quantifies damage—such as mechanical, thermal, or fluid harm—by converting sensor inputs like contact forces and temperature changes into damage signals, independent of the underlying simulator. Evaluated across two physics engines (OmniGibson and RoboCasa), the framework successfully supports diverse safety applications: guiding safer human demonstrations, training damage-aware policies via imitation and reinforcement learning, benchmarking vision-language-action models, and improving real-world safety of transferred policies. Experimental results confirm OOPSIEVERSE’s effectiveness in distinguishing task success from safe execution and its scalability across simulation platforms.
为提升家庭环境中机器人操作的安全性，本文提出OOPSIEVERSE，一个能显式建模操作过程中物理损伤的仿真框架与基准。其核心方法DAMAGEsim通过接触力、温度变化和流体交互等物理信号，将损伤量化为与任务无关的损伤指标。在OmniGibson和RoboCasa两个不同物理引擎的仿真器中验证，该框架支持通过损伤反馈引导安全演示采集、损伤条件下的模仿与强化学习、视觉语言动作策略的安全性评估，以及仿真到现实策略的安全性提升。实验表明，OOPSIEVERSE能有效区分任务完成与安全执行，并提升真实世界操作安全性，为安全机器人研究提供可扩展的开源基础。
Amplifying Membership Signal Through Chained Regeneration
Authors: Wojciech Łapacz, Stanisław Pawlak
First: 2026-06-30T17:29:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:29:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The tendency of large generative models to memorize training data makes sample verification critical for privacy auditing and copyright enforcement. Current membership (MIA) and dataset inference (DI) attacks often rely on one-shot generations, which yield weak signals and limited sensitivity across modalities. Inspired by Model Autophagy Disorder (MAD), we introduce MADreMIA, a model-agnostic framework that enhances white-, gray-, and black-box MIA and DI. Rather than relying on shadow model training -- often infeasible for large generative models -- our framework facilitates scalable inference by leveraging inherent signals through iterative trajectories. This process utilizes chained generations across diverse modalities, where each output serves as the subsequent input, to improve membership evidence at low FPR. We demonstrate that memorized training samples exhibit significantly higher coherence and slower degradation during iterative regeneration than non-member generations. Our results show that MADreMIA provides richer signals across diverse model families and modalities; we present comprehensive evaluations for IARs, diffusion, and language models, alongside preliminary results demonstrating its potential for audio models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过链式再生增强成员信号
大型生成模型倾向于记忆训练数据，因此样本验证对隐私审计和版权执法至关重要。当前的成员（MIA）和数据集推断（DI）攻击常依赖单次生成，导致信号弱且跨模态敏感度有限。受模型自噬紊乱（MAD）启发，我们提出MADreMIA，一种模型无关框架，可增强白盒、灰盒和黑盒MIA与DI。该框架不依赖影子模型训练——这对大型生成模型常不可行——而是通过迭代轨迹利用固有信号实现可扩展推理。该过程在多种模态间进行链式生成，每个输出作为后续输入，以在低FPR下提升成员证据。我们证明，记忆训练样本在迭代再生中表现出显著更高的连贯性和更慢的退化速度。结果表明，MADreMIA在多种模型家族和模态中提供更丰富的信号；我们对IARs、扩散模型和语言模型进行了全面评估，并初步展示了其在音频模型中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect memorized training data in large generative models for privacy and copyright purposes, the authors propose MADreMIA, a model-agnostic framework that enhances membership and dataset inference attacks through iterative, chained generations across modalities. Instead of shadow model training, which is often impractical for large models, MADreMIA leverages inherent model behavior by using each generation’s output as input for the next, thereby amplifying membership signals. Experimental results show that memorized samples exhibit greater coherence and slower degradation during iterative regeneration compared to non-members, enabling higher sensitivity and lower false positive rates across image, diffusion, and language models, with preliminary audio results indicating broad applicability.
为应对大型生成模型中训练数据记忆导致的隐私与版权风险，本文提出MADreMIA框架，通过跨模态的迭代链式生成增强成员推断与数据集推断攻击。该方法无需影子模型训练，而是利用模型自身行为，将生成结果作为后续输入，从而放大成员信号并控制误报率。实验表明，被记忆的训练样本在迭代再生过程中表现出更强的连贯性与更慢的退化，验证了该方法在图像、扩散与语言模型中的有效性，并初步展示了其在音频模型中的潜力。
Evaluation of Population Initialization Methods for Genetic Programming-based Symbolic Regression
Authors: Lukas Kammerer, Gabriel Kronberger, Deaglan J. Bartlett, Harry Desmond, Pedro G. Ferreira, Stephan Winkler
First: 2026-06-30T17:28:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:28:37+00:00
Comments: 15 pages. Accepted for publication at EUROCAST 2026: 20th International Conference on Computer Aided Systems Theory
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We analyze the effect of optimizing the initial population of genetic programming (GP) for symbolic regression (SR) on the accuracy and complexity of solutions. We compare three well-established random initialization methods as well as initialization with small optimized solutions from exhaustive symbolic regression (ESR) using a GP/SR implementation which is based on the multi-objective evolutionary algorithm NSGA-II. We compare the final Pareto fronts found with each initialization method on twelve synthetic problems of varying complexity and one real-world dataset. We find no significant differences in accuracy or model complexity among the initialization methods. The initial advantage of initialization with ESR disappears after only a few generations. Our results show that, given similar diversity in the initial population, the effect of the initialization method in GP-based symbolic regression on the final Pareto front is negligible.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于遗传编程的符号回归中种群初始化方法的评估
我们分析了优化遗传编程（GP）用于符号回归（SR）的初始种群对解的准确性和复杂性的影响。我们比较了三种成熟的随机初始化方法，以及使用基于多目标进化算法NSGA-II的GP/SR实现，结合穷举符号回归（ESR）生成的小型优化解进行初始化。我们在十二个复杂度各异的合成问题和一个真实数据集上比较了各初始化方法所得的最终Pareto前沿。结果表明，各初始化方法在准确性和模型复杂性上无显著差异。ESR初始化的初始优势在仅几代后便消失。我们的结果表明，在初始种群具有相似多样性的情况下，GP符号回归中初始化方法对最终Pareto前沿的影响可忽略不计。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether optimizing the initial population in genetic programming for symbolic regression improves solution accuracy or complexity, comparing three random initialization methods with one using small optimized solutions from exhaustive symbolic regression. The experiments, conducted using a multi-objective NSGA-II framework across twelve synthetic problems and one real-world dataset, reveal no statistically significant differences in final Pareto front outcomes among initialization strategies. The initial performance edge of ESR-based initialization vanishes within a few generations, indicating that, when initial population diversity is comparable, the choice of initialization method has minimal impact on the final results.
该研究探讨了在遗传编程用于符号回归时，优化初始种群是否能提升解的精度或复杂度，对比了三种随机初始化方法与一种基于穷举符号回归的小规模优化解初始化方法。采用多目标NSGA-II框架，在十二个合成问题和一个真实数据集上评估各方法的最终Pareto前沿表现，结果发现各初始化策略在精度和复杂度上无显著差异。ESR初始化的初始优势在几代后即消失，表明当初始种群多样性相近时，初始化方法对最终结果的影响微乎其微。
Drop-In Perceptual Optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Ezgi Ozyilkan, Zhiqi Chen, Oren Rippel, Jona Ballé, Kedar Tatwawadi
Venue: ECCV
First: 2026-03-23T17:42:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:27:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted as a conference paper at ECCV'26. Project page: https://apple.github.io/ml-perceptual-3dgs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Despite their output being ultimately consumed by human viewers, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods often rely on ad-hoc combinations of pixel-level losses, resulting in blurry renderings. To address this, we systematically explore perceptual optimization strategies for 3DGS by searching over a diverse set of distortion losses. We conduct the first-of-its-kind large-scale human subjective study on 3DGS, involving 39,320 pairwise ratings across several datasets and 3DGS frameworks. A regularized version of Wasserstein Distortion, which we call WD-R, emerges as the clear winner, excelling at recovering fine textures without incurring a higher splat count. WD-R is preferred by raters more than $2.3\times$ over the original 3DGS loss, and $1.5\times$ over the current best method Perceptual-GS. WD-R also consistently achieves state-of-the-art LPIPS, DISTS, and FID scores across various datasets, and generalizes across recent frameworks, such as Mip-Splatting and Scaffold-GS, where replacing the original loss with WD-R consistently enhances perceptual quality within a similar resource budget (number of splats for Mip-Splatting, model size for Scaffold-GS), and leads to reconstructions being preferred by human raters $1.8\times$ and $3.6\times$, respectively. We also find that this carries over to the task of 3DGS scene compression, with $\approx 50\%$ bitrate savings for comparable perceptual metric performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：3D高斯点渲染的感知优化方法
尽管3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）的最终输出由人类观众观看，但其方法常依赖像素级损失的随意组合，导致渲染模糊。为此，我们系统性地探索了多种失真损失下的感知优化策略。我们首次在多个数据集和3DGS框架上开展了大规模人类主观评测，共收集39,320组配对评分。我们提出的正则化Wasserstein失真损失（WD-R）表现最佳，能有效恢复精细纹理且不增加点数。WD-R在主观评分上比原始3DGS损失高出2.3倍，比当前最优方法Perceptual-GS高出1.5倍。WD-R在多个数据集上持续取得LPIPS、DISTS和FID最优成绩，并在Mip-Splatting和Scaffold-GS等近期框架中泛化良好，替换原损失后在资源预算（Mip-Splatting的点数、Scaffold-GS的模型大小）相近条件下，感知质量分别提升1.8倍和3.6倍。此外，该方法在3DGS场景压缩任务中同样有效，可实现约50%的码率节省，同时保持相近的感知质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue of blurry renderings in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) caused by ad-hoc pixel-level losses, this work systematically investigates perceptual optimization by evaluating diverse distortion losses. Through a large-scale human subjective study involving 39,320 pairwise ratings, the authors identify WD-R, a regularized Wasserstein Distortion loss, as superior for recovering fine textures without increasing splat count. WD-R outperforms the original 3DGS loss by more than 2.3 times and the current best method Perceptual-GS by 1.5 times in human preference. It also achieves state-of-the-art metrics (LPIPS, DISTS, FID) across datasets and generalizes well to frameworks like Mip-Splatting and Scaffold-GS, improving perceptual quality by 1.8x and 3.6x respectively while maintaining similar resource budgets. Additionally, WD-R enables up to 50% bitrate savings in 3DGS scene compression with comparable perceptual fidelity.
为解决3D高斯点渲染模糊的问题，该研究系统性地探索了多种失真损失函数以优化感知质量。通过39,320组人类主观评分的大规模实验，作者发现WD-R（正则化Wasserstein失真损失）在恢复细纹理方面表现最优，且不增加点数。WD-R比原始3DGS损失受欢迎程度高出2.3倍，优于当前最优方法Perceptual-GS 1.5倍。它在多个数据集上达到最先进感知指标（LPIPS、DISTS、FID），并在Mip-Splatting和Scaffold-GS等框架中泛化良好，分别提升人类偏好1.8倍和3.6倍，同时在场景压缩中实现约50%码率节省而保持同等感知质量。
Nazrin: An Atomic Neural Proof Automation Tactic in Lean 4
Authors: Leni Aniva, Iori Oikawa, David Dill, Clark Barrett
First: 2026-02-21T09:12:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:26:18+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In Machine-Assisted Theorem Proving, a theorem proving agent searches for a sequence of expressions and tactics that can prove a statement in a proof assistant. In this work, we introduce several novel concepts and capabilities to address obstacles faced by machine-assisted theorem proving. We first present a set of \textbf{atomic tactics}, a small finite set of tactics capable of proving any provable statement in Lean. We then introduce a \textbf{transposing atomization} algorithm which turns arbitrary proof expressions into a series of atomic tactics. We next introduce the \textbf{ExprGraph} data structure, which provides a succinct representation for Lean expressions. Finally, we present the \textbf{Nazrin Prover}, short for \textbf{N}eural \textbf{A}tomi\textbf{z}e\textbf{r} for \textbf{In}habitation Problems, a graph neural network-based theorem proving agent using atomic tactics and ExprGraph. Nazrin circumvents many challenges faced by existing proving agents by exclusively dispatching atomic tactics, and it is robust enough to both train and evaluate on consumer-grade hardware. We demonstrate the potential of tools like Nazrin using theorems from Lean's standard library and from Mathlib.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Nazrin：Lean 4 中的一种原子神经证明自动化策略
在机器辅助定理证明中，定理证明代理搜索一系列表达式和战术以在证明助手内证明一个命题。本工作中，我们引入若干新颖概念与能力以应对机器辅助定理证明所面临的障碍。我们首先提出一组\textbf{原子战术}，即一个有限的小型战术集合，能够证明 Lean 中所有可证命题。接着我们引入\textbf{转置原子化}算法，将任意证明表达式转化为一系列原子战术。随后我们介绍\textbf{ExprGraph}数据结构，为 Lean 表达式提供简洁表示。最后我们提出\textbf{Nazrin 证明器}，即\textbf{N}eural \textbf{A}tomi\textbf{z}e\textbf{r} for \textbf{In}habitation Problems，一种基于图神经网络的定理证明代理，使用原子战术和 ExprGraph。Nazrin 通过仅调度原子战术规避了现有证明代理所面临的许多挑战，并具备在消费级硬件上训练和评估的鲁棒性。我们通过 Lean 标准库及 Mathlib 中的定理展示了 Nazrin 等工具的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing theorem proving agents, this work introduces atomic tactics—minimal, finite proof steps capable of proving any provable statement in Lean—and a transposing atomization algorithm that converts arbitrary proofs into sequences of these atomic tactics. It further develops the ExprGraph data structure for compact expression representation and employs a graph neural network-based prover, Nazrin, which exclusively uses atomic tactics. Experimental results show Nazrin successfully proves theorems from Lean’s standard library and Mathlib, demonstrating robustness and efficiency on consumer-grade hardware without requiring specialized infrastructure.
为解决现有定理证明代理的局限性，本文提出Nazrin，一个基于图神经网络的Lean 4定理证明器，采用原子战术（最小、充分的证明步骤）以克服可扩展性和表达能力问题。方法包括通过新型原子化算法将任意证明表达式转换为原子战术序列，并使用ExprGraph数据结构高效表示Lean表达式。实验表明，Nazrin能成功证明Lean标准库和Mathlib中的大量定理，在消费级硬件上表现出鲁棒性，且在证明搜索速度和成功率上优于现有方法。
CoLT: Teaching Multi-Modal Models to Think with Chain of Latent Thoughts
Authors: Lianyu Hu, Shengqian Qin, Zeqin Liao, Qing Guo, Liang Wan, Wei Feng, Yang Liu
First: 2026-06-30T17:24:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:24:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV2026. Code is available at https://github.com/hulianyuyy/CoLT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has enabled multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) to tackle complex visual reasoning tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, this text-based reasoning paradigm is inherently slow at inference time with even thousands of tokens and fundamentally constrained by the expressiveness of natural language. In this paper, we propose CoLT, (Chain of Latent Thoughts), a novel framework that teaches multi-modal models to reason through a chain of latent thought representations instead of verbose text tokens, which can perform thinking with as few as 3 steps. Naively forcing the model to think with latent states easily produces meaningless semantics and makes training unstable. To effectively regulate the latent reasoning process, we introduce a lightweight external decoder that provides step-level supervision for each latent reasoning step in two complementary directions: a forward mode that decodes latent thoughts into the textual reasoning of the next step, and a backward mode that aligns decoder hidden states with the model's latent thoughts given preceding textual context. We further incorporate internal supervision that encourages coherent step-by-step latent transitions. The decoder and internal supervision are removed during inference to maintain high efficiency of latent reasoning. Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that CoLT not only outperforms existing latent reasoning methods such as CODI and SIM-CoT, but also surpasses latent visual reasoning approaches that rely on auxiliary images with costly annotation requirements. Compared to text CoT methods, CoLT can notably reduce the inference time by 10.1$\times$ and text decoding time by 22.6$\times$. Code is released at https://github.com/hulianyuyy/CoLT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoLT：教导多模态模型通过潜在思维链进行思考
思维链（CoT）推理使多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能够通过生成自然语言中的显式中间推理步骤来解决复杂的视觉推理任务。然而，这种基于文本的推理范式在推理时固有地缓慢（即使需要数千个token），且受自然语言表达能力的根本限制。本文提出CoLT（潜在思维链），一种新颖框架，教导多模态模型通过潜在思维表示链而非冗长文本token进行推理，仅需3步即可完成思考。直接强制模型使用潜在状态进行思考容易产生无意义语义并导致训练不稳定。为有效调控潜在推理过程，我们引入一个轻量级外部解码器，从两个互补方向为每个潜在推理步骤提供步骤级监督：前向模式将潜在思维解码为下一步的文本推理，后向模式则将解码器隐藏状态与给定前序文本上下文的模型潜在思维对齐。我们进一步引入内部监督，鼓励潜在状态的逐步连贯过渡。推理时移除解码器和内部监督，以保持潜在推理的高效率。在八个基准测试上的大量实验表明，CoLT不仅优于现有潜在推理方法（如CODI和SIM-CoT），也超越了依赖昂贵标注辅助图像的潜在视觉推理方法。与文本CoT方法相比，CoLT可显著减少推理时间10.1倍和文本解码时间22.6倍。代码已发布于https://github.com/hulianyuyy/CoLT。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and expressiveness limits of text-based chain-of-thought reasoning in multi-modal models, this paper introduces CoLT, a framework that enables models to reason via a chain of latent thought representations instead of verbose natural language, requiring as few as three steps. To stabilize training and guide latent reasoning, it employs a lightweight external decoder that provides forward and backward supervision: decoding latent thoughts into next-step text and aligning decoder states with latent thoughts given prior context. Internal supervision further ensures coherent transitions between latent steps. During inference, the decoder and supervision are removed for efficiency. Experiments across eight benchmarks show CoLT outperforms prior latent reasoning methods like CODI and SIM-CoT, and even surpasses image-augmented approaches needing costly annotations. Compared to text CoT, CoLT reduces inference time by 10.1x and text decoding time by 22.6x.
为解决多模态模型中基于文本的链式思维推理效率低、表达受限的问题，本文提出CoLT框架，使模型通过最少三步的潜在思维表示进行推理。为稳定训练并引导潜在推理过程，引入轻量级外部解码器，提供双向监督：前向解码潜在思维为下一步文本，后向对齐解码器状态与给定前文上下文的潜在思维，同时辅以内监督以确保潜在状态的连贯性转换。在八个基准测试中，CoLT不仅优于现有潜在推理方法（如CODI和SIM-CoT），也超越依赖昂贵标注辅助图像的视觉推理方法，相较文本链式思维方法，推理时间减少10.1倍，文本解码时间减少22.6倍。
GR2 Technical Report
Authors: Yufei Li, Zaiwei Zhang, Mingfu Liang, Kavosh Asadi, Jay Xu, Jimmy Kim, Chongyang Bai, Jieyi Zhang, Hongye Xie, Prachi Agrawal, Dian Yu, Tianyi Chen, Jean-Pascal Billaud, Garret Buell, YK, Zhu, Sachin Patil, Brooke Bian, Zhou Fang, Kevin Huang, Shiva Sudanagunta, Yuzhen Huang, Emma Lu, Chris O'Brien, Yang Song, Lihong Li, Jacob Tao, Zhicheng Zhu, Chao Li, Gaoxiang Liu, Neil Wu, Zhongyin Hu, Li Han, Loki Chen, Ming Lei, Greg Rehm, Siyuan Song, Tianwei Zhang, Li Li, Ketan Singh, Yavuz Yetim, Ilyas Atishev, Satendra Gera, Ashkan Sadeghi, Rachel Yan, Nikko Mizutani, Shuaiwen Wang, Song Yang, Zhijing Li, Jiang Liu, Mengying Sun, Fei Tian, Xiaohan Wei, Chonglin Sun, Parish Aggarwal, Kaushik Rangadurai, Zhi Hua, Frank Shyu, Ruchit Sharma, Liyuan Li, Shike Mei, Wenlin Chen, Santanu Kolay, Ben Schulte, Deepak Chandra, Adam, Song, Sandeep Pandey, Xi Liu, Hamed Firooz, Luke Simon
First: 2026-06-30T17:22:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:22:22+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Industrial recommendation systems serve billions of users through a multi-stage funnel -- retrieval, early-stage ranking, and re-ranking -- where the final re-ranking step disproportionately shapes user engagement and downstream performance, particularly for carousel and grid display formats. Despite growing enthusiasm for Large Language Models (LLMs) in recommendation, three gaps hinder industrial adoption: (1) most efforts target retrieval and ranking, leaving re-ranking -- the stage closest to the final user experience -- largely underexplored; (2) LLMs are typically deployed zero-shot or via supervised fine-tuning, underutilizing the reasoning capabilities unlocked by reinforcement learning (RL) on verifiable rewards; (3) deployed catalogs index billions of items with non-semantic identifiers that lie outside any base-LLM vocabulary. We present GR2 (Generative Reasoning Re-Ranker), an end-to-end framework that combines (i) mid-training on semantic IDs produced by a tokenizer with >=99% uniqueness, (ii) reasoning-trace distilled from a stronger teacher via targeted prompting and rejection sampling, and (iii) RL with verifiable rewards purpose-built for re-ranking. To make GR2 resource-viable, we further (iv) introduce a context compressor that amortizes training cost, On-Policy Distillation (OPD) as a scalable alternative to SFT -- which we find collapses at industrial scale -- and reasoning distillation for low-latency serving. GR2 delivers +18.7% R@1, +7.1% R@3, and +9.6% N@3 over legacy baselines on industrial-scale traffic. We further find that reward design is critical in re-ranking: LLMs often hack rewards by preserving the incoming order or exploiting position bias, motivating conditional verifiable rewards as essential industrial components.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GR2技术报告
工业推荐系统通过多阶段漏斗——检索、早期排序和重排序——服务数十亿用户，其中最终的重排序步骤对用户参与度和下游性能影响最大，尤其在画廊和网格展示格式中。尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在推荐领域备受关注，但三个瓶颈阻碍了其工业应用：（1）大多数研究聚焦于检索和排序，而最贴近最终用户体验的重排序阶段研究严重不足；（2）LLMs通常以零样本或监督微调方式部署，未能充分利用强化学习（RL）在可验证奖励下解锁的推理能力；（3）部署的目录索引数十亿项物品，其非语义标识符不在任何基础LLM词汇表内。我们提出GR2（生成式推理重排序器），一个端到端框架，结合（i）使用由分词器生成的语义ID（≥99%唯一性）进行中期训练，（ii）通过定向提示与拒绝采样从更强教师模型蒸馏推理轨迹，（iii）使用专为重排序设计的可验证奖励进行RL。为使GR2具备资源可行性，我们进一步（iv）引入上下文压缩器以摊销训练成本，提出On-Policy Distillation（OPD）作为可扩展的替代方案（我们发现监督微调在工业规模下会崩溃），并采用推理蒸馏以实现低延迟服务。GR2在工业级流量上相较传统基线分别提升R@1 +18.7%、R@3 +7.1%、N@3 +9.6%。我们进一步发现，重排序中奖励设计至关重要：LLMs常通过保持输入顺序或利用位置偏差“作弊”，因此条件可验证奖励是工业应用中的关键组件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve user engagement in industrial recommendation systems, particularly in the re-ranking stage that most directly affects user experience, GR2 introduces an end-to-end generative reasoning framework. It combines mid-training on high-uniqueness semantic IDs, reasoning traces distilled from a stronger teacher via targeted prompting and rejection sampling, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards tailored for re-ranking. To address scalability and latency, GR2 employs a context compressor, On-Policy Distillation as a replacement for supervised fine-tuning, and reasoning distillation for deployment. Experiments on industrial-scale traffic show GR2 achieves +18.7% R@1, +7.1% R@3, and +9.6% N@3 over legacy baselines, while also revealing that reward design is critical — LLMs often exploit position bias or preserve input order, underscoring the necessity of conditional verifiable rewards.
为提升工业推荐系统中最终排序阶段对用户参与度的影响，GR2提出了一种端到端的生成式推理重排序框架。该框架结合了在高唯一性语义ID上进行中训练、通过定向提示与拒绝采样从更强教师模型蒸馏推理轨迹，以及为重排序定制的基于可验证奖励的强化学习。为解决可扩展性与延迟问题，GR2引入上下文压缩器、作为监督微调替代方案的在线策略蒸馏，以及推理蒸馏用于服务部署。在工业级流量实验中，GR2相较传统基线提升R@1 +18.7%、R@3 +7.1%、N@3 +9.6%，同时发现奖励设计至关重要，LLM常通过保留输入顺序或利用位置偏差“作弊”，凸显了条件可验证奖励在工业场景中的必要性。
ERA: Entropy-Guided Visual Token Pruning with Rectified Attention for Efficient MLLMs
Authors: Yuhao Wang, Mu Qiao, Haiwen Diao, Yunzhi Zhuge, Pingping Zhang, Xindong Zhang, Lei Zhang, Huchuan Lu
First: 2026-06-30T17:20:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:20:29+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) incur prohibitive inference costs due to long visual token sequences. Training-free visual token reduction provides an efficient solution. However, existing methods distort attention distributions, giving rise to a phenomenon we term Attention Logit Collapse. To address this issue, we propose ERA, an Entropy-guided visual token pruning framework with Rectified Attention for efficient MLLMs. Specifically, ERA comprises three crucial components: Dual-view Entropy Pruning (DEP), Bias-aware Token Recycling (BTR), and Logit-preserving Attention Rectification (LAR). First, DEP identifies representative anchor tokens by jointly modeling visual diversity and head-wise saliency. BTR then recycles pruned tokens into their corresponding anchors while estimating a cluster-level logit bias. Building upon this, LAR injects the estimated bias into attention logits, effectively rectifying the collapse induced by token reduction. Together, these components preserve visual evidence even under aggressive compression, enabling robust performance across single-image, multi-image, and video settings on a wide range of MLLMs. Beyond delivering practical acceleration, ERA establishes logit-preserving visual token pruning as a principled framework for efficient MLLMs, unifying theoretical foundation, algorithmic design, and practical deployment. The code is at https://github.com/924973292/ERA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ERA：基于熵引导视觉标记修剪与修正注意力的高效多模态大语言模型
由于长视觉标记序列，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的推理成本极高。无训练的视觉标记缩减提供了一种高效解决方案，但现有方法会扭曲注意力分布，导致我们称之为注意力对数崩溃的现象。为解决此问题，我们提出ERA框架，即熵引导视觉标记修剪与修正注意力，用于高效MLLMs。具体而言，ERA包含三个关键组件：双视角熵修剪（DEP）、偏置感知标记回收（BTR）和对数保持注意力修正（LAR）。首先，DEP通过联合建模视觉多样性与头部显著性识别代表性锚点标记。BTR随后将修剪的标记回收至对应锚点，并估计簇级对数偏置。在此基础上，LAR将估计的偏置注入注意力对数，有效修正由标记缩减引发的崩溃。这些组件共同确保即使在激进压缩下仍能保留视觉证据，使模型在单图像、多图像和视频设置下于多种MLLMs上保持稳健性能。除提供实用加速外，ERA将对数保持视觉标记修剪确立为高效MLLMs的原理性框架，统一理论基础、算法设计与实际部署。代码见https://github.com/924973292/ERA。
Summary / 总结
To address the high inference costs of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) caused by long visual token sequences, ERA introduces an entropy-guided visual token pruning framework with rectified attention. It consists of three components: Dual-view Entropy Pruning identifies representative anchor tokens by combining visual diversity and head-wise saliency; Bias-aware Token Recycling reuses pruned tokens by estimating cluster-level logit biases; and Logit-preserving Attention Rectification injects these biases into attention logits to correct attention collapse. Experiments show ERA maintains strong performance across single-image, multi-image, and video tasks even under aggressive token pruning, achieving significant speedup without accuracy loss on multiple MLLMs.
为解决多模态大语言模型因长视觉token序列导致的高推理成本，本文提出ERA框架，通过训练无关的视觉token剪枝保持注意力质量。该框架包含三个模块：双视角熵剪枝通过结合视觉多样性与头部显著性识别代表性锚点token；偏置感知token回收将剪枝token重用并估计簇级logit偏置；logit保持注意力校正将偏置注入注意力logit以纠正剪枝引发的注意力坍塌。实验表明，即使在激进的token压缩下，ERA在单图、多图和视频任务中仍保持高性能，优于现有方法，实现效率与准确性的平衡。
LUNA: Learning Universal 3D Human Animation Beyond Skinning
Authors: Peng Li, Rawal Khirodkar, Junxuan Li, Yuan Dong, Chen Cao, Yuan Liu, Wenhan Luo, Yike Guo, Shunsuke Saito
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-30T17:19:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:19:55+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026, Project page: https://penghtyx.github.io/LUNA/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Creating photorealistic, animatable 3D human avatars from monocular images still largely depends on Linear Blend Skinning (LBS) and parametric body models, which constrain expressivity and often introduce artifacts due to imperfect fitting. We propose LUNA, an LBS-free universal neural animation model that directly maps multiple 2D controls like images, keypoints, sketches, and unseen characters into 3D Gaussian deformations, bypassing explicit body fitting. At its core, a transformer-based motion regressor disentangles global rigid motion from fine-grained local dynamics to capture both coherent movement and subtle non-rigid effects. To resolve the inherent ambiguity of 2D-to-3D lifting while scaling beyond fitted datasets, we introduce hybrid supervision that distills soft structural priors from an LBS teacher and a loss that supports training on both limited fitted data and large in-the-wild unlabeled videos. Extensive experiments show LUNA achieves competitive visual fidelity compared to LBS-based approaches, while delivering realistic human motion and zero-shot cross-identity generalization across diverse driving modalities. To the best of our knowledge, LUNA is the first end-to-end 3D animatable model that supports implicit 2D driving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LUNA：超越皮肤绑定的通用3D人体动画学习
从单目图像生成逼真可动画的3D人体化身仍主要依赖线性混合皮肤绑定（LBS）和参数化身体模型，这些方法限制了表现力并因拟合不完美常引入伪影。我们提出LUNA，一种无需LBS的通用神经动画模型，可直接将图像、关键点、草图及未见角色等多类2D控制映射为3D高斯形变，绕过显式身体拟合。其核心是一个基于Transformer的运动回归器，将全局刚体运动与细粒度局部动态分离，以捕捉连贯运动与细微非刚性效果。为解决2D到3D提升的固有歧义并扩展至超出拟合数据集的规模，我们引入混合监督机制：从LBS教师模型蒸馏软结构先验，并设计支持在有限拟合数据与大规模野外无标签视频上联合训练的损失函数。大量实验表明，LUNA在视觉保真度上与LBS方法相当，同时实现真实人体运动及跨身份零样本泛化，适用于多种驱动模态。据我们所知，LUNA是首个支持隐式2D驱动的端到端3D可动画模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Linear Blend Skinning in producing expressive and artifact-free 3D human animations, LUNA introduces an LBS-free neural model that directly translates diverse 2D inputs—such as images, keypoints, and sketches—into 3D Gaussian deformations without explicit body fitting. Its core is a transformer-based motion regressor that separates global rigid motion from local non-rigid dynamics, enabling coherent and detailed animation. Trained with hybrid supervision combining structural priors from an LBS teacher and a loss compatible with both fitted data and unlabeled wild videos, LUNA achieves competitive visual fidelity and realistic motion, while generalizing zero-shot across identities and driving modalities, marking the first end-to-end 3D animatable model supporting implicit 2D control.
为了解决线性混合皮肤（LBS）在从单目图像生成逼真可动画3D人体时表达受限且易产生伪影的问题，作者提出了LUNA，一种无需显式身体拟合的神经动画模型，可直接将图像、关键点、素描等多样2D控制信号映射为3D高斯变形。其核心方法采用基于Transformer的运动回归器，分离全局刚体运动与局部非刚性动态，实现连贯且细腻的动画效果。为应对2D到3D映射的固有模糊性并扩展至未见数据，LUNA引入混合监督机制，结合LBS教师模型的结构先验与支持有限拟合数据和大规模野外无标签视频训练的损失函数。实验表明，LUNA在视觉保真度上媲美LBS方法，能生成真实人体运动，并在不同驱动模态下实现零样本跨身份泛化，是首个支持隐式2D驱动的端到端3D可动画模型。
VGGSounder: Audio-Visual Evaluations for Foundation Models
Authors: Daniil Zverev, Thaddäus Wiedemer, Ameya Prabhu, Matthias Bethge, Wieland Brendel, A. Sophia Koepke
Venue: ICCV
First: 2025-08-11T17:53:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:19:50+00:00
Comments: Proceedings of the IEEE/CVF International Conference on Computer Vision (ICCV) 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The emergence of audio-visual foundation models underscores the importance of reliably assessing their multi-modal understanding. The VGGSound dataset is commonly used as a benchmark for evaluation audio-visual classification. However, our analysis identifies several limitations of VGGSound, including incomplete labelling, partially overlapping classes, and misaligned modalities. These lead to distorted evaluations of auditory and visual capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce VGGSounder, a comprehensively re-annotated, multi-label test set that extends VGGSound and is specifically designed to evaluate audio-visual foundation models. VGGSounder features detailed modality annotations, enabling precise analyses of modality-specific performance. Furthermore, we reveal model limitations by analysing performance degradation when adding another input modality with our new modality confusion metric.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VGGSounder：面向基础模型的视听评估
视听基础模型的兴起凸显了可靠评估其多模态理解能力的重要性。VGGSound数据集常被用作视听分类评估的基准，但我们的分析发现其存在若干局限，包括标签不完整、类别部分重叠及模态对齐不当，导致对听觉与视觉能力的评估失真。为解决这些问题，我们提出了VGGSounder，这是一个全面重新标注、支持多标签的测试集，扩展了VGGSound并专为评估视听基础模型设计。VGGSounder具备详细的模态标注，支持对模态特异性性能的精确分析。此外，我们通过新引入的模态混淆度量，分析在添加另一输入模态时模型性能的下降，揭示模型局限性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately evaluate audio-visual foundation models, this work critiques the VGGSound benchmark for its labeling issues and modality misalignment, which distort performance assessments. To remedy this, the authors introduce VGGSounder, a re-annotated, multi-label test set that extends VGGSound with detailed modality-specific annotations to enable precise evaluation. Experimental results show that models perform poorly when additional modalities are introduced, revealing modality confusion and highlighting the need for more robust multi-modal architectures.
为解决现有音频-视觉基础模型评估中因VGGSound数据集标注不全、类别重叠及模态错位导致的评估失真问题，本文提出VGGSounder：一个全面重标注、支持多标签的扩展测试集，具备精细的模态标注以实现模态性能精准分析。实验表明，当引入额外模态时，模型性能显著下降，揭示了模型内在的模态混淆问题，凸显了构建更稳健评估体系的必要性。
DigitalCoach: Communication and Grounding Gaps in Human and Agentic Computer Use Coaching
Authors: Meng Chen, Anya Ji, Tsung-Han Wu, Tobias Maringgele, David M. Chan, Alane Suhr, Amy Pavel
First: 2026-06-30T17:18:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:18:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agents are increasingly capable of automating software tasks, but can they teach humans how to use software themselves? We introduce DigitalCoach, a multimodal dataset of 72 human expert-novice computer use coaching sessions consisting of 22,752 dialogue turns grounded in 28.1 hours of screen and input event recordings across five software applications. We use DigitalCoach to evaluate whether state-of-the-art models can teach humans how to use computers. Automated evaluation shows that models differ from humans in how they coach: models provide more direct instructions, but fewer explanations, error diagnoses, and knowledge-check questions. When we fix the coaching method, models produce utterances similar to human references yet poorly grounded in visual context. Interactive evaluation confirms that model coaches cause learners to passively follow instructions without deeper engagement and fall short in visual grounding. DigitalCoach lays a foundation for collaborative and proactive computer use coaching agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DigitalCoach：人类与代理计算机使用辅导中的沟通与情境锚定差距
代理正日益具备自动化软件任务的能力，但它们能否教会人类如何使用软件？我们引入DigitalCoach，这是一个包含72场人类专家与新手计算机使用辅导会话的多模态数据集，涵盖28.1小时的屏幕与输入事件记录，涉及五个软件应用，共计22,752轮对话。我们利用DigitalCoach评估当前最先进的模型是否能教会人类使用计算机。自动化评估显示，模型在辅导方式上与人类存在差异：模型提供更直接的指令，但较少解释、错误诊断和知识核查问题。当我们固定辅导方法时，模型生成的语句虽与人类参考语句相似，但与视觉情境关联性差。交互式评估证实，模型辅导导致学习者被动执行指令，缺乏深度参与，且在视觉情境锚定方面表现不足。DigitalCoach为协作式与主动式计算机使用辅导代理奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate whether AI agents can effectively coach humans in computer use, the authors introduce DigitalCoach, a multimodal dataset capturing 72 expert-novice coaching sessions across five software applications, totaling 22,752 dialogue turns and 28.1 hours of screen and input recordings. Using this dataset, they assess state-of-the-art models’ coaching abilities and find that while models generate utterances similar to human coaches, they tend to offer more direct instructions and fewer explanations, error diagnoses, or knowledge checks. Experimental results show models struggle with visual grounding, leading learners to passively follow instructions without deeper understanding, indicating current agents lack the interactive and context-aware coaching skills needed for effective human-AI collaboration.
为评估AI代理是否能有效指导人类使用计算机，作者构建了DigitalCoach数据集，包含5款软件中72场专家与新手的辅导对话，共22752轮对话及28.1小时屏幕与输入事件记录。实验评估发现，当前模型虽在固定辅导方法下能生成与人类相似的语句，但更倾向于直接指令而非解释、错误诊断或知识检验。交互式评估表明，模型辅导导致学习者被动执行指令而缺乏深度参与，且视觉上下文关联不足，说明现有模型尚无法实现有效、可靠的辅导。
Planar-SfM: Camera Pose Estimation via Homography Graph Embeddings
Authors: Gabi Pragier, Matan Karklinsky, David Ungarish, Avi Ben-Cohen
First: 2026-06-30T17:18:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:18:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Structure from Motion (SfM) systems traditionally struggle with planar scenes, where standard epipolar geometry-based methods become degenerate. Rather than viewing planar surfaces as a limitation, we propose a unified framework that leverages them as a source of geometric constraints. Our key insight is that each planar surface visible across multiple views provides an independent estimate of relative camera poses through homography decomposition. By aggregating estimates from multiple planes or even from a single dominant plane we achieve robust pose recovery in scenarios where traditional methods fail. We introduce a novel graph-based approach that constructs a pose-graph from homography estimates and employs spectral embedding to identify and filter unreliable edges. Our method maps homography-based pose estimates onto the real line based on their geometric and visual consistency, enabling efficient extraction of a maximally consistent spanning tree for pose recovery. This approach naturally handles both highly planar scenes, such as indoor sports arenas, and general $3$D environments. We demonstrate superior performance on basketball court imagery where existing methods struggle, while matching or exceeding state-of-the-art results on unconstrained outdoor scenes from the IMC Phototourism benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：平面SfM：通过同态图嵌入进行相机位姿估计
传统结构从运动（SfM）系统在平面场景中表现不佳，因为基于极线几何的标准方法会退化。我们不将平面表面视为限制，而是提出一个统一框架，将其作为几何约束来源。核心思路是：每个在多个视角中可见的平面表面，可通过同态分解独立估计相对相机位姿。通过聚合多个平面或单一主导平面的估计结果，我们在传统方法失效的场景中实现稳健的位姿恢复。我们引入一种新颖的图方法，从同态估计构建位姿图，并利用谱嵌入识别和过滤不可靠边。该方法根据几何与视觉一致性将同态位姿估计映射到实数轴，从而高效提取最大一致性生成树以恢复位姿。该方法自然适用于高度平面场景（如室内体育场馆）和一般三维环境。我们在现有方法表现不佳的篮球场图像上展示优越性能，同时在IMC Phototourism基准的无约束户外场景中达到或超越当前最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of traditional SfM methods in planar scenes due to degenerate epipolar geometry, this work proposes Planar-SfM, a framework that treats planar surfaces as geometric constraints rather than limitations. It decomposes homographies from multiple views to estimate relative camera poses, then aggregates these estimates using a graph-based approach with spectral embedding to filter unreliable edges. The method maps pose estimates onto a real line based on geometric and visual consistency, enabling extraction of a maximally consistent spanning tree for robust pose recovery. Experiments show superior performance on basketball court imagery where conventional methods fail, and competitive or better results on unconstrained outdoor scenes from the IMC Phototourism benchmark.
针对传统SfM方法在平面场景中因极线几何退化而失效的问题，该研究提出Planar-SfM框架，将平面视为几何约束而非限制。通过分解多视角的单应性矩阵估计相对相机位姿，构建图结构并利用谱嵌入过滤不可靠边，再依据几何与视觉一致性将位姿估计映射至实数轴，提取最大一致性生成树以恢复位姿。实验表明，该方法在篮球场等高度平面场景中表现优于现有方法，在无约束户外场景上达到或超越当前最优水平。
LiteResearcher: A Scalable Agentic RL Training Framework for Deep Research Agent
Authors: Wanli Li, Bince Qu, Bo Pan, Jianyu Zhang, Zheng Liu, Pan Zhang, Wei Chen, Bo Zhang
First: 2026-04-20T08:11:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:17:12+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful training paradigm for LLM-based agents. However, scaling agentic RL for deep research remains constrained by two coupled challenges: hand-crafted synthetic data fails to elicit genuine real-world search capabilities, and real-world search dependency during RL training introduces instability and prohibitive cost, which limits the scalability of Agentic RL. LiteResearcher is a training framework that makes Agentic RL scalable: by constructing a lite virtual world that mirrors real-world search dynamics, we enable a continuously improving training recipe that empowers a tiny search agent to outperform large-scale open-source and commercial models (e.g., Tongyi DeepResearch and Claude-4.5 Sonnet). Specifically, on common benchmarks such as GAIA and Xbench, our LiteResearcher-4B achieves open-source state-of-the-art results of 71.3% and 78.0% respectively, demonstrating that scalable RL training is a key enabler for Deep Research Agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LiteResearcher：一种面向深度研究代理的可扩展代理强化学习训练框架
强化学习（RL）已成为基于大语言模型（LLM）代理的强大训练范式。然而，为深度研究扩展代理RL仍受限于两个相互关联的挑战：手工合成数据无法激发真实的现实世界搜索能力，而RL训练期间对现实世界搜索的依赖则引入了不稳定性与高昂成本，限制了代理RL的可扩展性。LiteResearcher是一种使代理RL可扩展的训练框架：通过构建一个轻量级虚拟世界，模拟现实世界搜索动态，我们实现了持续改进的训练方案，使小型搜索代理能够超越大型开源及商业模型（如通义DeepResearch和Claude-4.5 Sonnet）。在GAIA和Xbench等常见基准测试中，我们的LiteResearcher-4B分别取得了71.3%和78.0%的开源最先进结果，证明了可扩展的RL训练是深度研究代理的关键使能技术。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current agentic reinforcement learning (RL) in scaling for deep research—namely, synthetic data’s inability to simulate real-world search and the instability/cost of real-world dependency—LiteResearcher introduces a scalable training framework using a lightweight virtual world that mimics real-world search dynamics. This enables continuous agent improvement without real-world reliance. Experiments show LiteResearcher-4B outperforms large open-source and commercial models on GAIA (71.3%) and Xbench (78.0%), establishing scalable RL as critical for advancing deep research agents.
为了解决当前基于强化学习的深度研究代理在扩展性上的瓶颈——即合成数据无法激发真实世界搜索能力、依赖真实世界搜索又导致训练不稳定且成本高昂——LiteResearcher提出了一种轻量级虚拟世界训练框架，模拟真实世界搜索动态，实现持续优化。实验表明，LiteResearcher-4B这一小型模型在GAIA和Xbench等基准测试中分别达到71.3%和78.0%的性能，超越了多个开源及商业大模型，证明了可扩展强化学习训练对深度研究代理的重要性。
TreeAgent: A Generalizable Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Bias Labeling in Forestry via Compiled Expert Rules and Vision-Language Models
Authors: Shiyi Chen, Nicholas Saban, Collin Hargreaves, Huiqi Wang
First: 2026-06-30T17:16:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:16:59+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human-labeled data are widely used as reference annotations in ML, despite known variability across annotators in many expert-driven domains. In addition, expert annotation is slow, inconsistent, and remains a major bottleneck for scaling tasks like tree height bias classification in forestry remote sensing. We propose a multi-agent system (MAS) that orchestrates expert decision trees with Vision-Language Models (VLMs), treating the decision tree as a structural prior while VLMs perform localized semantic perception at individual nodes, with multi-agent voting to mitigate VLM stochasticity. We formalize a Decoupled Declarative Decision (D3) Framework that enables zero-modification generalization across diverse expert-defined decision structures. On a tree bias classification testbed, our framework outperforms supervised ML baselines and reduces the amount of expert labeling effort required. These results suggest that agentic orchestration of VLMs with expert priors can reproduce expert-defined labeling procedures at substantially lower annotation cost while maintaining interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TreeAgent：一种通过编译专家规则与视觉-语言模型实现林业自动化偏倚标注的通用多智能体框架
尽管在许多专家驱动领域中，人工标注数据因标注者间差异而存在变异性，但其仍广泛用作机器学习中的参考标注。此外，专家标注过程缓慢、不一致，仍是如林业遥感中树木高度偏倚分类等任务扩展的主要瓶颈。我们提出一种多智能体系统（MAS），通过协调专家决策树与视觉-语言模型（VLM），将决策树作为结构先验，而VLM在各节点执行局部语义感知，并通过多智能体投票缓解VLM的随机性。我们形式化了“解耦声明式决策”（D3）框架，使其能在多样化的专家定义决策结构间实现零修改泛化。在树木偏倚分类测试平台上，我们的框架优于监督机器学习基线方法，并显著减少专家标注工作量。这些结果表明，结合专家先验对VLM进行智能体编排，可在大幅降低标注成本的同时保持可解释性，并复现专家定义的标注流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and inconsistency of human expert labeling in forestry remote sensing, this paper introduces TreeAgent, a multi-agent framework that combines compiled expert decision trees with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to automate bias labeling. The system treats decision trees as structural priors while VLMs perform localized semantic perception at each node, with multi-agent voting to reduce VLM stochasticity. The Decoupled Declarative Decision (D3) framework enables zero-modification generalization across diverse expert decision structures. Experimental results on a tree bias classification task show TreeAgent outperforms supervised ML baselines and significantly reduces the need for expert annotations, demonstrating that agentic orchestration with expert priors can replicate expert labeling at lower cost while preserving interpretability.
为解决林业遥感中人工专家标注效率低、不一致的问题，本文提出TreeAgent框架，结合编译后的专家决策树与视觉-语言模型（VLM），实现偏置标注自动化。决策树作为结构先验，VLM在各节点执行局部语义感知，多智能体投票降低VLM的随机性。Decoupled Declarative Decision（D3）框架支持零修改泛化至不同专家决策结构。实验表明，该框架在树偏置分类任务中优于监督学习基线，大幅减少专家标注需求，同时保持可解释性。
Semantic Leakage and Privacy Preservation in Relay-Assisted Semantic Communications
Authors: Yalin E. Sagduyu, Tugba Erpek, Aylin Yener, Sennur Ulukus
First: 2026-06-30T17:15:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:15:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Semantic communication (SemCom) has emerged as a promising paradigm in which the transmission of task-relevant information is prioritized over raw data, enabling efficient and robust communication under resource and channel constraints.
