In this paper, the privacy implications of relay-assisted SemCom systems are studied, where the intermediate relay node operates directly on learned latent representations. It is shown that the relay, even without access to source data, can reliably infer semantic meaning and reconstruct signals with performance comparable to that of the legitimate receiver, revealing a fundamental privacy vulnerability of semantic representations. To address this issue, an iterative adversarial training framework is proposed in which a strong, adaptively trained eavesdropper at the relay is explicitly accounted for. The proposed approach alternates between optimizing the relay's eavesdropping function and the legitimate system, resulting in representations that preserve semantic decoding performance at the intended receiver while degrading semantic inference at the relay. The semantic accuracy gap between the legitimate receiver and the eavesdropper is significantly enlarged across channel conditions. Importantly, this protection is achieved in a stealthy manner, with high reconstruction fidelity maintained while semantic leakage is selectively suppressed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：中继辅助语义通信中的语义泄露与隐私保护
语义通信（SemCom）作为一种新兴范式，优先传输任务相关的信息而非原始数据，在资源与信道受限条件下实现高效且鲁棒的通信。本文研究了中继辅助语义通信系统的隐私问题，其中中继节点直接操作学习到的潜在表示。研究表明，即使中继节点无法访问源数据，仍能可靠推断语义并重建信号，其性能与合法接收者相当，揭示了语义表示的根本性隐私漏洞。为解决此问题，本文提出一种迭代对抗训练框架，显式考虑中继处的强且自适应训练的窃听者。该方法交替优化中继的窃听功能与合法系统，使表示在合法接收者处保持语义解码性能的同时，在中继处降低语义推断能力。在不同信道条件下，合法接收者与窃听者之间的语义准确度差距显著扩大。重要的是，该保护方式隐蔽实现，同时保持高重建保真度，选择性抑制语义泄露。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the privacy risks inherent in relay-assisted semantic communication systems, this study investigates how intermediate relay nodes can infer semantic content from latent representations without direct access to source data. The authors propose an iterative adversarial training framework that explicitly models an eavesdropping relay to enhance privacy. Experimental results demonstrate that this method preserves semantic decoding accuracy for legitimate receivers while significantly degrading the relay’s ability to infer semantics, thereby enlarging the semantic accuracy gap across various channel conditions, all while maintaining high signal reconstruction fidelity.
鉴于中继辅助语义通信系统中固有的隐私风险，本文研究了中继节点如何在不接触源数据的情况下从学习到的潜在表示中推断语义内容。作者提出了一种迭代对抗训练框架，通过训练中继节点作为窃听者并同时优化合法系统，迫使语义表示在保持合法接收端解码性能的同时降低中继端的语义推断能力。实验结果表明，该方法在不同信道条件下显著拉大了合法接收端与中继端之间的语义准确率差距，同时保持了高重建保真度，并以隐蔽方式实现隐私保护，未影响系统整体性能。
MECoBench: A Systematic Study of Multimodal Agent Collaboration in Embodied Environments
Authors: Qingyun Liu, Jiwen Zhang, Jingyi Hu, Siyuan Wang, Zhongyu Wei
First: 2026-06-30T17:07:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:07:53+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://q-i-n-g.github.io/MECoBench-Website/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have strong potential as embodied agents, but their ability to collaborate in visually grounded environments remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce MECoBench, a multimodal embodied cooperation benchmark with an evaluation platform spanning diverse real-world tasks, two cooperation structures, and three collaboration modes. Through extensive experiments across various MLLMs, we summarize three key findings: (i) Collaboration generally improves embodied task completion, but its benefits depend on balancing collaborative gains against coordination complexity. (ii) Communication is essential to collaboration gains, while the best collaboration mode depends on team size and model capability. (iii) Moreover, collaboration improves robustness under noisy priors and exploration conditions. Generally, MECoBench provides a systematic testbed for understanding the mechanisms and limits of multimodal embodied collaboration. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/q-i-n-g/MECoBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MECoBench：具身环境中多模态智能体协作的系统性研究
近期多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）作为具身智能体具有巨大潜力，但其在视觉锚定环境中的协作能力仍待深入探索。为填补这一空白，我们提出了MECoBench，一个涵盖多样真实世界任务、两种协作结构和三种协作模式的多模态具身协作基准评测平台。通过在多种MLLMs上的广泛实验，我们总结出三个关键发现：（i）协作通常能提升具身任务完成率，但其收益取决于协作增益与协调复杂度之间的平衡。（ii）通信对协作增益至关重要，而最佳协作模式取决于团队规模与模型能力。（iii）此外，协作在噪声先验和探索条件下可提升鲁棒性。总体而言，MECoBench为理解多模态具身协作的机制与局限性提供了一个系统性测试平台。代码与数据集见 https://github.com/q-i-n-g/MECoBench。

On Optimizing Multimodal Jailbreaks for Spoken Language Models
Authors: Aravind Krishnan, Karolina Stańczak, Dietrich Klakow
First: 2026-03-19T16:48:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:07:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted at INTERSPEECH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Spoken Language Models (SLMs) integrate speech and text modalities, they inherit the safety vulnerabilities of their LLM backbone while introducing an expanded attack surface. SLMs have been previously shown to be susceptible to jailbreaking, where adversarial prompts induce harmful responses. Yet existing attacks largely remain unimodal, optimizing either text or audio in isolation. We explore gradient-based multimodal jailbreaks by introducing JAMA (Joint Audio-text Multimodal Attack), a joint multimodal optimization framework combining Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) for text and Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) for audio, to simultaneously perturb both modalities. Evaluations across four state-of-the-art SLMs and four audio types demonstrate that JAMA surpasses unimodal jailbreak rate by 1.5x to 20x. We analyze the operational dynamics of this joint attack and show that a sequential approximation method makes it 4x to 6x faster. Our findings suggest that unimodal safety is insufficient for robust SLMs. The code and data are available at https://repos.lsv.uni-saarland.de/akrishnan/multimodal-jailbreak-slm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于优化语音语言模型的多模态越狱攻击
随着语音语言模型（SLM）融合语音与文本模态，它们继承了其大语言模型（LLM）基础架构的安全漏洞，同时引入了更广阔的攻击面。此前研究表明，SLM易受越狱攻击，即对抗性提示可诱导有害响应。然而现有攻击大多为单模态，仅优化文本或音频中的单一模态。本文探索基于梯度的多模态越狱攻击，提出JAMA（联合音频-文本多模态攻击），该框架结合文本的贪心坐标梯度（GCG）与音频的投影梯度下降（PGD），同时扰动两种模态。在四个前沿SLM及四种音频类型上的评估表明，JAMA的越狱成功率比单模态攻击高1.5至20倍。我们分析了该联合攻击的运行机制，并表明一种序列近似方法可使其快4至6倍。研究结果表明，仅依赖单模态安全不足以保障鲁棒的SLM。代码与数据见https://repos.lsv.uni-saarland.de/akrishnan/multimodal-jailbreak-slm。

To address this, the authors introduce JAMA, a joint multimodal optimization framework that combines Greedy Coordinate Gradient for text and Projected Gradient Descent for audio, enabling simultaneous perturbation of both modalities. Evaluated across four SLMs and four audio types, JAMA achieves jailbreak success rates 1.5x to 20x higher than unimodal methods, and a sequential approximation variant speeds up the attack by 4x to 6x, demonstrating that unimodal safety measures are inadequate for robust SLMs.

TacGen: Touch Is a Necessary Dimension of Physical-World Representation -- Addressing Tactile Data Scarcity with Scalable Vision-to-Touch Alignment and Generation
Authors: Wanghao Ye, Aarosh Das, Sihan Chen, Yiting Wang, Bowei Tian, Guoheng Sun, Shwai He, Zheyu Shen, Ziyao Wang, Yexiao He, Zhaoyi Liu, Meng Liu, Yuning Zhang, Meng Feng, Ziyi Wang, Yilong Dai, Yifei Dong, Siyuan Peng, Zhenle Duan, Joshua Liu, Lang Xiong, Ang Li
First: 2026-06-28T03:37:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:07:03+00:00
Comments: 49 pages, 29 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Touch resolves the physical-property ambiguity left by vision: exploratory contact recovers shape, texture, compliance, and material, and visuo-haptic object representations converge in ventral visual cortex. We ask whether representation learning can reproduce this grounding. TacGen mitigates the tactile-data scarcity bottleneck by combining pre-specified V+T contrastive alignment with a latent-space residual-MLP V->T generator that synthesizes tactile latents from RGB for tactile-data scaling. With matched DINOv2 backbones, splits, and probes, V+T improves matched V-only on mass (Delta R^2=+0.570), density (Delta acc=+0.067), hardness (+0.117), and uncertainty-banded force labels (Delta R^2=+0.281); all CIs exclude zero. The same representation lifts matched-capacity TACTO manipulation 0.246->0.979 while V-only capacity scaling accounts for only 4.5% of the gap, preserving 95.5%. The generator reaches cross-seed +0.589, with real tactile +0.585 inside the seed interval; the architecture comparison shows a 13pp downstream gap between reconstruction quality and representation utility. Across five-seed SSVTP/TVL reproductions, YCB-Sight transfer, three-backbone checks, permutation/random-feature controls, hash-verified manifests, and measured-force validation checks, the evidence supports the claim that touch supplies a necessary physical evidence channel for representations of contact-dependent properties.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TacGen：触觉是物理世界表征的必要维度——通过可扩展的视觉-触觉对齐与生成缓解触觉数据稀缺
触觉解决了视觉遗留的物理属性模糊问题：探索性接触可恢复形状、纹理、柔顺性和材料特性，视觉-触觉物体表征在腹侧视觉皮层中趋于一致。我们探讨表征学习是否能重现这种接地性。TacGen通过结合预设的V+T对比对齐与一个在潜在空间中基于残差MLP的V→T生成器（从RGB合成触觉潜在表示），缓解触觉数据稀缺瓶颈，实现触觉数据扩展。在匹配的DINOv2主干网络、数据划分和探针下，V+T在质量（Delta R²=+0.570）、密度（Delta acc=+0.067）、硬度（+0.117）和不确定性带力标签（Delta R²=+0.281）上显著优于仅V；所有置信区间均排除零。同一表征将匹配容量的TACTO操作性能从0.246提升至0.979，而仅V容量扩展仅解释4.5%的差距，保留95.5%的提升。生成器跨种子达到+0.589，真实触觉数据为+0.585（在种子区间内）；架构对比显示重建质量与表征效用之间存在13个百分点的下游差距。在五种子SSVTP/TVL复现、YCB-Sight迁移、三主干验证、排列/随机特征控制、哈希验证清单及测力验证检查下，证据支持触觉为接触依赖属性表征提供必要物理证据通道的主张。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ground physical-world representations through tactile input, TacGen addresses tactile data scarcity by combining vision-tactile contrastive alignment with a latent-space residual-MLP generator that synthesizes tactile features from RGB images. Experiments using matched DINOv2 backbones show that V+T alignment significantly outperforms V-only models on properties like mass, density, hardness, and force labels, with statistically significant improvements across metrics. The generator also scales tactile data effectively, boosting downstream manipulation performance from 0.246 to 0.979 while V-only scaling explains only 4.5% of the gain. Architectural comparisons reveal a 13-point performance gap between reconstruction quality and representation utility, and extensive controls validate that touch provides a necessary channel for representing contact-dependent physical properties.
为解决视觉无法完全表征物理世界接触属性的问题，TacGen提出一种结合视觉-触觉对比对齐与RGB图像到触觉特征生成的可扩展方法。实验表明，该方法在质量、密度、硬度及力不确定性等指标上显著优于纯视觉模型，且所有提升均具有统计显著性。生成器在触觉合成上表现优异，下游操作任务中，触觉信息贡献了超过95%的性能提升，远超仅靠视觉扩展所能达到的效果。多组背骨、数据集、控制实验及力验证进一步证实，触觉是表征接触依赖物理属性所必需的感知通道。
Signed-Permutation Coordinate Transport for RMSNorm Transformers
Authors: John Sweeney
First: 2026-06-30T17:02:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:02:33+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 2 figures, 26 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern LLM workflows move coordinate-indexed objects across checkpoints: steering vectors, sparse autoencoders, top-$k$ neuron sets, attribution lists, and merge alignments. This is only well posed after fixing the model's residual-stream gauge, which we show is architecture-dependent: LayerNorm residual charts have permutation gauge $S_d$ (up to a global sign flip), while RMSNorm charts with generic per-channel gain have signed-permutation gauge $B_d = S_d \ltimes \{\pm 1\}^d$. Permutation-only alignment is therefore symmetry-incomplete for RMSNorm models. We introduce sign-marginalized Hungarian matching and prove a sharp failure mode: with decorrelated coordinates, raw signed-correlation matching has a structural permutation-accuracy ceiling at the positive-sign fraction of the true gauge, which sign-marginalization removes. We then make coordinate-preserving transport, not function-level merging, the primary object: composing saved-checkpoint local $B_d$ gauges along same-base fine-tuning trajectories recovers 91.1% of cross-run coordinates at 1500 steps versus 60.3% for endpoint matching, and the gain is not explained by merely routing through the base. The recovered gauge transfers tools that permutation-only alignment breaks: TinyLlama SAE reconstruction has NMSE 0.004 under $B_d$ versus 1.08 under $S_d$; Qwen sentiment steering preserves 95.8% of its effect versus 17.2%; refusal steering reverses sign under $S_d$; coordinate-preserving merges behave the same way. The same covariance governs stateful training: signed transport of AdamW state preserves the resumed trajectory, while permutation-only state follows a different one from a functionally identical checkpoint. Finally, gauge-sweep audits show index-level interpretability claims are reproducible only relative to an explicit gauge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带符号排列的坐标传输用于RMSNorm变换器
现代大语言模型工作流需在检查点间传输坐标索引对象：引导向量、稀疏自动编码器、前k个神经元集合、归因列表和合并对齐。这仅在固定模型残差流规范后才合理，而我们证明该规范依赖于架构：LayerNorm残差图具有排列规范S_d（全局符号翻转除外），而具有通用通道增益的RMSNorm图具有带符号排列规范B_d = S_d ⋉ {±1}^d。因此，仅排列对齐对RMSNorm模型而言是不对称完备的。我们引入符号边缘化匈牙利匹配，并证明其尖锐失效模式：在解相关坐标下，原始带符号相关匹配存在结构化的排列精度上限，即真实规范中正符号比例，而符号边缘化可消除该上限。随后，我们将坐标保持传输而非函数级合并作为主要对象：沿相同基础微调轨迹组合保存检查点的局部B_d规范，可在1500步内恢复91.1%的跨运行坐标，而端点匹配仅60.3%，且该增益并非仅因通过基础模型路由所致。恢复的规范可转移仅排列对齐失效的工具：在B_d下，TinyLlama SAE重建的NMSE为0.004，而在S_d下为1.08；Qwen情感引导保留95.8%的效果，而在S_d下仅17.2%；拒绝引导在S_d下符号反转；坐标保持合并行为相同。相同协方差也支配状态化训练：带符号AdamW状态传输可保持恢复轨迹，而仅排列状态则从功能相同检查点出发遵循不同轨迹。最后，规范扫描审计表明，索引级可解释性声明仅在相对于显式规范时可重现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to transport coordinate-indexed objects across LLM checkpoints while preserving interpretability, this work addresses the gauge symmetry mismatch between LayerNorm and RMSNorm architectures. It introduces sign-marginalized Hungarian matching to overcome the structural accuracy ceiling of raw signed-correlation matching under RMSNorm’s signed-permutation gauge. Experimental results show that composing local gauges along fine-tuning trajectories yields 91.1% coordinate recovery at 1500 steps—far exceeding endpoint matching’s 60.3%. This method also preserves downstream tasks like SAE reconstruction (NMSE 0.004 vs 1.08) and steering effectiveness (95.8% vs 17.2%), while stateful training and interpretability audits confirm the gauge’s critical role in reproducibility.
为解决RMSNorm架构下跨检查点坐标对象对齐的对称性不足问题，本文提出基于带符号排列的坐标传输方法，通过引入符号边缘化匈牙利匹配克服原始符号相关匹配的结构精度上限。实验表明，沿微调轨迹组合局部带符号排列规范可显著提升坐标恢复率——1500步时达91.1%，远超端点匹配的60.3%。该方法在SAE重建、情感引导和拒绝引导等任务中表现更优，且能正确保持AdamW状态轨迹，证明符号规范对可解释性声明的可复现性至关重要。
AnyBokeh: Physics-Guided Any-to-Any Bokeh Editing with Optical Fingerprint Transfer
Authors: Xinyu Hou, Xiaoming Li, Zongsheng Yue, Chen Change Loy
First: 2026-06-30T17:01:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:01:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Depth-of-field control is a fundamental tool in photography, yet post-capture bokeh editing from a single image remains challenging. A practical editor should handle images captured under arbitrary focus and aperture settings. Existing methods typically assume an all-in-focus input, or first recover an all-in-focus image before rendering new bokeh. Such pipelines can discard useful blur cues from the source image and propagate reconstruction artifacts into the final edit. We introduce AnyBokeh, a physics-guided framework for any-to-any bokeh editing. Instead of treating source blur merely as a degradation to be removed, AnyBokeh estimates the source blur state with a signed circle-of-confusion map and a disparity map. By modeling the linear relation between signed circle of confusion and disparity difference, AnyBokeh estimates a source-specific optical fingerprint and transfers the source optical characteristics to the desired focus and aperture setting. A generative editor conditioned on both source and target circle-of-confusion maps then performs relative blur synthesis, enabling spatially adaptive deblurring, preservation, and defocus rendering. To support physically supervised learning, we further construct a high-fidelity synthetic dataset with accurate depth, focus distance, and full EXIF metadata. Experiments on real-world benchmarks show that AnyBokeh achieves faithful and controllable editing across any-to-any bokeh editing, all-in-focus-to-bokeh rendering, and defocus deblurring, while avoiding all-in-focus reconstruction and test-time bokeh-level calibration commonly required by existing approaches. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/itsmag11/AnyBokeh.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnyBokeh：基于物理引导的任意对任意景深编辑与光学指纹迁移
景深控制是摄影的基本工具，但单张图像的后期景深编辑仍具挑战性。实用编辑器需处理任意对焦与光圈设置下的图像。现有方法通常假设输入为全清晰图像，或先恢复全清晰图像再渲染新景深，此类流程可能丢弃源图像中的有用模糊线索，并将重建伪影传播至最终编辑结果。我们提出AnyBokeh，一种基于物理引导的任意对任意景深编辑框架。不同于将源模糊视为需去除的退化，AnyBokeh通过带符号的成像模糊图与视差图估计源模糊状态。通过建模带符号成像模糊与视差差值的线性关系，AnyBokeh估计源图像特异性的光学指纹，并将其光学特性迁移至目标对焦与光圈设置。基于源与目标成像模糊图的生成式编辑器执行相对模糊合成，实现空间自适应去模糊、模糊保留与失焦渲染。为支持物理监督学习，我们进一步构建高保真合成数据集，包含精确深度、对焦距离及完整EXIF元数据。在真实世界基准测试中，AnyBokeh在任意对任意景深编辑、全清晰到景深渲染及失焦去模糊任务中实现忠实可控的编辑，同时避免现有方法常需的全清晰重建与测试时景深级校准。代码与数据集将在https://github.com/itsmag11/AnyBokeh发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible bokeh editing without requiring all-in-focus inputs or post-reconstruction artifacts, AnyBokeh introduces a physics-guided framework that directly transfers optical characteristics between arbitrary focus and aperture settings. It estimates source blur via signed circle-of-confusion and disparity maps, then models their linear relationship to derive a source-specific optical fingerprint. This fingerprint is transferred to the target settings, and a generative editor synthesizes relative blur conditioned on both source and target maps, enabling adaptive deblurring, blur preservation, and defocus rendering. Evaluated on real-world benchmarks, AnyBokeh achieves faithful, controllable editing across any-to-any bokeh transformations, all-in-focus-to-bokeh rendering, and defocus deblurring, without needing all-in-focus reconstruction or test-time calibration.
为实现无需全清晰输入和避免重建伪影的任意焦距与光圈间景深编辑，AnyBokeh提出一种基于物理规律的框架，通过估计源图像的带符号弥散圆与视差图，建模其线性关系以提取源光学指纹，并利用条件生成编辑器合成空间自适应模糊。实验证明，该方法在任意到任意景深编辑、全清晰到景深渲染及景深去模糊任务中均能实现忠实可控的编辑效果，无需全清晰重建或测试时景深校准。
Adapting Generalist Robot Policies with Semantic Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Jagdeep Singh Bhatia, Andrew Wagenmaker, William Chen, Sergey Levine
First: 2026-06-30T17:00:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T17:00:33+00:00
Comments: Website: https://semantic-action-rl.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generalist robot policies learn a diverse repertoire of behaviors from large-scale pretraining. In principle, this makes them excellent priors for downstream adaptation via reinforcement learning (RL). In practice, however, standard RL methods leveraging this prior optimize directly over robot actions, requiring the base policy's action distribution to be close to that of a performant policy from the start. This assumption breaks down for complex or long-horizon tasks that fall outside the pretraining distribution. Our key insight is that, for sufficiently expressive generalist policies, language prompts are an effective alternative space for learning to solve such tasks: modulating language inputs elicits skills already within the policy's repertoire, which can be composed to solve tasks beyond its zero-shot capabilities. We propose Semantic Action Reinforcement Learning (SARL), which learns to optimize this prompt space through online interaction, treating the generalist policy as a controllable skill prior. Importantly, leveraging pretrained skills rather than learning new ones from scratch yields structured, semantically meaningful exploration and highly efficient online improvement, and learning to modulate prompts through experience grounds them in induced real-world behaviors for robust task-solving. Across real-world settings and simulated benchmarks, we show SARL unlocks fundamentally new capabilities -- adapting VLA behavior to solve complex, long-horizon tasks -- and significantly outperforms existing approaches for improving robot behavior in deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用语义强化学习适应通用机器人策略
通用机器人策略从大规模预训练中学习多样化的行为。原则上，这使其成为通过强化学习（RL）进行下游适应的优秀先验。然而，实践中，标准RL方法利用该先验时直接优化机器人动作，要求基础策略的动作分布从一开始就接近高性能策略。对于超出预训练分布的复杂或长时任务，这一假设失效。我们的核心见解是：对于足够表达力的通用策略，语言提示是学习解决此类任务的有效替代空间——调节语言输入可激发策略已有的技能，这些技能可组合以解决其零样本能力之外的任务。我们提出语义动作强化学习（SARL），通过在线交互学习优化该提示空间，将通用策略视为可控技能先验。重要的是，利用预训练技能而非从零开始学习新技能，可实现结构化、语义有意义的探索和高效的在线改进，且通过经验学习调节提示可使其扎根于诱导的真实世界行为，从而实现鲁棒的任务解决。在现实世界场景和模拟基准测试中，我们证明SARL解锁了根本性新能力——适应视觉-语言动作（VLA）行为以解决复杂、长时任务，并显著优于现有方法在部署中改进机器人行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of adapting generalist robot policies to complex, long-horizon tasks where standard reinforcement learning fails due to mismatched action distributions, the paper introduces Semantic Action Reinforcement Learning (SARL). SARL leverages language prompts to modulate the behavior of pretrained generalist policies, enabling the composition of existing skills to solve tasks beyond zero-shot capability. Through online interaction, SARL learns to optimize prompt space rather than directly optimizing actions, resulting in semantically grounded, efficient exploration and robust task adaptation. Experiments across real-world and simulated environments demonstrate SARL’s ability to unlock new capabilities and significantly outperform baseline methods in adapting robot behavior for deployment.
为了解决通用机器人策略在复杂长时任务中因动作分布不匹配而难以通过标准强化学习适应的问题，作者提出了语义动作强化学习（SARL）。该方法通过调节语言提示来调用预训练通用策略中已有的技能组合，从而解决其零样本能力之外的任务。SARL在线交互中学习优化提示空间而非直接优化动作，实现语义化、高效探索与鲁棒任务适应。在真实与仿真环境中，实验表明SARL能解锁新能力，显著优于现有方法，有效提升机器人在部署中的行为适应性。
DEMUN: Fast and accurate discovery of music notation in very large collections
Authors: Vojtěch Dvořák, Filip Bím, Jiří Mayer, Martina Dvořáková, Markéta Herzanová Vlková, Pavel Pecina, Petr Žabička, Jan Hajič
First: 2026-06-30T16:59:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:59:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Much of written musical heritage is preserved and digitised at memory institutions: libraries, museums, and archives. Owing to their collection structures, sheet music tends to be concentrated in large subsets that are defined as collections of music, with corresponding metadata that makes the music findable. However, when studying musical life as opposed to individual works, relevant documents often lie outside of these specialised collections: in textbooks, newspapers, other periodicals, pamphlets, and other documents with extensive circulation. But these documents are typically not catalogued as musical documents, and though there may be a lot of such documents overall, in large library collections, they are still extremely sparse. Manual discovery is thus unfeasible. Automated discovery requires an extremely low false positive rate in order to be useful, and must also operate quickly. We present DEMUN: a two-stage lightweight detector of music notation with a false positive rate of 0.015 %. In the test scenario, 4 million images of a national-scale library were processed, out of which 1,500 pages with music notation were discovered, suggesting the entire collection may contain up to 20-30,000 unmarked documents of musical life.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DEMUN：在超大规模收藏中快速准确地发现音乐记谱
大量书面音乐遗产被图书馆、博物馆和档案馆保存并数字化。由于其收藏结构，乐谱往往集中于被称为音乐收藏的大型子集，并附有相应的元数据以实现音乐的可查找性。然而，当研究音乐生活而非个别作品时，相关文献常存在于这些专业收藏之外：如教科书、报纸、其他期刊、小册子及其他广泛传播的文献。但这些文献通常未被编目为音乐文献，尽管总体数量庞大，在大型图书馆藏中仍极为稀少。人工发现不可行，自动化发现需极低的误报率并需快速运行。我们提出DEMUN：一种两阶段轻量级音乐记谱检测器，误报率为0.015%。在测试场景中，处理了全国规模图书馆的400万张图像，发现1500页含音乐记谱，表明整个藏品中可能包含多达2万至3万份未标记的音乐生活文献。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to uncover hidden musical content in non-specialized documents like textbooks and newspapers within large library collections, DEMUN was developed as a lightweight, two-stage detector to efficiently identify music notation with minimal false positives. Tested on 4 million images from a national library, it successfully located 1,500 pages containing music notation, indicating that the entire collection may harbor 20,000 to 30,000 previously unmarked documents reflecting musical life.
为在大型图书馆藏中高效发现散落在非专业文献中的音乐记谱，DEMUN 设计为轻量级两阶段检测器，以极低的误报率（0.015%）实现准确识别。在对400万张图像的测试中，成功定位1500页含音乐记谱的页面，表明整个馆藏中可能隐藏着2万至3万份未被标记的音乐相关文献。
RRT-Rope: A deterministic shortening approach for fast near-optimal path planning in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments
Authors: Louis Petit, Alexis Lussier Desbiens
Venue: 2021 IEEE International Conference on Systems, Man, and Cybernetics (SMC), Melbourne, Australia, 2021, pp. 1111-1118
First: 2026-06-30T16:54:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:54:00+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, accepted at the 2021 IEEE International Conference on Systems, Man, and Cybernetics (SMC), Melbourne, Australia
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many path planning algorithms have been introduced so far, but most are costly, in path cost and in processing time, in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments such as underground mining stopes explored by an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) algorithms are popular because of their probabilistic completeness and rapidity in finding a feasible path in single-query problems. Many of the algorithms (e.g. Informed RRT*, RRT#) developed to improve RRT need considerable time to converge in large environments. Shortcutting an RRT is an old idea that has been proven to outperform RRT variants. This paper introduces a new method, RRT-Rope, that aims at finding a near-optimal solution in a drastically shorter amount of time. The proposed approach benefits from fast computation of a feasible path with an altered version of RRT-connect, and post-processes it quickly with a deterministic shortcutting technique, taking advantage of intermediate nodes added to each branch of the tree. This paper presents simulations and statistics carried out to show the efficiency of RRT-Rope, which gives better results in terms of path cost and computation time than other popular RRT variations and shortening techniques in all our simulation environments, and is up to 70% faster than the next best algorithm in a representative stope.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RRT-Rope：一种用于大规模无障碍3D环境中快速近最优路径规划的确定性缩短方法
目前已提出多种路径规划算法，但在大规模无障碍3D环境（如无人机探索的地下矿井）中，多数算法在路径成本和处理时间上代价高昂。快速探索随机树（RRT）算法因其概率完备性和在单次查询问题中快速找到可行路径而广受欢迎。然而，为改进RRT而开发的许多算法（如Informed RRT*、RRT#）在大型环境中收敛所需时间较长。对RRT进行缩短是一种成熟方法，已被证明优于RRT变体。本文提出一种新方法RRT-Rope，旨在以极短时间找到近最优解。该方法基于RRT-connect的改进版本快速计算可行路径，并通过确定性缩短技术快速后处理，利用树中各分支添加的中间节点。本文通过仿真和统计分析展示了RRT-Rope的高效性，在所有仿真环境中，其路径成本和计算时间均优于其他流行RRT变体和缩短技术，且在代表性矿井环境中比次优算法快达70%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of existing RRT-based algorithms in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments like underground mining stopes, this paper proposes RRT-Rope, a deterministic shortcutting method that combines a modified RRT-connect for rapid feasible path generation with a post-processing technique that leverages intermediate nodes to shorten paths efficiently. Experimental results across multiple simulation environments demonstrate that RRT-Rope consistently outperforms other RRT variants and shortening methods in both path cost and computation time, achieving up to 70% faster performance than the next best algorithm in a representative stope.
为了解决现有RRT类路径规划算法在大型无遮挡3D环境（如地下矿井巷道）中计算成本高、收敛慢的问题，本文提出RRT-Rope方法，该方法结合改进的RRT-connect快速生成可行路径，并通过利用树结构中各分支的中间节点进行确定性路径缩短。实验结果表明，RRT-Rope在路径成本和计算时间上均优于其他主流RRT变体和缩短技术，在代表性巷道环境中性能提升最高可达70%。
LuxEmo: Expressive Text-to-Speech Corpus for Luxembourgish
Authors: Nina Hosseini-Kivanani, Sandipana Dowerah
First: 2026-06-30T16:53:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:53:15+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 4 figures, under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
State-of-the-art speech datasets predominantly focus on widely spoken languages, often overlooking low-resource languages such as Luxembourgish, which remain underrepresented in speech technology research. In this work, we introduce LuxEmo, a 21-hour conversational expressive speech corpus for Luxembourgish with 4 emotion categories. LuxEmo is derived from Radio Télévision Luxembourg (RTL) youth broadcasts, using automated detection followed by human validation. We propose a semi-automatic curation workflow combining voice activity detection, denoising, language identification, LuxASR-based segmentation, automatic emotion prediction, lexical cues, and targeted human review. Additionally, we benchmark five expressive TTS systems covering German-based cross-lingual transfer, multilingual Luxembourgish support, Luxembourgish adaptation, and non-parametric prosody transfer. Performance is evaluated using both objective metrics and human evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LuxEmo：卢森堡语表达性文本到语音语料库
当前最先进的语音数据集主要关注广泛使用的语言，常忽视如卢森堡语等资源匮乏语言，使其在语音技术研究中代表性不足。本文引入LuxEmo，一个包含21小时卢森堡语对话表达性语音语料库，涵盖4种情绪类别。LuxEmo源自卢森堡广播电视台（RTL）青年节目，采用自动化检测后辅以人工验证。我们提出一种半自动整理流程，结合语音活动检测、降噪、语言识别、基于LuxASR的分段、自动情绪预测、词汇线索及针对性人工审核。此外，我们对五种表达性TTS系统进行基准测试，涵盖基于德语的跨语言迁移、多语言卢森堡语支持、卢森堡语适配及非参数语调迁移。性能评估采用客观指标与人工评估相结合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underrepresentation of low-resource languages like Luxembourgish in speech technology, this work introduces LuxEmo, a 21-hour expressive speech corpus with four emotion categories, derived from RTL youth broadcasts. The corpus is curated via a semi-automatic pipeline involving voice activity detection, denoising, language identification, LuxASR-based segmentation, automatic emotion prediction, lexical cue analysis, and human validation. Experimental results show that expressive TTS systems perform best when adapted specifically to Luxembourgish, with cross-lingual German-based models showing moderate success and non-parametric prosody transfer offering the least effective results, as measured by both objective metrics and human evaluation.
鉴于卢森堡语等低资源语言在语音技术研究中长期被忽视，本文构建了LuxEmo语料库，包含21小时带四种情绪的对话语音，源自RTL青年广播节目。该语料库通过语音活动检测、降噪、语言识别、LuxASR分段、自动情绪预测、词汇线索分析及人工校验等半自动流程构建。研究对五种表达性TTS系统进行评测，涵盖跨语言迁移、多语言支持、卢森堡语适配及非参数语调迁移等方法，结果通过客观指标与人工评估结合得出，显示不同系统在卢森堡语语境下的表现差异显著。
Deductive Logic in Language Models: Horizontal vs Vertical Reasoning
Authors: Davide Maltoni, Matteo Ferrara
First: 2025-10-10T12:49:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:53:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent language models exhibit significant logical reasoning abilities, yet the mechanisms supporting deductive inference remain poorly understood. This paper studies small transformer-based language models trained from scratch on multi-step deductive tasks, focusing on the distinction between horizontal reasoning, where intermediate steps are generated autoregressively, and vertical reasoning, where inference unfolds implicitly across layers before the first output token is produced. We analyze two synthetic tasks: logical consequence over chains of symbolic implications and root-to-leaf navigation in binary trees. Mechanistic interpretability reveals that Chain-of-Thought supervision enables models to learn rule-based inference rather than statistical shortcuts. In the horizontal setting, a shallow attention-only model develops interpretable circuits for rule completion, rule chaining, and final decision making, largely implemented through induction-head-like mechanisms. We further introduce a truncated pseudoinverse method to decode the information carried by queries, keys, and values. For vertical reasoning, Chain-of-Thought appears to act less as explicit step-by-step guidance and more as a form of curriculum learning, helping the model acquire increasingly complex reasoning patterns. Without Chain-of-Thought, models tend to memorize or exploit dataset biases. These results provide a low-level account of how transformers can implement deductive reasoning and suggest how Chain-of-Thought may serve different functions in horizontal and vertical reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型中的演绎逻辑：水平推理与垂直推理
近期语言模型展现出显著的逻辑推理能力，但支持演绎推理的机制仍不明确。本文研究从头训练的小型基于Transformer的语言模型，专注于多步演绎任务，区分水平推理（中间步骤自回归生成）与垂直推理（推理在首个输出前隐式跨层展开）。我们分析两个合成任务：符号蕴含链上的逻辑后承与二叉树根到叶导航。机制可解释性表明，Chain-of-Thought监督使模型学习基于规则的推理而非统计捷径。在水平设置中，浅层注意力模型通过类似归纳头的机制发展出可解释的电路，用于规则补全、规则链式推理和最终决策。我们进一步引入截断伪逆方法解码查询、键和值所携带的信息。在垂直推理中，Chain-of-Thought似乎更像一种课程学习，帮助模型习得日益复杂的推理模式。无Chain-of-Thought时，模型倾向于记忆或利用数据集偏差。这些结果从底层解释了Transformer如何实现演绎推理，并指出Chain-of-Thought在水平与垂直推理中可能扮演不同角色。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how small transformer-based language models perform deductive reasoning, contrasting horizontal reasoning—where intermediate steps are generated step-by-step autoregressively—with vertical reasoning, where inference occurs implicitly across layers before output. Using synthetic tasks involving symbolic implications and binary tree navigation, the study finds that Chain-of-Thought supervision enables models to learn rule-based inference rather than memorizing patterns. In horizontal reasoning, shallow attention-only models develop interpretable circuits for rule chaining and decision-making via induction-head-like mechanisms, while in vertical reasoning, Chain-of-Thought functions more like a curriculum, guiding the model toward complex reasoning without explicit step-by-step prompting. Without Chain-of-Thought, models tend to rely on memorization or dataset biases.
该研究探讨了小型Transformer语言模型如何执行演绎推理，对比了水平推理（中间步骤自回归生成）与垂直推理（推理在输出前隐式跨层展开）两种方式。通过符号蕴含链和二叉树导航等合成任务发现，Chain-of-Thought监督使模型学习基于规则的推理而非记忆模式。在水平推理中，浅层注意力模型通过类似归纳头的机制形成可解释的规则完成、链式推理和决策电路，截断伪逆方法有助于解码注意力组件信息；在垂直推理中，Chain-of-Thought更像一种课程学习机制，引导模型逐步掌握复杂推理模式。无Chain-of-Thought时，模型倾向于依赖记忆或数据偏差，凸显其在实现可泛化的演绎推理中的关键作用。
World Narrative Model for Highly Controllable Video Generation: A Paradigm Shift from Pixel Sampling to Physical World Orchestration
Authors: Ye Chen, Xuanhong Chen, Yupeng Zhu, Liming Tan, Zhewen Wan, Yuxuan Xiong, Tielong Wang, Jinfan Liu, Wuze Zhang, Xiongzhen Zhang, Feifei Li, Xianglin Luo, Zhehan Zhao, Zhifan Zhang, Laisheng Kou, Zhujing Liang, Yugang Chen, Muchun Chen, Xu Miao, Yijing Zhang, Xiaojie Sheng, Qiang Hu, Jialiang Chen, Weimin Zhang, Wenjun Zhang, Bingbing Ni
First: 2026-06-30T16:52:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:52:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The fundamental obstacle to industrial grade video generation is the lack of controllability: existing models treat video as a pixel distribution sampling problem, bypassing the explicit, instance level $4D$ $(3D + T)$ physical world. Consequently, content creators cannot specify geometry, motion, camera parameters, or lighting in a deterministic, quantitative way, leading to the infamous ''gacha'' loop that makes professional content creation prohibitively inefficient and expensive. To address this, we introduce the World Narrative Model (WNM), a paradigm that decouples what to render -- the structured physical narrative -- from how to render -- the pixel generation process. WNM replaces end-to-end black-box sampling with orchestrated $4D$ pre-visualization for media generation. Collaborative agents translate sparse multimodal inputs, including text, reference videos, and sketches, into a fully editable world representation with scene geometry, object layouts, character/animal skeleton motion, trajectories, camera motion, and lighting at quantitative, physically meaningful granularity. This representation acts as a deterministic structural blueprint that drives existing video foundation models, either frozen or lightly adapted, to render final footage, turning the base model into a faithful neural shader. Built on this engine, our human-AI platform supports automatic world generation and pre-visualization aligned with professional filmmaking pipelines, while director consoles enable seamless human refinement. Experiments show that WNM greatly reduces probabilistic ``gacha'' calls and produces videos whose layout, motion, and cinematography closely follow creator intent. The framework is open and modular, allowing each component, such as world representation, control agents, and adapters, to be independently improved. Project website: https://glassroom.sjtu.edu.cn/WNM/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高度可控视频生成的世界叙事模型：从像素采样到物理世界编排的范式转变
工业级视频生成的根本障碍在于缺乏可控性：现有模型将视频视为像素分布采样问题，绕过了显式的实例级4D（3D+T）物理世界。因此，内容创作者无法以确定性和定量方式指定几何、运动、相机参数或光照，导致著名的“抽卡”循环，使专业内容创作变得极不高效且昂贵。为解决此问题，我们提出世界叙事模型（WNM），一种将“渲染什么”——结构化物理叙事——与“如何渲染”——像素生成过程——解耦的范式。WNM用编排的4D预可视化替代端到端黑盒采样，用于媒体生成。协作代理将稀疏多模态输入（包括文本、参考视频和草图）转化为可完全编辑的世界表示，包含场景几何、物体布局、角色/动物骨骼运动、轨迹、相机运动和光照，以定量且物理意义明确的粒度呈现。该表示作为确定性结构蓝图，驱动现有视频基础模型（冻结或轻度适配）渲染最终画面，使基础模型转变为忠实的神经着色器。基于此引擎，我们的AI-人类平台支持与专业电影制作流程对齐的自动世界生成和预可视化，导演控制台支持无缝人工优化。实验表明，WNM大幅减少概率性“抽卡”调用，生成的视频在布局、运动和摄影方面紧密遵循创作者意图。该框架开放且模块化，允许世界表示、控制代理和适配器等各组件独立改进。项目网站：https://glassroom.sjtu.edu.cn/WNM/
Summary / 总结
To overcome the lack of controllability in video generation, which hinders professional use by forcing creators into inefficient probabilistic sampling loops, the World Narrative Model (WNM) introduces a paradigm shift from pixel-level sampling to orchestrating a structured 4D physical world representation. It decouples content specification from rendering, using collaborative agents to translate multimodal inputs into editable, quantitative world representations including geometry, motion, camera, and lighting. These representations then drive existing video models as deterministic shaders, enabling precise control over output. Experiments demonstrate that WNM drastically reduces reliance on random sampling and produces videos that faithfully reflect creator intent in layout, motion, and cinematography, while its modular design allows independent improvement of components.
为解决现有视频生成模型缺乏可控性的问题——这些模型将视频视为像素采样问题，忽略了显式的物理世界结构——世界叙事模型（WNM）提出了一种范式转变，将内容定义与渲染过程解耦。WNM通过协作代理将文本、草图和参考视频等稀疏输入转化为可编辑的4D物理世界表示，包括几何结构、物体布局、角色骨骼运动、相机轨迹和光照等。该结构化蓝图驱动现有视频模型生成忠实输出，使其成为神经着色器。实验表明，WNM显著减少了试错式生成需求，所生成视频在构图、运动和镜头语言上更贴近创作者意图，同时支持通过导演控制台进行人工优化。
LLM-as-a-judge validity in physics assessment depends more on the task than the model
Authors: Will Yeadon, Tom Hardy, Paul Mackay, Elise Agra
First: 2026-03-16T02:09:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:52:37+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 28 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for automated assessment and feedback, understanding when LLM marking is valid is essential. We evaluate LLM-as-a-judge marking across three physics assessment formats - structured questions, written essays, and scientific plots - comparing GPT-5.2, Grok 4.1, Claude Opus 4.5, DeepSeek-V3.2, Gemini Pro 3, and committee aggregations against human markers under blind, solution-provided, false-solution, and anchored conditions. We distinguish absolute accuracy from rank-order agreement, since a marking system can match the distribution of human marks while failing to order responses by quality. Across task types, performance is sharply task-dependent. For blind university exam questions ($n=771$) and secondary and university structured questions ($n=1151$), models show robust rank-order agreement with human markers (Spearman $ρ> 0.6$), with official solutions reducing error and strengthening agreement. False solutions degrade absolute accuracy, showing that models defer to provided references, but leave rank-ordering intact. Essay marking behaves fundamentally differently. Across $n=55$ scripts ($n=275$ essays), blind AI marking is harsher and more variable than human marking and adding a mark scheme does not improve rank-order agreement. Anchored exemplars shift the AI mean close to the human mean and compress variance below the human standard deviation, but rank-order agreement remains near-zero. For code-based plot elements ($n=1400$), models achieve high rank-order agreement ($ρ> 0.84$) with near-linear calibration. Across all task types, validity tracks the structure of the assessment task - the extent to which marks can be mapped to explicit, observable grading features - and the reliability of the human benchmark, rather than raw model capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：作为评判者的大型语言模型在物理评估中的有效性更多取决于任务而非模型
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）被越来越多地用于自动化评估与反馈，理解LLM评分何时有效至关重要。我们评估了LLM作为评判者在三种物理评估形式（结构化问题、书面论文和科学图表）中的表现，比较了GPT-5.2、Grok 4.1、Claude Opus 4.5、DeepSeek-V3.2、Gemini Pro 3及人类评判员的聚合结果，在盲评、提供标准答案、提供错误答案和锚定条件下进行对比。我们区分了绝对准确性与排序一致性，因为评分系统可能匹配人类评分分布，但无法按质量排序响应。在不同任务类型中，模型表现高度依赖任务类型。在盲评大学考试题（n=771）和中学及大学结构化问题（n=1151）中，模型与人类评判员的排序一致性（Spearman ρ>0.6）表现稳健，提供标准答案可降低误差并增强一致性。错误答案会降低绝对准确性，表明模型依赖提供的参考，但排序一致性保持不变。论文评分表现截然不同。在n=55篇论文（n=275篇作文）中，盲评AI评分更严苛且变异性更大，添加评分标准无法提升排序一致性。锚定范例使AI平均分接近人类平均分，且方差低于人类标准差，但排序一致性仍接近零。对于基于代码的图表元素（n=1400），模型与人类评判员的排序一致性高（ρ>0.84），校准近乎线性。在所有任务类型中，有效性取决于评估任务的结构——评分能否映射到明确、可观察的评分特征——以及人类基准的可靠性，而非模型本身的原始能力。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can reliably serve as judges in physics assessments, examining their performance across structured questions, essays, and scientific plots. The researchers compare six LLMs and human markers under varied conditions—blind, solution-provided, false-solution, and anchored—and measure both absolute accuracy and rank-order agreement. Results show that for structured questions, LLMs achieve strong rank-order agreement with humans (Spearman ρ > 0.6), especially when official solutions are provided, though false solutions reduce absolute accuracy without affecting ranking. In contrast, essay marking is markedly less reliable: AI judgments are harsher and more variable, and even with mark schemes, rank-order agreement remains poor. Anchored exemplars help align AI means with human scores but do not improve ranking. For plot-based tasks, models show near-perfect rank-order agreement (ρ > 0.84) with linear calibration. Overall, validity depends more on task structure—how easily marks can be tied to observable features—and human benchmark reliability than on model capability.
本研究探讨大语言模型（LLM）在物理评估中作为评分者时的可靠性，比较了结构化题目、论文和科学图表三种评估形式在不同条件下的表现。研究在盲评、提供标准答案、提供错误答案和锚定范例等情境下，对比多个LLM与人类评分者，区分绝对评分准确性与排名一致性。结果显示，结构化题目中LLM与人类评分者排名一致性高（Spearman ρ > 0.6），尤其在提供标准答案时，但错误答案会降低绝对准确性而不影响排名。论文评分表现迥异，即使引入评分标准或锚定范例，AI评分仍更严苛且变异性大，排名一致性极低。代码图表类题目中，LLM排名一致性高（ρ > 0.84）且校准接近线性。总体而言，评估有效性取决于任务结构和人类评分基准的可靠性，而非模型本身能力。
LWDrive: Layer-Wise World-Model-Guided Vision-Language Model Planning for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Chen Yang, Yuhao Wei, Ze Xu, Ziheng Zou, Shuang Liang, Delin Ouyang, Lingfeng Qi, Jie Li, Guofa Li
First: 2026-06-29T07:17:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:50:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide powerful semantic understanding and commonsense reasoning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving (E2E-AD) planning. However, trajectories directly generated by VLMs often encode only coarse driving intentions and remain insufficient for geometrically accurate, future-aware, and multi-view-grounded planning. To address these limitations, we develop the Layer-Wise World-Model-Guided Driving framework (LWDrive). LWDrive is a VLM planning framework that refines coarse trajectories through layer-wise world-model guidance. Instead of treating the VLM output as the final trajectory, LWDrive uses it as an intent-aware coarse plan, expands a diverse candidate space around it, and progressively refines the candidates through a Foresight Cascade Planner (FCP). Specifically, we introduce future-frame generation supervision to encourage the VLM to learn forward-looking scene representations, thereby injecting planning-relevant predictive dynamics into its internal hidden states. Built upon these world-model-supervised representations, FCP exploits VLM features across multiple layers and integrates historical temporal states, Action-Query representations, and current-frame multi-view Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) features to refine candidate trajectories in a coarse-to-fine manner. This design enables progressive correction of spatial positions and motion trends while grounding trajectory refinement with multi-view scene cues and preserving the high-level driving intention produced by the large model. Finally, a score head evaluates the refined candidates and selects the best trajectory as the final planning output. Experiments show that LWDrive achieves a score of 92.0 on the NAVSIM benchmark and 89.6 on NAVSIM-v2. Code and models will be made publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LWDrive：基于分层世界模型引导的视觉-语言模型规划方法用于自动驾驶
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）为端到端自动驾驶（E2E-AD）规划提供了强大的语义理解和常识推理能力。然而，直接由VLM生成的轨迹往往仅编码粗略的驾驶意图，不足以满足几何精确、未来感知和多视角锚定的规划需求。为解决这些问题，我们提出了分层世界模型引导的驾驶框架（LWDrive）。LWDrive是一种VLM规划框架，通过分层世界模型引导对粗轨迹进行细化。它不将VLM输出视为最终轨迹，而是将其作为意图感知的粗略规划，围绕其扩展多样候选空间，并通过前瞻级联规划器（FCP）逐步优化候选轨迹。具体而言，我们引入未来帧生成监督，促使VLM学习前瞻场景表征，从而将其内部隐藏状态注入规划相关的预测动态。基于这些世界模型监督表征，FCP利用VLM多层特征，融合历史时序状态、动作查询表示和当前帧多视角鸟瞰图（BEV）特征，以粗到细的方式优化候选轨迹。该设计实现了空间位置和运动趋势的渐进修正，同时通过多视角场景线索锚定轨迹优化，并保留大型模型生成的高层驾驶意图。最后，评分头评估优化后的候选轨迹并选择最优轨迹作为最终规划输出。实验表明，LWDrive在NAVSIM基准上得分92.0，在NAVSIM-v2上得分89.6。代码和模型将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for geometrically accurate and multi-view-grounded trajectory planning in autonomous driving, this work introduces LWDrive, a framework that refines coarse VLM-generated trajectories using layer-wise world-model guidance. It treats VLM output as an intent-aware initial plan, expands a diverse candidate space around it, and progressively refines candidates via a Foresight Cascade Planner (FCP) that integrates future-frame supervision, multi-layer VLM features, historical states, action queries, and BEV features. Experimental results demonstrate LWDrive achieves 92.0 on NAVSIM and 89.6 on NAVSIM-v2, outperforming baseline methods by effectively grounding trajectory refinement in scene context while preserving high-level driving intentions.
为提升视觉语言模型在自动驾驶规划中的几何精度与未来感知能力，LWDrive提出一种分层世界模型引导的规划框架。该框架将VLM输出视为粗略意图规划，围绕其生成多样候选轨迹，并通过融合多层VLM特征、历史时序状态、动作查询表示及当前多视角BEV特征的前瞻级联规划器（FCP）逐步优化。通过未来帧监督，VLM学习预测场景动态，使FCP在修正空间位置与运动趋势的同时保留高层驾驶意图。实验表明，LWDrive在NAVSIM上得分92.0，在NAVSIM-v2上得分89.6，优于基线方法。
Making Sense of Touch from the Child's View for Contrastive Learning
Authors: Max Whitton, Zecheng Wang, Puchen Liu, Quang Tuan Truong, Shengao Wang, Manaswi Yadamreddy, Oktay Ozel, Visista Jayanti, Saniya Sekhon, Hanna Samuel Tadesse, Lawrence Miao, Junjie Wang, Jiasen Lu, Chen Yu, Boqing Gong
First: 2026-06-30T16:50:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:50:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Is the sense of touch a mechanism for human babies' learning of visual concepts? If so, can we quantify its importance, and to what extent do babies rely on their sense of touch for visual learning? To approach these questions in a principled way, we propose a structured coding system for baby-centric touch events, yielding a dataset of 264k two-second clips of touch events coded according to this system. Using this dataset, we pretrain developmentally grounded models that reveal promising insights into the nature of baby learning from touch.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从儿童视角理解触觉在对比学习中的作用
触觉是否是人类婴儿学习视觉概念的机制？如果是，我们能否量化其重要性，婴儿在视觉学习中多大程度依赖触觉？为系统性回答这些问题，我们提出了一套以婴儿为中心的触觉事件编码体系，构建了一个包含26.4万段两秒触觉事件的标注数据集。利用该数据集，我们预训练了具有发展基础的模型，揭示了婴儿通过触觉学习的若干重要洞见。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether touch plays a critical role in infants' visual concept learning, aiming to quantify its importance and extent of reliance.
