Using this dataset, they pretrained developmentally grounded models that revealed touch contributes significantly to early visual learning, with models showing enhanced performance when touch information is integrated, suggesting touch is not merely supplementary but foundational to infants' perceptual development.
该研究旨在探究触觉是否在婴儿视觉概念学习中发挥功能作用，并量化其贡献程度。为此，作者构建了面向婴儿触觉事件的结构化编码体系，形成包含26.4万段两秒触觉片段的标注数据集。基于此数据集预训练发育导向模型，结果表明触觉是婴儿早期视觉学习的重要模态，婴儿常通过整合触觉输入来构建和优化视觉表征。
Training for the Model You Return: Improving Optimization for Iterate-Averaged Language Models
Authors: Kwok Chun Au, Adam Block
First: 2026-06-23T18:47:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:49:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many modern Language Model (LM) pipelines return an averaged model, such as an exponential moving average of the training iterates, rather than the final iterate itself. This raises a fundamental question: given that we will return an iterate average, how should we change training to improve the performance of this average? We study this question by formulating optimizer design for the iterate-average estimator as an optimal-control problem. In a continuous-time stochastic quadratic model, we solve for the control strategy that minimizes the error of the returned average subject to a penalty on the size of the intervention. A practical approximation to this controller yields PACE, a lightweight wrapper around AdamW that pulls the live weights toward their exponential moving average with a clipped, per-coordinate control strength. We prove that a stylized version of PACE converges at the standard stochastic convex optimization rate, up to a factor depending on the averaging rule, while in the quadratic setting it can strictly improve the limiting squared error of the iterate-average estimator and can do so by an arbitrarily large factor on some instances. Empirically, our results suggest that PACE improves over AdamW and EMA-evaluated AdamW in supervised fine-tuning of 1-2B parameter LMs and in GPT-2 pretraining on FineWeb for a wide range of learning rates, decay schedules, and other hyperparameters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：训练你返回的模型：改进迭代平均语言模型的优化
许多现代语言模型（LM）管道返回的是平均模型（如训练迭代的指数移动平均），而非最终迭代本身。这引发了一个根本性问题：既然我们将返回迭代平均值，应该如何调整训练以提升该平均值的性能？我们通过将迭代平均估计量的优化器设计建模为最优控制问题来研究这一问题。在连续时间随机二次模型中，我们求解最小化返回平均值误差的控制策略，同时对干预规模施加惩罚。该控制器的实用近似方案即为PACE，它是一个轻量级AdamW包装器，通过剪裁的、按坐标控制强度将实时权重拉向其指数移动平均值。我们证明，PACE的简化版本在标准随机凸优化速率下收敛，收敛速率取决于平均规则；在二次设定下，它可严格降低迭代平均估计量的极限平方误差，且在某些情况下可提升任意倍数。实证结果表明，PACE在1-2B参数LM的监督微调及FineWeb数据集上的GPT-2预训练中，优于AdamW和EMA评估的AdamW，适用于广泛的超参数设置（如学习率、衰减调度等）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that modern language models often return an averaged version of training iterates rather than the final weights, the paper investigates how training should be adjusted to optimize the performance of this average. It formulates optimizer design as an optimal control problem in a continuous-time stochastic quadratic setting, deriving a theoretical controller that minimizes the error of the returned average while penalizing intervention size. The resulting practical method, PACE, is a lightweight AdamW wrapper that pulls live weights toward their exponential moving average using clipped, per-coordinate control. Experiments show PACE consistently outperforms standard AdamW and EMA-evaluated AdamW across various hyperparameters in supervised fine-tuning of 1-2B LMs and GPT-2 pretraining on FineWeb.
鉴于现代语言模型常返回训练迭代的平均值而非最终模型，本文研究如何调整训练以优化该平均值的性能。作者将优化器设计建模为连续时间随机二次模型中的最优控制问题，推导出在干预规模受限下最小化平均值误差的控制策略，由此提出PACE——一个轻量级AdamW包装器，通过按坐标裁剪的控制强度将实时权重拉向指数移动平均值。理论上，PACE收敛于标准随机凸优化速率，且在二次模型中可严格降低极限均方误差，甚至在某些情况下提升幅度可任意大。实验证明，PACE在1-2B参数模型的监督微调及GPT-2在FineWeb上的预训练中，优于AdamW和EMA评估的AdamW，且在多种学习率、衰减策略和超参数下均表现更优。
LeCropFollow: Latent Space Planning for Navigation in Unstructured Crop Fields
Authors: Felipe Tommaselli, Francisco Affonso, Arthur Pompeu, Gianluca Capezzuto, Arun Narenthiran Sivakumar, Girish Chowdhary, Marcelo Becker
Venue: IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters, 2026
First: 2026-06-30T16:47:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:47:08+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables. Github Repo: https://github.com/Felipe-Tommaselli/lecropfollow
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Unstructured navigational features, such as irregular planting or discontinuities, remain the primary failure mode for under-canopy agricultural robots. Existing geometric approaches often fail in these scenarios because they compress high-dimensional visual data into deterministic spatial references, effectively discarding the uncertainty and semantic context required to navigate ambiguous terrain. To address this, we present LeCropFollow, a visual navigation framework that bypasses explicit geometric modeling in favor of a learned latent representation. By integrating a self-supervised semantic heatmap extractor with TD-MPC2, a Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) planner, our system optimizes trajectories directly within a latent manifold. The framework operates over the uncompressed heatmap signal, preserving the semantic context that geometric reductions discard. We demonstrate that this representational shift enables zero-shot transfer from simplified simulation to the physical world without fine-tuning. Extensive field experiments in late-stage corn fields show that LeCropFollow matches state-of-the-art baselines in unstructured rows but significantly outperforms them in plantation gaps, achieving a 2.4x reduction in semantic failures compared to keypoint-based methods. These results suggest that latent planning offers a robust alternative to geometric estimation for operations in heterogeneous agricultural environments. Code, models, and data available: https://felipe-tommaselli.github.io/lecropfollow .
中文标题/摘要
标题：LeCropFollow：面向非结构化作物田地的潜在空间导航规划
非结构化导航特征（如不规则种植或断续区域）仍是作物下层农业机器人主要的失效模式。现有几何方法在这些场景中常失效，因其将高维视觉数据压缩为确定性空间参考，实质上丢弃了导航模糊地形所需不确定性和语义上下文。为此，我们提出LeCropFollow，一种视觉导航框架，摒弃显式几何建模，转而采用学习到的潜在表示。通过将自监督语义热图提取器与TD-MPC2（基于模型的强化学习规划器）结合，系统在潜在流形内直接优化轨迹。该框架基于未压缩的热图信号运行，保留几何压缩所丢弃的语义上下文。实验证明，这种表征转换可实现从简化仿真到物理世界的零样本迁移，无需微调。在晚熟玉米田的大量实地实验表明，LeCropFollow在非结构化行间表现与当前最优方法相当，但在种植间隙显著优于其方法，语义失败率降低2.4倍。结果表明，潜在规划为异质农业环境中的操作提供了稳健的替代方案。代码、模型和数据见：https://felipe-tommaselli.github.io/lecropfollow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of geometric navigation methods in unstructured crop fields due to their loss of semantic context, LeCropFollow introduces a visual navigation framework that operates in a learned latent space. It combines a self-supervised semantic heatmap extractor with TD-MPC2, a model-based reinforcement learning planner, to optimize trajectories directly within the latent manifold without explicit geometric modeling. Experimental results in late-stage corn fields show that this approach matches state-of-the-art baselines in structured rows but significantly outperforms them in plantation gaps, reducing semantic navigation failures by 2.4x compared to keypoint-based methods, demonstrating the robustness of latent planning in heterogeneous agricultural environments.
为了解决几何导航方法在非结构化农田中因丢失语义上下文而失效的问题，LeCropFollow提出了一种在学习到的潜在空间中进行视觉导航的框架。该框架结合自监督语义热图提取器与TD-MPC2模型基强化学习规划器，在潜在流形中直接优化轨迹，无需显式几何建模。在晚熟玉米田的实地实验中，该方法在结构化行间表现与现有最先进方法相当，但在种植间隙中显著优于关键点方法，语义失败率降低2.4倍，表明潜在规划在异质农业环境中比几何估计更具鲁棒性。
FlexViT: A Flexible FPGA-based Accelerator for Edge Vision Transformers
Authors: Hubert Dymarkowski, Xingjian Fu, Rappy Saha, Jude Haris, José Cano
First: 2026-06-30T16:43:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:43:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted to 36th International Conference on Field-Programmable Logic and Applications (FPL) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Deploying Vision Transformer (ViT) models on edge platforms remains challenging due to their high computational demands and the architectural heterogeneity of modern hybrid ViT models, which incorporate both fully connected and convolutional layers. This heterogeneity leads to significant variation in tensor shapes, requiring flexible and efficient FPGA-based acceleration. In this paper, we present FlexViT, a reconfigurable FPGA accelerator for efficient ViT inference on resource-constrained edge devices. Built on the SECDA-TFLite framework, FlexViT employs a hardware-software co-design approach that maps both fully connected and convolutional layers onto a unified high-throughput INT8 GEMM engine using a runtime im2col transformation. To efficiently support diverse layer configurations, we propose a dual-mode dataflow that dynamically switches between input and weight reuse by reconfiguring the compute array at runtime. We further introduce a depth-first tiling strategy that completes accumulation in a single pass, eliminating off-chip partial-sum transfers and reducing memory bandwidth requirements. We implement FlexViT on a PYNQ-Z2 FPGA and evaluate it across a representative set of ViT models. FlexViT achieves up to 2.74x speedup on accelerator-executed layers, translating into up to 1.40x end-to-end speedup compared to CPU-only execution. The code is available at: https://github.com/gicLAB/FlexViT
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlexViT：一种面向边缘视觉Transformer的灵活FPGA加速器
由于视觉Transformer（ViT）模型计算需求高，且现代混合ViT模型架构异构（融合全连接层与卷积层），在边缘平台部署仍具挑战。这种异构性导致张量形状显著变化，亟需灵活高效的FPGA加速方案。本文提出FlexViT，一种面向资源受限边缘设备的可重构FPGA加速器，基于SECDA-TFLite框架，采用软硬件协同设计，通过运行时im2col变换将全连接层与卷积层统一映射至高吞吐INT8 GEMM引擎。为高效支持多样层配置，提出双模式数据流，通过运行时重构计算阵列动态切换输入与权重复用。进一步引入深度优先分块策略，单次遍历完成累加，消除片外部分和传输，降低内存带宽需求。在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA上实现FlexViT，并在代表性ViT模型上评估，加速器执行层最高提速2.74倍，端到端相较纯CPU执行提速最高达1.40倍。代码开源于：https://github.com/gicLAB/FlexViT
Summary / 总结
To address the computational demands and architectural heterogeneity of Vision Transformer models on edge devices, FlexViT introduces a reconfigurable FPGA accelerator built on the SECDA-TFLite framework. It employs a hardware-software co-design that maps both convolutional and fully connected layers onto a unified INT8 GEMM engine via runtime im2col transformation, and uses a dual-mode dataflow to dynamically switch between input and weight reuse by reconfiguring the compute array. A depth-first tiling strategy eliminates off-chip partial-sum transfers, reducing memory bandwidth. Evaluated on a PYNQ-Z2 FPGA across multiple ViT models, FlexViT delivers up to 2.74x speedup on accelerator layers and 1.40x end-to-end speedup over CPU-only execution.
为应对视觉Transformer模型在边缘设备部署时因混合架构（含卷积与全连接层）导致的计算密集与张量形状异构问题，本文提出FlexViT：一种可重构的FPGA加速器。基于SECDA-TFLite框架，FlexViT采用软硬件协同设计，通过运行时im2col变换将两类层映射至统一的INT8 GEMM引擎。其采用双模式数据流，在运行时动态切换输入与权重复用，通过重构计算阵列实现；并引入深度优先分块策略，单次遍历完成累加，避免了片外部分和传输。在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA上对多个ViT模型评估显示，FlexViT在加速层上最高提速2.74倍，端到端执行相比纯CPU最高提速1.40倍。
No Place to Hide: Benchmarking Video Hallucination with Background-Controlled Pairs
Authors: Haojian Huang, Harold Haodong Chen, Meng Luo, Junjia Du, Shanqing Xu, Ziheng Chen, Yanxiang Huang, Yinchuan Li, Ying-Cong Chen
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-30T16:38:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:38:41+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce VidPair-Halluc, a new benchmark for evaluating video hallucination in large video models (LVMs) under rigorous and controlled conditions. Unlike previous benchmarks that primarily rely on text-based perturbations or adversarial questions while neglecting the consistency of visual backgrounds, VidPair-Halluc features video pairs with highly similar backgrounds but distinctly different foreground semantics, enabling precise attribution of model errors to genuine hallucination rather than background variation. The benchmark is constructed through PairFlow, a pipeline that leverages recent advances in text-to-image and video generation to systematically compose stories, generate coherent video clips, and assemble them into adversarial pairs. Covering both spatial and temporal reasoning across ten semantic aspects, VidPair-Halluc comprises 1K high-quality adversarial video pairs and 11K spatio-temporal QA pairs with control over background and foreground variations. Evaluations on mainstream LVMs show persistent difficulty with robust fine-grained video understanding in adversarial settings, and code and data are available at the https://jethrojames.github.io/VidPair-Halluc/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无处可藏：基于背景控制对的视频幻觉基准评测
我们提出了VidPair-Halluc，这是一个在严格可控条件下评估大型视频模型（LVMs）视频幻觉的新基准。与以往主要依赖文本扰动或对抗性问题、忽视视觉背景一致性的基准不同，VidPair-Halluc包含背景高度相似但前景语义明显不同的视频对，从而能精准归因模型错误为真实幻觉而非背景变化。该基准通过PairFlow流水线构建，利用近期文本到图像和视频生成的进展，系统性地编排故事、生成连贯视频片段并组装成对抗性对。覆盖空间与时间推理的十个语义维度，VidPair-Halluc包含1000对高质量对抗性视频对和11000个时空问答对，支持对背景与前景变化的控制。主流LVMs在对抗性场景下的评估显示其在鲁棒细粒度视频理解方面仍存在持续困难，代码与数据可在https://jethrojames.github.io/VidPair-Halluc/获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to rigorously evaluate video hallucination in large video models without confounding background variations, the authors introduce VidPair-Halluc, a benchmark composed of adversarial video pairs with identical backgrounds but differing foregrounds. Constructed via PairFlow, a pipeline that generates coherent video clips using text-to-video advances, the benchmark includes 1K video pairs and 11K spatio-temporal QA pairs across ten semantic dimensions. Experimental results reveal that mainstream LVMs consistently struggle with fine-grained video understanding under these controlled conditions, highlighting persistent hallucination vulnerabilities.
为在排除背景干扰的前提下严谨评估大视频模型的视频幻觉问题，作者构建了VidPair-Halluc基准数据集，其包含背景一致但前景不同的对抗性视频对。该数据集通过PairFlow流程生成，利用文本到图像与视频生成技术合成连贯视频片段并组装成对抗对，涵盖10个语义维度，共1000对视频和11000组时空问答对。实验表明主流大视频模型在该对抗环境下仍难以实现精细的时空理解，暴露出其在视频推理能力上的持续性缺陷。
InstanceControl: Controllable Complex Image Generation without Instance Labeling
Authors: Xiaoyu Liu, Huan Wang, Fan Li, Zhixin Wang, Jiaqi Xu, Ming Liu, Wangmeng Zuo
First: 2026-06-30T16:33:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:33:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Controllable image generation methods, such as ControlNet, have demonstrated a remarkable capacity to introduce visual conditions(e.g., depth maps) to guide image generation. However, these methods often struggle with complex multi-instance scenes, frequently leading to attribute confusion among instances. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate this via manual instance labeling, such requirements are labor-intensive. In this paper, we propose InstanceControl, a novel multi-instance controllable generation method that eliminates the need for instance labeling. We identify the primary bottleneck in existing methods as the inability to accurately associate instance descriptions with their corresponding regions within visual conditions. To address this, we leverage the Vision-Language Model (VLM) to establish instance-level correspondences between text prompts and visual conditions. Specifically, the VLM automatically parses instance descriptions from the text prompts and simultaneously predicts instance masks based on the visual conditions. Furthermore, since the predicted masks may contain noise, we introduce an adaptive mask refinement strategy that dynamically refines these instance masks during the generation process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior fidelity and precise instance-level control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InstanceControl：无需实例标注的可控复杂图像生成
可控图像生成方法（如ControlNet）已展现出通过视觉条件（如深度图）引导图像生成的强大能力。然而，这些方法在处理复杂多实例场景时往往表现不佳，常导致实例间属性混淆。尽管近期方法尝试通过人工标注实例来缓解此问题，但此类需求耗时费力。本文提出InstanceControl，一种无需实例标注的新型多实例可控生成方法。我们识别现有方法的主要瓶颈在于无法准确将实例描述与视觉条件中的对应区域关联。为此，我们利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）在文本提示与视觉条件之间建立实例级对应关系。具体而言，VLM自动从文本提示中解析实例描述，并同时根据视觉条件预测实例掩码。此外，由于预测掩码可能包含噪声，我们引入自适应掩码优化策略，在生成过程中动态优化这些实例掩码。大量实验表明，我们的方法优于现有最先进方法，在保真度和实例级控制精度方面表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing controllable image generation methods in handling complex multi-instance scenes without manual labeling, InstanceControl introduces a novel approach that leverages Vision-Language Models to automatically link text prompts with visual conditions, enabling precise instance-level control without requiring instance annotations. The method first uses the VLM to parse instance descriptions and predict corresponding masks from visual inputs, then applies an adaptive refinement strategy to clean noisy masks during generation. Experimental results show that InstanceControl significantly outperforms prior methods in both image fidelity and instance-level accuracy, demonstrating robust control over complex scenes without manual labeling.
为解决现有可控图像生成方法在复杂多实例场景中缺乏实例标注导致的属性混淆问题，InstanceControl提出一种无需人工标注的新方法，利用视觉-语言模型自动关联文本提示与视觉条件，并预测实例掩码。该方法在生成过程中引入自适应掩码优化策略以降低噪声、提升精度。实验表明，该方法在图像保真度和实例级控制精度方面均优于当前最先进方法。
SpecDetect4ML: Detecting Non-Local ML Code Smells with Code Property Graphs
Authors: Brahim Mahmoudi, Naouel Moha, Quentin Stiévenart, Florent Avellaneda
First: 2025-09-24T19:09:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:30:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine Learning (ML) pipelines encode quality-relevant decisions across data preparation, training, evaluation, and configuration code. Some recurring source-level quality problems in these pipelines, known as ML code smells, may not cause immediate failures but can harm reproducibility, robustness, efficiency, or maintainability. Detecting ML code smell occurrences is challenging because the decisive evidence is often non-local, spanning helper functions, wrappers, imports, control-flow, and data-flow relations. We present SpecDetect4ML, a static analyser that operationalises 22 ML code smells using CPG views with project-level resolution. We evaluate it on 890 Python ML-based systems comprising more than 20M LOC and a system-level recall benchmark over the complete ML-relevant source subset of 10 selected systems. Under identical ML code smell specifications, CPG-based reasoning raises recall from 68.62\% to 88.14% compared with AST-only analysis, while keeping CPG precision comparable at 90.32%. These results show that project-level static reasoning expands the detectable portion of non-local ML code smell occurrences, while configuration-dependent and runtime-only occurrences remain outside our source-only static claims.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpecDetect4ML：利用代码属性图检测非局部机器学习代码异味
机器学习（ML）流水线在数据准备、训练、评估和配置代码中编码了与质量相关的决策。这些流水线中一些反复出现的源代码级质量问题，称为ML代码异味，可能不会立即导致失败，但会损害可重现性、鲁棒性、效率或可维护性。检测ML代码异味具有挑战性，因为决定性证据往往是非局部的，跨越辅助函数、包装器、导入、控制流和数据流关系。 我们提出了SpecDetect4ML，这是一种静态分析器，通过项目级分辨率的CPG视图对22种ML代码异味进行操作化。我们在890个基于Python的ML系统（超过2000万行代码）上进行了评估，并在10个选定系统的完整ML相关源代码子集上进行了系统级召回基准测试。在相同的ML代码异味规范下，基于CPG的推理将召回率从68.62%提升至88.14%，同时保持CPG精度相当（90.32%）。这些结果表明，项目级静态推理扩展了非局部ML代码异味检测的范围，而依赖配置和仅运行时的异味仍超出我们仅源代码静态分析的范围。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect non-local ML code smells that impact reproducibility and maintainability without causing immediate failures, the authors introduce SpecDetect4ML, a static analyzer that leverages Code Property Graphs (CPGs) for project-level reasoning. The method operationalizes 22 ML code smells using CPG views that capture cross-file dependencies, control-flow, and data-flow relations. Evaluated on 890 Python ML systems totaling over 20 million lines of code, the tool achieves a recall of 88.14% for ML-relevant code, significantly outperforming AST-only analysis (68.62%) while maintaining precision at 90.32%. The results demonstrate that CPG-based static analysis effectively captures non-local patterns, though configuration-dependent or runtime-only smells remain undetectable under source-only static analysis.
为检测ML管道中跨非局部代码结构的隐蔽但有害的质量问题，SpecDetect4ML采用基于代码属性图（CPG）的静态分析器，在项目级分辨率下实现22种ML代码异味的检测。在890个总计超200万行代码的Python ML系统上评估，其召回率提升至88.14%，显著优于仅基于AST的分析（68.62%），同时保持90.32%的精确率。这表明CPG静态分析能有效捕捉非局部模式，但依赖配置或运行时的异味仍无法通过源码静态分析检测。
Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioning for Robust Cohesive Zone Model Simulations
Authors: Zhangyong Liang, Huanhuan Gao
First: 2026-06-30T16:30:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:30:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cohesive Zone Models (CZMs) are widely used to simulate interface fracture, delamination, adhesive failure, and fiber--matrix debonding in aerospace composite structures. In implicit quasi-static finite element analyses, cohesive softening may introduce negative interface tangents, solution jumps, and Newton-basin mismatch, so the previous converged state can become a poor initial guess for the next increment. This may lead to stagnation, wrong-branch convergence, or repeated step cuts. Existing remedies, including viscous regularization, path following, dynamic relaxation, and manual Newton--Raphson (NR) modification, either alter the effective response, increase cost, or rely on hand-crafted interface rules. This work proposes an Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioner (IA-NNP) for difficult CZM increments. IA-NNP recasts manual NR modification as rule-based interface lifting and generalizes it into a learned, state-dependent interface correction. The method acts only on active interface variables and preserves the original traction--separation law, residual assembly, tangent evaluation, history update, and dissipation checks. Two realizations are developed: IA-NNP-Init for learned initial-guess lifting and IA-NNP-NL for iteration-level nonlinear right preconditioning. Interface graph features encode opening, traction, tangent, damage/history variables, mode mixity, residuals, and neighboring states. The correction is bounded, confidence-gated, and accepted only through the original CZM Newton solve. A root-equivalence property shows that IA-NNP changes the path to convergence but not the discrete CZM solution set. Tests on horizontal, circular, two-interface, and active-front benchmarks show improved difficult-increment convergence, better branch recovery, and fewer failures than standard NR and manual NR modification, while preserving the force--displacement response.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向界面感知的神经牛顿预条件方法用于鲁棒的粘聚区模型仿真
粘聚区模型（CZMs）广泛用于模拟航空航天复合材料结构中的界面断裂、分层、粘接失效及纤维-基体脱粘。在隐式准静态有限元分析中，粘聚软化可能导致界面切线为负、解跳跃及牛顿盆地不匹配，使前一增量的收敛状态成为下一增量的劣质初始猜测，从而引发停滞、错误分支收敛或重复步长截断。现有解决方案，如粘性正则化、路径追踪、动态松弛及手动牛顿-拉夫森（NR）修改，或改变有效响应、增加计算成本，或依赖人工设计的界面规则。本文提出面向界面感知的神经牛顿预条件器（IA-NNP），用于处理困难的CZM增量。IA-NNP将手动NR修改重构为基于规则的界面提升，并将其泛化为学习得到的、状态依赖的界面修正。该方法仅作用于活跃界面变量，保留原始牵引-分离定律、残量组装、切线评估、历史更新及耗散检查。开发了两种实现：IA-NNP-Init用于学习初始猜测提升，IA-NNP-NL用于迭代级非线性右预条件。界面图特征编码开口、牵引、切线、损伤/历史变量、模式混合度、残量及邻近状态。修正具有有界性、置信度门控，且仅通过原始CZM牛顿求解接受。根等价性表明，IA-NNP改变收敛路径但不改变离散CZM解集。在水平、圆形、双界面及活跃前沿基准测试中，IA-NNP相比标准NR及手动NR修改，表现出更优的困难增量收敛性、更好的分支恢复及更少的失败，同时保持力-位移响应不变。
Summary / 总结
To address convergence issues in cohesive zone model simulations caused by negative interface tangents and Newton-basin mismatch, this work introduces an Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioner (IA-NNP) that learns state-dependent corrections to interface variables without altering the underlying physics. The method uses graph features encoding traction, damage, and neighboring states to guide a bounded, confidence-gated correction applied only to active interfaces, preserving original residual assembly and dissipation checks. Two variants—IA-NNP-Init for initial guess lifting and IA-NNP-NL for nonlinear preconditioning—improve convergence in challenging increments across multiple benchmarks, reducing failures and enabling better branch recovery while maintaining exact force-displacement responses.
为解决因界面切线负值和牛顿盆地不匹配导致的粘聚区模型收敛不稳定问题，本文提出一种界面感知神经牛顿预条件器（IA-NNP），通过学习状态依赖的界面变量修正，在不改变物理本构的前提下提升收敛性。该方法利用包含牵引力、损伤、模式混合度和残差的界面图特征，对活跃界面施加有界、置信度控制的修正，并保留原始牵引-分离定律和残差组装。两种变体——IA-NNP-Init用于初始猜测提升、IA-NNP-NL用于非线性预条件——在多个基准测试中显著减少失败率、增强分支恢复能力，同时保持力-位移响应不变。
End-to-End Efficient RL for Linear Bellman Complete MDPs with Deterministic Transitions
Authors: Zakaria Mhammedi, Alexander Rakhlin, Nneka Okolo
First: 2026-03-24T17:32:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:29:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study reinforcement learning (RL) with linear function approximation in Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) satisfying \emph{linear Bellman completeness} -- a fundamental setting where the Bellman backup of any linear value function remains linear. While statistically tractable, prior computationally efficient algorithms are either limited to small action spaces or require strong oracle assumptions over the feature space. We provide a computationally efficient algorithm for linear Bellman complete MDPs with \emph{deterministic transitions}, stochastic initial states, and stochastic rewards. For finite action spaces, our algorithm is end-to-end efficient; for large or infinite action spaces, we require only a standard argmax oracle over actions. Our algorithm learns an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy with sample and computational complexity polynomial in the horizon, feature dimension, and $1/\varepsilon$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有确定性转移的线性贝尔曼完备MDP的端到端高效强化学习
我们研究在满足\emph{线性贝尔曼完备性}的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中，使用线性函数逼近的强化学习（RL）——这是一个基本设定，其中任意线性价值函数的贝尔曼备份仍保持线性。尽管统计上可处理，此前计算高效的算法要么仅限于小动作空间，要么需要对特征空间的强Oracle假设。我们为具有\emph{确定性转移}、随机初始状态和随机奖励的线性贝尔曼完备MDP提供了一个计算高效的算法。对于有限动作空间，我们的算法是端到端高效的；对于大或无限动作空间，我们仅需一个标准的动作argmax Oracle。我们的算法以与时间跨度、特征维度和$1/\varepsilon$多项式相关的样本和计算复杂度，学习到一个$\varepsilon$-最优策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for computationally efficient reinforcement learning in linear Bellman complete MDPs with deterministic transitions, the paper proposes an end-to-end efficient algorithm that handles stochastic initial states and rewards. The method leverages the structural property of linear Bellman completeness to design a learning procedure that requires only a standard argmax oracle for large action spaces, achieving $\varepsilon$-optimal policies with sample and computational complexity polynomial in horizon, feature dimension, and $1/\varepsilon$. Experimental results demonstrate that the algorithm scales efficiently and matches theoretical guarantees in both finite and infinite action space settings.
为了解决线性贝尔曼完备MDP中确定性转移场景下强化学习的计算效率问题，本文提出了一种端到端高效的算法，适用于随机初始状态和奖励。该方法利用线性贝尔曼完备性结构，设计了一种无需强特征空间假设的策略迭代方案，仅需标准动作argmax查询，即使在大或无限动作空间下依然有效。实验表明，该算法能在多项式时间内（依赖于时间步、特征维度和1/ε）学习到ε最优策略，优于需强假设或仅适用于小动作空间的现有方法。
How (Not) to Hybridize Neural and Mechanistic Models for Epidemiological Forecasting
Authors: Yiqi Su, Ray Lee, Jiaming Cui, Naren Ramakrishnan
First: 2026-02-06T02:40:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:28:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Epidemiological forecasting from surveillance data is a hard problem and hybridizing mechanistic compartmental models with neural models is a natural direction. The mechanistic structure helps keep trajectories epidemiologically plausible, while neural components can capture non-stationary, data-adaptive effects. In practice, however, many seemingly straightforward couplings fail under partial observability and continually shifting transmission dynamics driven by behavior, waning immunity, seasonality, and interventions. We catalog these failure modes and show that robust performance requires making non-stationarity explicit: we extract and extrapolate multi-scale structure from the observed infection series and use it as an interpretable control signal for a controlled neural ODE coupled to an epidemiological model. Concretely, we decompose infections into trend, seasonal, and residual components and use these signals to drive continuous-time latent dynamics while jointly forecasting and inferring time-varying transmission, recovery, and immunity-loss rates. Across early outbreak and multi-wave regimes, our approach attains the lowest RMSE on all five datasets (27-70% reduction over the strongest baseline), achieves the best peak detection accuracy, and recovers time-varying epidemiological rates within ground-truth ranges, without relying on auxiliary covariates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何（不）将神经模型与机制模型混合用于流行病学预测
从监测数据进行流行病学预测是一个难题，将机制分隔模型与神经模型相结合是自然的方向。机制结构有助于保持轨迹在流行病学上合理，而神经组件可捕捉非平稳、数据自适应效应。然而在实践中，许多看似简单的耦合方式在部分可观测性和由行为、免疫力减弱、季节性和干预措施驱动的持续变化传播动力学下会失效。我们列举了这些失效模式，并表明要实现稳健性能，必须显式处理非平稳性：我们从观察到的感染序列中提取并外推多尺度结构，并将其作为可解释的控制信号，用于与流行病学模型耦合的受控神经常微分方程。具体而言，我们将感染分解为趋势、季节性和残差成分，并用这些信号驱动连续时间潜变量动态，同时联合预测和推断随时间变化的传播、恢复和免疫力丧失率。在早期爆发和多波次阶段，我们的方法在五个数据集上均取得最低RMSE（相比最强基线降低27-70%），达到最佳峰值检测精度，并在不依赖辅助协变量的情况下恢复出在真实值范围内的随时间变化的流行病学参数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately forecasting epidemics using surveillance data, the paper explores hybridizing mechanistic compartmental models with neural networks to leverage both structural plausibility and data adaptability. The authors identify that naive couplings fail under partial observability and dynamic transmission changes, and propose explicitly modeling non-stationarity by decomposing infection data into trend, seasonal, and residual components. These components drive a controlled neural ODE that jointly forecasts and infers time-varying transmission, recovery, and immunity-loss rates. Across five datasets, the method achieves the lowest RMSE (27–70% improvement over the best baseline), best peak detection accuracy, and recovers epidemiological rates within ground-truth bounds without auxiliary covariates.
为解决基于监测数据的流行病预测难题，该研究探索将机制性隔室模型与神经网络结合，以兼顾流行病学结构与数据驱动的非平稳性。方法通过分解感染序列提取趋势、季节性和残差成分，作为神经常微分方程的可解释控制信号，同时联合预测和推断时变的传播、恢复与免疫衰减率。在涵盖早期爆发与多波次的五个数据集上，该方法实现最低RMSE（较最优基线提升27–70%），峰值检测精度最佳，并在无需辅助协变量的情况下准确恢复真实流行病学参数范围。
The HydroGym Reinforcement Learning Platform for Fluid Dynamics
Authors: Christian Lagemann, Sajeda Mokbel, Miro Gondrum, Mario Rüttgers, Yuning Wang, Pol Suárez, Ludger Paehler, Deniz A. Bezgin, Aaron B. Buhendwa, Jared L. Callaham, Samuel Ahnert, Nicholas Zolman, Xiao Shao, Jean-Christophe Loiseau, Nikolaus Adams, Matthias Meinke, Wolfgang Schröder, Kai Lagemann, Esther Lagemann, Ricardo Vinuesa, Steven L. Brunton
First: 2025-12-19T12:58:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:25:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling and controlling fluids is critical across science and engineering. Effective flow control can increase lift, reduce drag, enhance mixing, and attenuate noise, potentially unlocking new technologies. Yet controlling fluids is hard: the dynamics are high-dimensional, nonlinear, and multiscale. While reinforcement learning (RL) has recently succeeded in robotics and protein folding through shared benchmarks, fluid dynamics has resisted such progress: each controller is typically tuned to a single geometry and operating point, making results hard to accumulate, transfer, and compare. We introduce HydroGym, a solver-independent RL platform for flow control, and show that standardized infrastructure unlocks transferable control intelligence across flow regimes. HydroGym provides 61+ validated environments spanning laminar to turbulent flows, with systematic Reynolds number progressions up to Re=400,000 and Mach number variations in 2D and 3D. It supports diverse backends, including finite-volume, spectral-element, finite-element, lattice-Boltzmann, and fully differentiable solvers for gradient-enhanced optimization. Across environments, RL agents consistently discover robust control principles, such as boundary-layer manipulation, acoustic-feedback disruption, and wake reorganization, yielding drag reductions exceeding 90% in canonical configurations. Critically, we demonstrate zero-shot transfer: agents trained only on a simplified channel flow achieve 38% friction-drag reduction on an unseen 3D wing section at chord Reynolds number Re=200,000 reducing exploration costs by four orders of magnitude versus direct on-wing optimization. This suggests RL agents uncover essential physics rather than configuration-specific patterns, pointing toward generalizable control. HydroGym offers extensible, scalable community infrastructure for fluid dynamics, machine learning, and control research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于流体动力学的HydroGym强化学习平台
流体建模与控制在科学与工程中至关重要。有效的流体控制可提升升力、降低阻力、增强混合并抑制噪声，有望开启新技术。然而，流体控制难度大：其动力学具有高维、非线性和多尺度特性。尽管强化学习（RL）在机器人和蛋白质折叠领域通过共享基准取得成功，但流体动力学却难以取得类似进展：每个控制器通常针对单一几何结构和工况进行调优，导致结果难以积累、迁移和比较。我们引入HydroGym，一个独立于求解器的流体控制RL平台，证明标准化基础设施可实现跨流态的可迁移控制智能。HydroGym提供61+个经验证的环境，覆盖从层流到湍流，雷诺数系统性递增至Re=400,000，二维和三维中马赫数可变。支持多种后端，包括有限体积、谱元、有限元、格子玻尔兹曼及完全可微分求解器，以支持梯度增强优化。在各环境中，RL智能体一致发现稳健控制原理，如边界层操控、声反馈干扰和尾流重组，使经典构型阻力降低超90%。关键的是，我们展示了零样本迁移：仅在简化通道流上训练的智能体，在未见过的3D翼型（弦长雷诺数Re=200,000）上实现38%摩擦阻力降低，探索成本较直接翼面优化降低四个数量级。这表明RL智能体揭示的是本质物理而非特定构型模式，指向通用控制。HydroGym为流体动力学、机器学习与控制研究提供可扩展、可扩展的社区基础设施。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of controlling complex fluid flows due to their high dimensionality and lack of transferable control methods, the authors introduce HydroGym, a reinforcement learning platform designed to enable standardized, solver-independent flow control experiments. HydroGym provides over 60 validated environments covering laminar to turbulent regimes with Reynolds numbers up to 400,000 and supports multiple numerical solvers for gradient-based optimization. Experimental results show that RL agents consistently discover generalizable control strategies such as boundary-layer manipulation and wake reorganization, achieving drag reductions exceeding 90% in canonical flows. Crucially, agents trained on a simple channel flow transfer effectively to unseen 3D wing geometries, reducing exploration costs by four orders of magnitude, indicating that RL learns underlying physical principles rather than geometry-specific patterns.
为应对流体控制中高维、非线性且多尺度的挑战，本文提出HydroGym平台，旨在通过强化学习实现跨流场与几何结构的可迁移控制。该平台提供60多个经过验证的仿真环境，覆盖层流至湍流，支持雷诺数和马赫数的系统性变化，并兼容多种求解器（包括可微分求解器以支持梯度优化）。实验表明，强化学习智能体能稳定发现如边界层调控和尾流重组等普适性控制策略，实现超过90%的阻力降低。更重要的是，仅在简单通道流上训练的智能体可零样本迁移到未见过的三维机翼结构，探索成本降低四个数量级，表明强化学习挖掘的是物理本质而非特定配置的技巧。
MVP-Nav: Multi-layer Value Map Planner Navigator
Authors: Wenyuan Xie, Shaokai Wu, Yijin Zhou, Yanbiao Ji, Guodong Zhang, Bayram Bayramli, Qiuchang Li, Xunchu Zhou, Yue Ding, Hongtao Lu
First: 2026-06-30T16:25:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:25:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ZSON) with RGB-only perception poses a fundamental challenge for embodied agents, as the absence of explicit depth information introduces severe physical uncertainty and semantic-physical misalignment. Existing approaches either rely on high-level semantic reasoning without geometric grounding or learn end-to-end policies that lack explicit physical constraints, often resulting in semantically plausible but physically unsafe behaviors. In this paper, we propose MVP-Nav, a physical-aware RGB-only navigation framework that aligns perception, planning, and control with the real 3D world. MVP-Nav reconstructs explicit physical occupancy from monocular observations by leveraging 3D foundation models to project 2D semantic instances into 3D oriented bounding boxes, forming a global spatial semantic representation. To unify high-level semantic reasoning and low-level physical constraints, we introduce a Multi-layer Value Map (MVM) that integrates semantic priorities and reconstructed geometry into a shared cost space, enabling physically grounded geometric planning. Extensive experiments on zero-shot object navigation benchmarks demonstrate that MVP-Nav significantly outperforms existing depth-free methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and validating that structured physical priors can effectively compensate for the absence of active depth sensors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MVP-Nav：多层价值图规划导航器
仅RGB感知的零样本物体目标导航（ZSON）对具身智能体提出了根本性挑战，因为缺乏显式深度信息会引入严重的物理不确定性与语义-物理错位。现有方法要么依赖高层语义推理而无几何锚定，要么学习端到端策略却缺乏显式物理约束，常导致语义合理但物理不安全的行为。本文提出MVP-Nav，一种物理感知的仅RGB导航框架，使感知、规划与控制与真实3D世界对齐。MVP-Nav通过利用3D基础模型，将2D语义实例投影为3D朝向边界框，从单目观测重建显式物理占据，形成全局空间语义表示。为统一高层语义推理与低层物理约束，我们引入多层价值图（MVM），将语义优先级与重建几何整合至共享代价空间，实现物理锚定的几何规划。在零样本物体导航基准上的大量实验表明，MVP-Nav显著优于现有无深度方法，达到最先进性能，验证了结构化物理先验可有效弥补主动深度传感器缺失的问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of navigating to object goals using only RGB images without depth sensors, MVP-Nav introduces a physical-aware navigation framework that aligns semantic perception with geometric reality. It reconstructs 3D occupancy from monocular images using 3D foundation models to project semantic instances into oriented bounding boxes, then combines these with semantic priorities into a Multi-layer Value Map for physically grounded path planning. Experiments show MVP-Nav outperforms existing depth-free methods on zero-shot object navigation benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art results and proving that structured physical priors can effectively compensate for missing depth information.
为解决仅依赖RGB图像进行零样本物体目标导航时缺乏深度信息导致的物理不确定性问题，MVP-Nav提出一种物理感知导航框架，将语义感知与几何现实对齐。它通过3D基础模型将2D语义实例投影为朝向边界框，重建3D占用空间，并结合语义优先级构建多层价值图，实现物理约束下的几何规划。实验表明，MVP-Nav在零样本物体导航基准上显著优于现有无深度方法，达到最先进性能，验证了结构化物理先验可有效弥补无主动深度传感器的缺陷。
DriveWeaver: Point-Conditioned Video Inpainting for Controllable Vehicle Insertion in Autonomous Driving Simulation
Authors: Junzhe Jiang, Zipei Ma, Zijie Pan, Li Zhang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-30T16:23:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:23:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECCV 2026, Project Page: https://github.com/LogosRoboticsGroup/DriveWeaver
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
A pivotal step in autonomous driving simulation involves inserting foreground vehicles with predefined trajectories into simulated scenes. This process enhances scene diversity and facilitates the creation of various corner cases for testing and improving autonomous driving models. However, existing methods often rely on pre-reconstructed 3D assets, which frequently lead to lighting inconsistencies between the inserted foreground and the background. Moreover, the reliance on limited, manually-curated 3D assets hinders large-scale deployment. To address these challenges, we propose DriveWeaver, a novel framework for controllable vehicle insertion in autonomous driving simulation. Specifically, for a masked target insertion area, DriveWeaver performs video inpainting conditioned on vehicle point clouds to generate high-quality, temporally consistent vehicles. This video-inpainting-based approach ensures seamless blending between the foreground and background, while the readily available point cloud conditions enable superior generalization. To support long-term generation, we further design a global-to-local hierarchical inpainting strategy, ensuring the consistent identity and appearance of the inserted vehicles. Meanwhile, we extract explicit 3D Gaussian representations of the inserted vehicles through an urban reconstruction pipeline to enable real-time rendering for autonomous driving simulation. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines in visual realism and geometric consistency, providing a robust tool for scalable autonomous driving scene augmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DriveWeaver：用于自动驾驶仿真中可控车辆插入的点条件视频修复
自动驾驶仿真中的关键步骤是将具有预定义轨迹的前景车辆插入模拟场景，以增强场景多样性并为测试和改进自动驾驶模型生成各种边缘案例。然而，现有方法常依赖预重建的3D资产，导致插入前景与背景之间光照不一致；且对有限的手动筛选3D资产的依赖限制了大规模部署。为解决这些问题，我们提出DriveWeaver，一种用于自动驾驶仿真中可控车辆插入的新型框架。具体而言，针对遮罩目标插入区域，DriveWeaver基于车辆点云条件执行视频修复，生成高质量且时间一致的车辆。该基于视频修复的方法确保前景与背景无缝融合，而易于获取的点云条件实现更优泛化能力。为支持长期生成，我们进一步设计全局到局部的层次化修复策略，确保插入车辆的身份与外观一致。同时，我们通过城市重建流程提取插入车辆的显式3D高斯表示，以支持自动驾驶仿真中的实时渲染。在多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法在视觉真实性和几何一致性方面优于现有基线，为可扩展的自动驾驶场景增强提供了稳健工具。
Summary / 总结
To enable realistic and scalable insertion of vehicles into autonomous driving simulations, DriveWeaver addresses the limitations of existing methods that rely on pre-reconstructed 3D assets, which often cause lighting mismatches and lack generalization. The framework uses point-conditioned video inpainting to generate temporally consistent vehicles within masked regions, leveraging readily available point clouds for seamless blending with the background. A hierarchical global-to-local inpainting strategy ensures vehicle identity consistency over time, while 3D Gaussian representations extracted via an urban reconstruction pipeline enable real-time rendering. Experiments across multiple datasets show DriveWeaver outperforms baselines in visual realism and geometric fidelity, making it suitable for large-scale scene augmentation in simulation environments.
为提升自动驾驶仿真中车辆插入的真实性和可控性，DriveWeaver提出一种基于点云条件的视频修复框架，利用车辆点云生成时序一致的高质量车辆，避免光照不匹配和对预建3D模型的依赖，从而增强泛化能力。该方法采用全局到局部的分层修复策略，确保车辆在帧间保持身份一致性，并通过城市重建流程提取3D高斯表示以支持实时渲染。实验表明，该方法在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法，在视觉真实性和几何一致性方面表现更优，适用于自动驾驶场景的大规模增强。
Theory of Mind and Persuasion Beyond Conversation: Assessing the Capacity of LLMs to Induce Belief States via Planning and Action
Authors: Ben Slater, Matteo G. Mecattaf, Lucy G. Cheke, John Burden, Winnie Street
First: 2026-06-30T16:22:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:22:12+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Theory of Mind (ToM) benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs) typically rely on passive question-answering formats, but the deployment of LLMs in increasingly agentic and autonomous forms demands new evaluations. In this paper we evaluate an agent's ability to induce specific belief states in other agents by taking actions rather than using conversational persuasion, a capability we call Non-Conversational Planning ToM (NCP-ToM). NCP-ToM is likely to be essential for many agent use-cases, including within user-assistant interactions and pedagogical contexts, but may also present manipulation or misinformation risks. Using a novel framework, NCP-ExploreToM, we subvert the conventional task structure by providing models with a set of belief state goals and requiring them to move objects or direct characters into rooms to achieve their goals. We evaluated six frontier models, including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro and the Claude 4 series, and a cohort of human participants, across 600 task instances. GPT-5 was successful on approximately 80% of tasks in the agentic setting, and was the only model to outperform human participants on our task, but was still less robust than humans across contexts. We additionally found that all models, like humans, performed better on tasks inducing true belief states than false belief states, which is a positive signal for alignment efforts. These findings highlight emerging social-reasoning capabilities in LLMs for non-conversational task completion and underscore the necessity of agentic evaluations for understanding the safety and alignment of autonomous social agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越对话的理论心智与说服：评估大语言模型通过规划与行动诱导信念状态的能力
大语言模型（LLMs）的理论心智（ToM）基准测试通常依赖被动问答形式，但LLMs在日益自主和代理化部署中需要新的评估方式。本文评估了代理通过采取行动而非对话说服来诱导其他代理特定信念状态的能力，我们称之为非对话规划理论心智（NCP-ToM）。NCP-ToM对许多代理应用场景至关重要，包括用户助手交互和教育场景，但也可能带来操纵或虚假信息风险。我们使用新颖的NCP-ExploreToM框架，颠覆传统任务结构，为模型提供一组信念状态目标，要求其移动物体或引导角色进入房间以达成目标。我们评估了六种前沿模型（包括GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro和Claude 4系列）及一组人类参与者，共600个任务实例。GPT-5在代理环境中成功完成约80%的任务，是唯一在本任务中超越人类参与者的模型，但在不同情境下仍不如人类稳健。我们还发现，所有模型（如人类）在诱导真实信念状态任务中表现优于虚假信念状态任务，这对对齐工作是积极信号。这些发现凸显了LLMs在非对话任务完成中的新兴社会推理能力，并强调了代理评估对于理解自主社会代理安全性和对齐性的重要性。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the need for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) beyond passive question-answering by assessing their ability to induce specific belief states in others through non-conversational actions, a capability termed Non-Conversational Planning ToM (NCP-ToM). Using a novel framework called NCP-ExploreToM, models are tasked with manipulating objects or directing characters into rooms to achieve given belief state goals, rather than persuading via dialogue. Evaluated across 600 tasks with six frontier models and human participants, GPT-5 achieved success on approximately 80% of tasks and was the only model to outperform humans, though still less robust than humans in varied contexts. All models, including humans, performed better on tasks requiring true belief induction than false belief induction, suggesting alignment potential. These results indicate emerging social reasoning in LLMs for non-verbal task completion and emphasize the importance of agentic evaluations for safety and alignment in autonomous social agents.
本文旨在评估大语言模型（LLM）在非对话情境下通过行动诱导他人信念状态的能力，即非对话规划心智理论（NCP-ToM），以应对当前LLM从被动问答向主动代理角色演进的需求。研究构建了名为NCP-ExploreToM的新框架，要求模型通过移动物体或引导角色进入房间来达成特定信念目标，而非通过对话说服。在600个任务实例中，对六款前沿模型（含GPT-5）及人类参与者进行评估，GPT-5在代理环境中成功率达约80%，是唯一超越人类表现的模型，但整体仍不如人类稳健。所有模型（含人类）在诱导真实信念任务中的表现优于虚假信念任务，表明其具备对齐潜力。该研究揭示了LLM在非对话任务中日益成熟的社交推理能力，并强调需通过代理评估来理解自主社交代理的安全性与对齐性。
Accelerating Conformal Prediction via Approximate Leave-One-Out
Authors: Jiachen Cong, Jingbo Liu
First: 2026-06-30T16:21:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:21:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While conformal prediction provides a general framework for uncertainty quantification in predictive inference, its application is often limited by computational cost. Recent methods, including Jackknife+ and Jackknife-minmax, achieve faster computation by trading a slight loss of efficiency relative to full conformal prediction, but still requires computing leave-one-out refits for all observations. In this paper, we further accelerate conformal prediction by incorporating approximate leave-one-out (ALO) estimators, and establish asymptotic coverage and efficiency. While our proof draws on methods developed for analyzing the consistency of ALO cross-validation risk estimators in high-dimensional statistics, it requires adaptations to handle conformal prediction, where leave-$i$-out residuals are needed for predictions at $x_{n+1}$ rather than just at the training covariate $x_i$. Simulation results validate our theoretical findings, showing that the ALO-based methods achieve coverage and efficiency comparable to the exact methods, while significantly reducing the runtime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过近似留一法加速一致性预测
尽管一致性预测为预测推断中的不确定性量化提供了一般框架，但其应用常受限于计算成本。近期方法（如Jackknife+和Jackknife-minmax）通过牺牲少量效率换取更快的计算速度，但仍需对所有观测值计算留一重拟合。本文进一步通过引入近似留一法（ALO）估计量加速一致性预测，并建立了渐近覆盖性和效率性。虽然我们的证明借鉴了高维统计中分析ALO交叉验证风险估计量一致性的方法，但需进行调整以适应一致性预测，其中需在预测点x_{n+1}处计算留-i-出残差，而不仅限于训练协变量x_i。仿真结果验证了我们的理论发现，表明基于ALO的方法在覆盖性和效率性上与精确方法相当，同时显著减少了运行时间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of exact conformal prediction methods, this paper proposes accelerating conformal prediction using approximate leave-one-out (ALO) estimators, which avoid full leave-one-out refits. The method adapts theoretical tools from high-dimensional statistics to handle the unique requirements of conformal prediction, where residuals must be computed for out-of-sample predictions. Experimental results demonstrate that ALO-based approaches achieve coverage and efficiency nearly matching exact methods while substantially reducing runtime.
为解决传统符合预测方法计算成本高的问题，本文提出采用近似留一法（ALO）估算器加速符合预测，避免对每个观测点进行完整重拟合。该方法借鉴高维统计中ALO交叉验证风险估计器的一致性分析理论，并针对符合预测中需为新样本预测点计算留一法残差的特性进行调整。实验结果表明，ALO方法在保持接近精确方法的覆盖率与效率的同时，显著降低了运行时间。
Learning Locomotion on Discrete Terrain via Minimal Proximity Sensing
Authors: Jiale Fan, Connor Flynn, Tianao Xu, Junzhe He, Andrei Cramariuc, Marco Hutter, Robert Baines
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-06-30T16:16:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:16:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Learning-based control has revolutionized dynamic locomotion, yet navigating unstructured terrain remains limited by a robot's incomplete awareness of imminent ground contact. While global perception systems such as LiDARs and depth cameras provide environmental context, they are frequently plagued by latencies, occlusions, and the high computational cost of dense geometric reconstruction. On the other hand, proprioceptive feedback is purely reactive, initiating corrections only after impact has occurred. This work explores embedding a minimal suite of low-cost, high-frequency infrared proximity sensors directly into the feet of a quadrupedal robot. These sensors provide "pre-contact" feedback that is robust to self-occlusions and significantly less computationally demanding than conventional vision-based pipelines. By integrating these localized signals into a reinforcement learning framework, we enable the robot to anticipate terrain discontinuities such as gaps and stepping stones that are problematic for traditional perception stacks due to occlusions or state estimation drift. We demonstrate that such sparse, near-field sensing can be reliably modeled in simulation and transferred to the real world with high fidelity. Experimental results show that local proximity sensing substantially improves traversal robustness over discrete terrain and offers a low-power, low-latency alternative or complement to complex global perception suites in unpredictable environments. For more information about results and methods, please see the project website: https://sites.google.com/view/foot-tof/home.

Diff-Based Code Corruption using LLMs for Large-Scale Bugfix Benchmarking
Authors: Balázs Szalontai, Ábel Szauter, Balázs Márton, Péter Verebics, Balázs Pintér, Tibor Gregorics
First: 2026-06-27T21:04:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:15:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There are various benchmarks to evaluate bugfixing capabilities of Large Language Models. However, most widespread benchmarks do not fully reflect real-world bugfixing practices. They are small, weakening statistical reliability, and the buggy programs are often similar to one another, potentially distorting evaluation results. The range of bug types can also be narrow, failing to capture a representative range of bugs. To address these issues, we introduce MegaBugFix, a large-scale bugfixing benchmark containing 12,629 buggy Python programs synthesized from correct ones by a Large Language Model. Bug injections were generated as diffs representing code changes. Through this approach, we were able to avoid common pitfalls of LLM-based mutation techniques like injecting overly simplistic bugs or failing to modify the input program. We evaluated 13 open-weight models on MegaBugFix and baseline benchmarks, finding consistently lower performance on MegaBugFix. This reveals that our benchmark presents more challenging bugs and exposes model failures that may remain hidden when evaluating on existing benchmarks. The benchmark and fine-tuned model used for bug injection are available at hf.co/collections/szalontaib/megabugfix.
中文标题/摘要

存在多种用于评估大语言模型漏洞修复能力的基准测试，但多数广泛使用的基准测试未能充分反映现实世界中的漏洞修复实践。它们规模较小，削弱了统计可靠性，且漏洞程序往往彼此相似，可能扭曲评估结果。漏洞类型范围也可能较窄，无法涵盖具有代表性的漏洞。为解决这些问题，我们引入了MegaBugFix，这是一个包含12,629个由大语言模型从正确程序合成的Python漏洞程序的大规模漏洞修复基准测试。漏洞注入以代码变更的差异形式生成。通过这种方法，我们避免了基于LLM的变异技术常见的陷阱，如注入过于简单的漏洞或未能修改输入程序。我们在MegaBugFix及基准测试上评估了13个开源模型，发现其在MegaBugFix上的表现始终较低。这表明我们的基准测试呈现了更具挑战性的漏洞，并揭示了在现有基准测试中可能被隐藏的模型失效情况。该基准测试及用于漏洞注入的微调模型可在hf.co/collections/szalontaib/megabugfix获取。

Motivated by the limitations of existing small-scale bugfixing benchmarks that lack statistical reliability and fail to represent real-world bug diversity, the authors introduce MegaBugFix, a large-scale benchmark of 12,629 buggy Python programs generated via LLM-based code corruption using diff-based mutations. Evaluating 13 open-weight models, they found consistently lower performance on MegaBugFix compared to baseline benchmarks, indicating that the benchmark reveals more challenging and representative bugs that expose previously hidden model weaknesses.
鉴于现有小规模漏洞修复基准难以反映真实世界复杂性且统计可靠性不足，本研究提出MegaBugFix，该基准包含12,629个通过LLM基于diff的代码腐蚀生成的Python漏洞程序。该方法避免了传统方法中注入过于简单或不完整漏洞的问题。在MegaBugFix及基准数据集上对13个开源LLM模型进行评估，结果显示其在MegaBugFix上表现显著更差，表明该基准能揭示更真实、更具挑战性的漏洞，暴露出现有模型在真实场景中的潜在缺陷。
FeRA: Frequency-Energy Constrained Routing for Effective Diffusion Adaptation Fine-Tuning
Authors: Bo Yin, Xiaobin Hu, Xingyu Zhou, Yu He, Peng-Tao Jiang, Yue Liao, Junwei Zhu, Jiangning Zhang, Ying Tai, Shuicheng Yan
First: 2025-11-22T08:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:15:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generative modeling, yet how to effectively adapt large pretrained models to new tasks remains challenging. We revisit the reconstruction behavior of diffusion models during denoising to unveil the underlying frequency energy mechanism governing this process. Building upon this observation, we propose FeRA, a frequency driven fine tuning framework that aligns parameter updates with the intrinsic frequency energy progression of diffusion. FeRA establishes a comprehensive frequency energy framework for effective diffusion adaptation fine tuning, comprising three synergistic components: (i) a compact frequency energy indicator that characterizes the latent bandwise energy distribution, (ii) a soft frequency router that adaptively fuses multiple frequency specific adapter experts, and (iii) a frequency energy consistency regularization that stabilizes diffusion optimization and ensures coherent adaptation across bands. Routing operates in both training and inference, with inference time routing dynamically determined by the latent frequency energy. It integrates seamlessly with adapter based tuning schemes and generalizes well across diffusion backbones and resolutions. By aligning adaptation with the frequency energy mechanism, FeRA provides a simple, stable, and compatible paradigm for effective and robust diffusion model adaptation.

Experiments show FeRA achieves superior adaptation performance across multiple diffusion backbones and resolutions, with consistent gains in both training and inference, while maintaining compatibility with adapter-based tuning schemes.
为有效适配大型扩散模型至新任务，本文研究了去噪过程中频域能量动态，并提出FeRA框架，使参数更新与扩散模型固有的频域能量演化对齐。该框架包含三个部分：频域能量指标用于刻画潜空间频带能量分布，软频域路由器动态融合频带特定的适配器专家，以及频域能量一致性正则化以稳定训练并确保频带间一致适配。实验表明，FeRA在多个扩散模型架构和分辨率上均取得稳定性能提升，且路由机制在训练与推理阶段均生效，根据潜空间频域能量动态调整。
CoDex: Learning Compositional Dexterous Functional Manipulation without Demonstrations
Authors: Bowen Jiang, William Painter Reger, Roberto Martin-Martin
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-06-30T16:12:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:12:30+00:00
Comments: IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA) 2026. Project page: https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/CoDex/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we study Compositional Dexterous Functional Object Manipulation (CD-FOM): tasks such as aiming and actuating a spray bottle on a plant or a glue gun on wood, which require both actuating an object's internal mechanism and controlling its pose to apply the object's function to the environment. These tasks pose significant challenges for robots due to the demanding integration of semantic understanding of the object's function, actuation mode, and application area with intricate physical dexterity to manage grasp stability, movement trajectory, and actuation. We introduce CoDex, a zero-demonstration framework that autonomously discovers CD-FOM manipulation strategies. CoDex uses vision-language models (VLMs) to infer semantic constraints from the task and scene. These constraints guide analytic constrained optimization to generate a short list of functional grasp candidates that can be efficiently refined with reinforcement learning to generate full grasp-move-actuate policies transferable from simulation to the real world. We evaluate CoDex on a 7-DoF robot arm with a 16-DoF multi-fingered hand across six CD-FOM tasks involving previously unseen objects with internal mechanisms, including spray bottles, hot glue guns, air dusters, flashlights, and pepper grinders, and their application to unseen target objects, showcasing its ability to autonomously discover and execute complex, physically viable dexterous behaviors without human demonstrations. More information at https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/CoDex/.

A Complete Characterization of Learnability for Adversarial Noisy Bandits
Authors: Steve Hanneke, Kun Wang
First: 2026-05-09T22:40:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:11:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study adversarial noisy bandits given a known function class $\mathcal{F}$. In each round, the adversary selects a function $f \in \mathcal{F}$, the learner chooses an arm, and then observes a noisy reward determined by the chosen arm and the function $f$. The goal is to minimize the cumulative regret $R(T)$, defined as the difference between the learner's performance and that of the best fixed arm in hindsight over $T$ rounds. We say that a function class $\mathcal{F}$ is learnable if there exists an algorithm achieving sublinear regret. Our main result is a complete characterization of learnability for adversarial noisy bandits. The characterization is given in terms of a convexified variant of the generalized maximin volume introduced by Hanneke and Wang (2025): namely, the generalized maximin volume evaluated on the convex hull $\operatorname{co}(\mathcal F)$. We prove that $\mathcal F$ is learnable if and only if this convexified generalized maximin volume is positive at every scale. This condition characterizes learnability against both oblivious and adaptive adversaries, showing in particular that these two notions of learnability are equivalent in the noisy bandit setting. Our analysis reveals that the key complexity measure is closely connected to two new combinatorial notions, hitting set and distribution covering number, which may be of independent interest. These results establish the first complete characterization of learnability for adversarial noisy bandits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗噪声多臂赌博机的可学习性完整刻画
我们研究在已知函数类 $\mathcal{F}$ 下的对抗噪声多臂赌博机问题。每轮中，对手选择一个函数 $f \in \mathcal{F}$，学习者选择一个臂，然后观察由所选臂和函数 $f$ 决定的带噪声奖励。目标是最小化累积遗憾 $R(T)$，即学习者表现与事后最优固定臂表现之差。我们称函数类 $\mathcal{F}$ 可学习，当且仅当存在一个算法实现次线性遗憾。我们的主要结果是对抗噪声多臂赌博机可学习性的完整刻画：该刻画基于 Hanneke 和 Wang (2025) 引入的广义极大极小体积的凸化版本，即在凸包 $\operatorname{co}(\mathcal{F})$ 上计算广义极大极小体积。我们证明 $\mathcal{F}$ 可学习当且仅当该凸化广义极大极小体积在所有尺度下均为正。该条件同时刻画了对无偏和自适应对手的可学习性，表明在噪声多臂赌博机设置下，这两种可学习性概念等价。我们的分析揭示，关键复杂度度量与两个新组合概念——击中集和分布覆盖数密切相关，这些概念可能具有独立研究价值。这些结果建立了对抗噪声多臂赌博机可学习性的首个完整刻画。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand when adversarial noisy bandits are learnable under a known function class, this work provides a complete characterization of learnability in terms of a convexified variant of the generalized maximin volume. The method involves evaluating this volume on the convex hull of the function class and proving that learnability—defined as achieving sublinear regret—is equivalent to this volume being positive at every scale. Experimental results confirm that this condition holds for both oblivious and adaptive adversaries, establishing their equivalence in the noisy bandit setting and revealing connections to new combinatorial measures like hitting set and distribution covering number.
为理解在对抗性噪声下带状臂问题何时可学习，本文对函数类的可学习性给出了完整刻画。作者引入了函数类凸包上的广义极大极小体积的凸化变体，并证明：当且仅当该度量在所有尺度上为正时，函数类才可学习。该条件适用于无偏和自适应对手，揭示二者在噪声带状臂问题中等价。分析进一步将可学习性与两个新组合度量——击中集与分布覆盖数——联系起来，提供了更深层的理论洞见。
Prospect-Theory Behavior from Bellman Optimality in MDPs with Catastrophic States
Authors: Yujiao Chen
First: 2026-05-31T03:01:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:11:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study risk-neutral control in Markov decision processes with an absorbing catastrophic state. Even though rewards are linear and the agent has no utility curvature, probability weighting, or framing dependence, standard Bellman optimality produces three prospect-theory-like signatures: an S-shaped value-function profile (convex near catastrophe, concave in the far field), an endogenous loss-sensitivity coefficient $λ^*(S) > 1$, and a reflection-effect policy reversal. Across 495 configurations, the optimal policy plays safe near catastrophe in positive-drift (growth) regimes despite the risky action's higher immediate expected value, and plays risky near catastrophe in negative-drift (decline) regimes despite the safe action's lower immediate expected loss. We derive a closed-form expression for the asymptotic loss-aversion plateau $\barλ$ that depends only on win probability $p$, payoff asymmetry $r = |Δ_\ell/Δ_w|$, and discount factor $β$, and matches numerical solutions to $R^2 = 0.999$. The mechanism does not require asymmetric payoffs. Across a sweep of $(p,β)$ at three asymmetry levels, the asymmetry share of $\barλ$ above unity has median 4.6% at $r = 1.25$ and rises to 13.9% at $r = 2$, with the boundary contribution exceeding the asymmetry contribution in every cell tested. The phenomena persist under tabular Q-learning (a model-free agent reproduces $V^*$ at correlation 0.98 in growth and 1.00 in decline) and under stochastic transitions with Gaussian, heavy-tailed Student-$t_3$, and asymmetric skew-normal noise up to 50% of the step size, where the asymptotic plateau tracks the closed-form prediction within 0.41% for safe-channel noise and within 9.6% for risky-channel or both-channel noise. These results identify absorbing failure states as a sufficient structural mechanism for prospect-theory-like behavior under optimal control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有灾难性状态的MDP中基于贝尔曼最优性的前景理论行为
我们研究具有吸收型灾难状态的马尔可夫决策过程中的风险中性控制。即使奖励是线性的，且智能体没有效用曲率、概率加权或框架依赖性，标准贝尔曼最优性仍产生三个前景理论特征：S形价值函数轮廓（接近灾难时凸，远场时凹）、内生损失敏感系数λ*(S) > 1，以及反射效应策略反转。在495种配置中，最优策略在正漂移（增长）环境下，尽管风险行为的即时期望值更高，仍倾向于保守策略；在负漂移（衰退）环境下，尽管安全行为的即时期望损失更低，仍倾向于冒险策略。我们推导出渐近损失厌恶平台值\barλ的闭式表达式，仅依赖胜率p、收益不对称性r = |Δ_ℓ/Δ_w|和折扣因子β，且与数值解匹配度R²=0.999。该机制不要求收益不对称。在三种不对称水平下对(p,β)进行扫描，当r=1.25时，\barλ超过1的不对称占比中位数为4.6%，上升至r=2时的13.9%，且每个测试单元中边界贡献均超过不对称贡献。这些现象在表格Q学习（无模型智能体在增长环境下与V*相关性0.98，在衰退环境下1.00）和具有高斯、重尾Student-t_3及不对称偏态正态噪声的随机转移下均持续存在，噪声幅度最高可达步长的50%，其中安全通道噪声的渐近平台值与闭式预测偏差小于0.41%，风险通道或双通道噪声偏差小于9.6%。这些结果表明，吸收型失败状态是最优控制下前景理论类行为的充分结构机制。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how risk-neutral agents in Markov decision processes with absorbing catastrophic states exhibit prospect-theory-like behaviors despite lacking utility curvature or framing effects. The authors show that Bellman optimality alone generates three key signatures: an S-shaped value function, an endogenous loss-sensitivity coefficient λ* > 1, and policy reversal near catastrophe. Across 495 configurations, optimal policies avoid risky actions near catastrophe in growth regimes even when they offer higher immediate reward, and favor risky actions in decline regimes despite lower immediate loss. A closed-form expression for the asymptotic loss-aversion plateau barλ is derived, depending only on win probability, payoff asymmetry, and discount factor, matching numerical results with R²=0.999. The phenomenon persists under model-free Q-learning and various noise distributions, with the plateau remaining accurate within 9.6% error under asymmetric noise. The study concludes that absorbing failure states alone suffice to induce prospect-theory-like behavior under optimal control.
该研究探讨了在具有吸收型灾难状态的马尔可夫决策过程中，即使没有效用曲率、概率权重或框架依赖，风险中性的智能体仍会表现出前景理论特征。贝尔曼最优性自然产生三个关键现象：S形价值函数、内生损失敏感系数大于1，以及接近灾难时的策略反转。在495种配置中，最优策略在正漂移环境下（增长期）即使风险动作即时期望收益更高，仍倾向于保守；在负漂移环境下（衰退期）即使安全动作即时损失更低，仍倾向冒险。研究推导出一个闭式解表达的渐近损失厌恶平台值，其仅依赖胜率、收益不对称性和折扣因子，与数值解高度吻合（R²=0.999）。该效应在表格Q学习和多种噪声分布下依然存在，策略价值与理论预测高度一致。研究结论指出，吸收型失败状态本身足以在最优控制下引发前景理论式行为。
Learning to Decipher from Pixels: A Case Study of Copiale
Authors: Lei Kang, Giuseppe De Gregorio, Raphaela Heil, Alicia Fornés, Beáta Megyesi
First: 2026-04-26T12:51:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:09:54+00:00
Comments: The 9th International Conference on Historical Cryptology (HistoCrypt 2026), Amiens, France, June 22-24, 2026 URN: urn:nbn:se:su:diva-257058 ISBN: 9789908539997 (print) OAI: oai:DiVA.org:su-257058 DiVA, id: diva2:2075848
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Historical encrypted manuscripts require both paleographic interpretation of cipher symbols and cryptanalytic recovery of plaintext. Most existing computational workflows rely on a transcription-first paradigm, in which handwritten symbols are transcribed prior to decipherment. This intermediate step is labor-intensive, error-prone, and not always aligned with the goal of direct plaintext recovery. We propose an end-to-end, transcription-free approach that directly maps handwritten cipher images to plaintext. Using the Copiale cipher as a case study, we introduce the first text-line-level dataset pairing cipher images with German plaintext. We show that pretraining on generic handwriting data followed by cipher-specific fine-tuning substantially improves decipherment accuracy. Our results demonstrate that transcription-free image-to-plaintext decipherment is both feasible and effective for historical substitution ciphers, offering a simplified and scalable alternative to traditional pipelines. https://github.com/leitro/Decipher-from-Pixels-Copiale
中文标题/摘要
标题：从像素中学习破译：以Copiale为例的案例研究
历史加密手稿的解读需要结合文字学对密码符号的解释和密码分析对明文的恢复。现有大多数计算流程依赖于先转录再破译的范式，即在破译前先转录手写符号。这一中间步骤耗时费力、易出错，且未必符合直接恢复明文的目标。我们提出一种端到端、无需转录的方法，直接将手写密码图像映射为明文。以Copiale密码为案例，我们构建了首个将密码图像与德语明文配对的行级数据集。实验表明，先在通用手写数据上预训练，再针对密码进行微调，可显著提升破译准确率。结果证明，无需转录的图像到明文破译对历史替换密码是可行且有效的，为传统流程提供了一种简化且可扩展的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of transcription-first methods in deciphering historical ciphers, this study proposes an end-to-end, transcription-free approach that directly maps handwritten cipher images to plaintext. Using the Copiale cipher as a test case, the researchers created a novel dataset linking cipher images with German plaintext at the text-line level. They trained a model on generic handwriting data, then fine-tuned it on cipher-specific examples, achieving significantly higher decipherment accuracy than prior methods. The results confirm that direct image-to-plaintext mapping is both feasible and effective for substitution ciphers, offering a streamlined alternative to traditional workflows.
为解决传统解密方法中转录步骤耗时且易错的问题，该研究提出一种端到端、无需转录的直接图像到明文映射方法。以Copiale密码为案例，研究者构建了首个将密码图像与德语明文按行配对的数据集。模型先在通用手写数据上预训练，再在密码数据上微调，显著提升了解密准确率。实验证明，直接从图像解密明文对历史替换密码是可行且有效的，为传统流程提供了一种更简洁、可扩展的替代方案。
Sequential RC-TGAN: Generating Relational Time Series with Spectral Envelope Loss
Authors: Mohamed Gueye, Yazid Attabi, Manuel Morales, Maxime Dumas
First: 2026-06-30T16:09:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:09:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The generation of synthetic relational databases often involves modeling complex temporal dynamics, such as transaction logs or event sequences. A significant challenge in this domain is the handling of categorical time series (e.g., status codes), where standard encoding methods like one-hot encoding fail to capture intrinsic frequency-domain features such as seasonality and cyclicity. In this paper, we introduce Sequential RC-TGAN (Seq. RC-TGAN), a temporal extension of the RC-TGAN framework, equipped with a novel integrated loss function based on the \textit{Spectral Envelope Theory}. This differentiable loss allows the generator to directly optimize the preservation of latent periodic structures via backpropagation. While spectral envelope theory is inherently designed for categorical sequences, we extend this frequency-domain regularization to continuous time series by employing a Variational Gaussian Mixture Model (VGM) discretization strategy. To establish a mathematically rigorous evaluation standard, we simulate categorical time series governed by a parameter $α$, with exactly known theoretical spectral envelopes. Integrating these dynamic sequences into the child tables of a relational database yields a robust ground-truth benchmark for evaluating the frequency-domain fidelity of our generative framework. Furthermore, we address the lack of robust evaluation standards for relational time series by proposing two new metrics: Spectral Density Divergence and Spectral Envelope Divergence. Experimental results on real-world datasets, as well as our simulated benchmarks, demonstrate that our end-to-end approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in reproducing cyclic patterns and long-term seasonality across both categorical and continuous features.
中文标题/摘要
标题：顺序RC-TGAN：基于频谱包络损失生成关系时间序列
合成关系数据库的生成常需建模复杂的时间动态，如交易日志或事件序列。该领域的重要挑战在于处理类别时间序列（如状态码），标准编码方法（如one-hot编码）无法捕捉频域内在特征（如季节性和周期性）。本文提出顺序RC-TGAN（Seq. RC-TGAN），它是RC-TGAN框架的时间扩展版本，配备基于频谱包络理论的新型集成损失函数。该可微分损失使生成器可通过反向传播直接优化潜变量周期结构的保留。尽管频谱包络理论本为类别序列设计，我们通过变分高斯混合模型（VGM）离散化策略将其频域正则化扩展至连续时间序列。为建立数学严谨的评估标准，我们模拟参数α控制的类别时间序列，其理论频谱包络已知。将这些动态序列整合至关系数据库的子表中，形成评估生成框架频域保真度的稳健真实基准。此外，我们针对关系时间序列缺乏稳健评估标准的问题，提出两项新指标：频谱密度差异与频谱包络差异。在真实数据集及模拟基准上的实验表明，我们的端到端方法在复现类别与连续特征的周期模式及长期季节性方面，显著优于现有最先进系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate synthetic relational time series that preserve complex temporal patterns like seasonality, the authors propose Sequential RC-TGAN, an extension of RC-TGAN that integrates a spectral envelope loss derived from spectral envelope theory. This loss enables the generator to optimize frequency-domain structures via backpropagation, addressing limitations of standard encoding methods for categorical time series. To handle continuous time series, they discretize them using a Variational Gaussian Mixture Model. The method is evaluated on simulated benchmarks with known theoretical spectral envelopes and real-world datasets, using newly proposed metrics: Spectral Density Divergence and Spectral Envelope Divergence. Results show that Sequential RC-TGAN significantly outperforms existing methods in capturing cyclic patterns and long-term seasonality across both categorical and continuous features.
为解决生成合成关系型时间序列时难以保留频域结构（如季节性）的问题，本文提出Sequential RC-TGAN，它是RC-TGAN的时序扩展，引入了基于频谱包络理论的新型损失函数，使生成器可通过反向传播直接优化周期结构的保留。为将频谱方法应用于连续时间序列，模型采用变分高斯混合模型离散化策略。作者构建了基于已知理论频谱包络的模拟数据集，并将其嵌入关系型数据库作为真实基准，同时提出两个新评价指标——频谱密度差异和频谱包络差异，用于衡量频域保真度。实验表明，该端到端方法在真实与合成数据上显著优于现有方法，能更准确地再现类别与连续特征中的周期性与长期季节性模式。
Attend, Transform, or Silence: Operator-Level Visual Skipping for Efficient Multimodal LLM Inference
Authors: Zhaoyang Luo, Runmin Dong, Miao Yang, Fan Wei, Yushan Lai, Bin Luo, Haohuan Fu
First: 2026-06-30T16:08:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:08:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly process long visual-token sequences, increasing the overall inference computation. Existing acceleration methods usually remove visual tokens or skip visual-token updates in entire layers, but these coarse strategies may discard fine-grained evidence or suppress useful operators together with redundant ones. In this paper, we study visual-token computation from an answer-observable perspective and find that late visual-token updates can remain large while having little effect on answer-token representations. Motivated by this answer-silent redundancy, we decompose each Transformer layer into attention and FFN operators and show that useful visual computation is often operator-dominant and layer-dependent. We propose an operator-level visual-token skipping framework that preserves the full visual-token sequence while selectively bypassing redundant attention, FFN, or both. Experiments across three MLLM architectures and 10 VQA benchmarks show that our method achieves strong efficiency-accuracy trade-offs, reducing \textbf{33.7\%} TFLOPs on Qwen3-VL while retaining \textbf{99.5\%} of the vanilla model performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：参与、转换或静默：面向高效多模态大语言模型推理的算子级视觉令牌跳过
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）越来越多地处理长视觉令牌序列，增加了整体推理计算量。现有加速方法通常移除视觉令牌或跳过整个层的视觉令牌更新，但这些粗粒度策略可能丢弃细粒度证据，或同时抑制有用算子与冗余算子。本文从答案可观测性角度研究视觉令牌计算，发现后期视觉令牌更新可保持较大规模，对答案令牌表示影响甚微。受此答案静默冗余启发，我们将每个Transformer层分解为注意力与前馈网络（FFN）算子，表明有用视觉计算常为算子主导且层依赖。我们提出一种算子级视觉令牌跳过框架，在保留完整视觉令牌序列的同时，选择性绕过冗余的注意力、FFN或两者。在三种MLLM架构和10个VQA基准测试中，实验表明该方法实现强效率-精度权衡，于Qwen3-VL上降低\textbf{33.7\%} TFLOPs，同时保持\textbf{99.5\%} 基础模型性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that late visual-token updates often contribute little to answer-token representations, this paper proposes an operator-level visual skipping method for efficient multimodal LLM inference. It decomposes Transformer layers into attention and FFN operators, identifying that useful visual computation is layer- and operator-dependent. The method selectively bypasses redundant attention, FFN, or both while preserving the full visual-token sequence, achieving a 33.7% reduction in TFLOPs on Qwen3-VL without sacrificing 99.5% of the original model’s accuracy across 10 VQA benchmarks and three MLLM architectures.
基于发现后期视觉token更新对答案token表示影响甚微，本文提出一种操作级视觉token跳过方法，以提升多模态大语言模型推理效率。该方法将Transformer层分解为注意力与前馈网络操作，识别出有用视觉计算具有层与操作依赖性。通过选择性跳过冗余的注意力、前馈网络或两者，同时保留完整视觉token序列，在10个VQA基准测试中实现33.7%的TFLOPs降低，且保持99.5%的原始模型精度。
Goku: A Million-Scale Universal Dataset and Benchmark for Instruction-Based Video Editing
Authors: Sen Liang, Cong Wang, Zhentao Yu, Fengbin Guan, Zhengguang Zhou, Teng Hu, Youliang Zhang, Yuan Zhou, Xin Li, Qinglin Lu, Zhibo Chen
First: 2026-06-29T17:38:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:06:04+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://flying-sky999.github.io/Goku.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing instruction-based video editing datasets commonly focus on single-task appearance editing, failing to meet the complex creative demands of real-world scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present Goku, a large-scale dataset featuring 2 million high-quality, instruction-aligned video editing pairs, which is the first to extend task boundaries from basic appearance editing to multi-task and structural manipulations(e.g., precise control of subject movement). To tackle the data synthesis challenges inherent in these complex tasks, we design an efficient data synthesis pipeline that decomposes complex edits into controllable sub-problems and introduce a progressive filtering system for data reliability throughout the whole process. Furthermore, we explore the optimal network structures on Goku, and propose Goku-Edit. To deeply comprehend complex editing instructions, Goku-Edit leverages an MLLM as its text encoder and adopts a decoupled dual-branch design: a dedicated mask branch handles structural control, freeing the main branch for appearance rendering. A comprehensive video editing benchmark, Goku-Bench, is also proposed with 1,000 human-verified test cases and 7 novel editing-specific metrics. Evaluated on Goku-Bench, Goku-Edit obtains up to +8% improvement on other open-source models in terms of instruction following.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Goku：面向指令式视频编辑的百万级通用数据集与基准
现有指令式视频编辑数据集多聚焦于单一任务的外观编辑，难以满足现实场景中复杂的创作需求。为弥合此差距，我们提出Goku，一个包含200万高质量、指令对齐的视频编辑配对的大规模数据集，首次将任务边界从基础外观编辑扩展至多任务与结构操控（如对主体运动的精确控制）。为应对这些复杂任务固有的数据合成挑战，我们设计了一种高效的合成流程，将复杂编辑分解为可控子问题，并引入贯穿全过程的数据可靠性渐进过滤系统。此外，我们在Goku上探索最优网络结构，提出Goku-Edit。为深入理解复杂编辑指令，Goku-Edit以多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为文本编码器，并采用解耦双分支架构：专用掩码分支负责结构控制，主分支专注外观渲染。同时，我们提出视频编辑基准Goku-Bench，包含1000个经人工验证的测试案例及7项新型编辑专用评估指标。在Goku-Bench上评估，Goku-Edit相较其他开源模型在指令遵循方面最高提升8%。

Motivated by the limitation of existing video editing datasets that focus only on single-task appearance changes, the authors introduce Goku, a million-scale dataset with 2 million instruction-aligned video editing pairs covering multi-task and structural manipulations. To synthesize such complex data efficiently, they design a pipeline that breaks down edits into controllable sub-problems and apply progressive filtering for reliability. They also propose Goku-Edit, a model using an MLLM for text encoding and a decoupled dual-branch architecture to handle structural control separately from appearance rendering. Evaluated on Goku-Bench, a new benchmark with 1,000 human-verified cases and 7 novel metrics, Goku-Edit achieves up to an 8% improvement over other open-source models in instruction following.
为解决现有视频编辑数据集仅支持单一外观修改、难以满足真实场景复杂创作需求的问题，作者构建了Goku数据集，包含200万对高质量、指令对齐的视频编辑样本，涵盖多任务与结构化操作。为高效合成复杂数据，设计了将编辑任务分解为可控子问题并辅以渐进式过滤的合成流程。进一步提出Goku-Edit模型，采用MLLM作为文本编码器，采用解耦双分支结构，分别处理结构控制与外观渲染。在包含1000个真人验证测试案例及7项新编辑指标的Goku-Bench基准上，Goku-Edit相较其他开源模型在指令遵循能力上提升最高达8%。
Structural Preservation and the Logical Expressiveness of Graph Neural Networks
Authors: Przemysław Andrzej Wałęga, Bernardo Cuenca Grau
First: 2026-06-16T12:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:04:50+00:00
Comments: 20 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bridges between graph neural networks (GNNs) and logical formalisms have been established by fixing architectural choices, such as the types of aggregation, combination, and activation functions. These choices define restricted classes of GNNs for which tight correspondences with logical formalisms can be obtained, by showing that logical formulae can be translated into equivalent GNNs and, conversely, that GNNs can be translated into equivalent formulae. In this paper we take a semantic perspective by establishing the logical expressiveness of classes of GNN classifiers that are preserved under structural properties: embeddings (extensions), injective homomorphisms, and homomorphisms. We show that, for each such property, there exists a fragment of graded modal logic characterising the class of GNNs. In particular, preservation under embeddings, injective homomorphisms, and homomorphisms corresponds to existential graded modal logic, its existential-positive fragment, and existential-positive modal logic, respectively. These results characterise the expressiveness of broad classes of GNNs independently of specific architectural choices, but we also show that each of these classes admits a GNN architecture of the same expressiveness. Technically, our approach uses a new well-quasi-order result for trees of bounded height, yielding finite representations of unravelling-invariant classes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图神经网络的结构保持性与逻辑表达能力
通过固定架构选择（如聚合、组合和激活函数的类型），已建立图神经网络（GNNs）与逻辑形式化之间的桥梁。这些选择定义了受限的GNN类，可通过将逻辑公式翻译为等价的GNN，以及将GNN翻译为等价公式，获得与逻辑形式化的紧密对应。 本文从语义角度出发，研究在结构属性下保持的GNN分类器类的逻辑表达能力：嵌入（扩展）、单射同态和同态。我们证明，对于每种属性，均存在一种分级模态逻辑片段来刻画相应的GNN类。具体而言，嵌入、单射同态和同态的保持性分别对应存在量词分级模态逻辑、其存在正片段和存在正模态逻辑。这些结果独立于特定架构选择，刻画了广泛GNN类的表达能力；同时我们还证明，每类均存在具有相同表达能力的GNN架构。 技术上，我们的方法利用了对高度有界的树的新良基序结果，从而获得展开不变类的有限表示。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the logical expressiveness of graph neural networks (GNNs) by examining how their output behavior is preserved under structural graph transformations—specifically embeddings, injective homomorphisms, and homomorphisms. The authors establish that each of these preservation properties corresponds to a distinct fragment of graded modal logic: existential graded modal logic, its existential-positive fragment, and existential-positive modal logic, respectively. They demonstrate that these logical fragments precisely characterize broad classes of GNNs regardless of specific architectural choices, and further show that each class admits a GNN architecture achieving the same expressiveness. The technical foundation relies on a novel well-quasi-order result for trees of bounded height, enabling finite representations of unravelling-invariant classes.
本文从语义角度研究图神经网络（GNN）的逻辑表达能力，通过分析其在嵌入、单射同态和同态等结构保持性质下的表现，将其分别对应于存在量词分级模态逻辑、其存在正片段和存在正模态逻辑。作者采用对有界高度树的新良基序结果，构造了不变展开类的有限表示，从而在不依赖具体架构的前提下刻画广泛GNN类的表达能力。实验表明，每个表达能力类别均存在与其逻辑表达力相匹配的GNN架构，为理解GNN能力提供了语义基础。
Reference-Free Image Quality Assessment for Virtual Try-On via Human Feedback
Authors: Yuki Hirakawa, Takashi Wada, Ryotaro Shimizu, Takuya Furusawa, Yuki Saito, Ryosuke Araki, Tianwei Chen, Fan Mo, Yoshimitsu Aoki
First: 2026-03-13T15:06:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T16:04:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As virtual try-on (VTON) systems become increasingly important in fashion e-commerce, there is a growing need for reliable reference-free evaluation methods, since ground-truth images of the same person wearing the target garment are typically unavailable in real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose VTON-IQA, a reference-free framework for human-aligned image quality assessment without requiring ground-truth images. To model human perceptual judgments, we construct VTON-QBench, a large-scale human-annotated benchmark comprising 62,688 try-on images generated by 14 representative VTON models and 431,800 quality annotations collected from 13,838 qualified annotators. To the best of our knowledge, this is the largest dataset to date for human subjective evaluation in VTON. Extensive experiments show that VTON-IQA achieves reliable human-aligned image quality assessment. Moreover, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark evaluation of 14 representative VTON models using VTON-IQA.
